Abstract: A magnetic field applied perpendicular to the helical axis of a monoaxial chiral helimagnet compresses the spin helix into a coplanar chiral soliton lattice (CSL). We show that optically driven high-harmonic generation from itinerant electrons coupled to a frozen CSL resolves the lattice-scale structure of the localized twist, rather than the continuum soliton shape. High-order harmonics remain perturbative for the uniform helix. As the winding localizes at fixed magnetic period, they grow by many orders of magnitude and acquire a nonperturbative dependence on the drive amplitude. The growth originates from a spatially nonuniform effective hopping that turns each soliton into a localized dip in the hopping amplitude. When the degree of winding localization is held fixed, high-order intensities fall by many orders of magnitude as the magnetic period increases toward the continuum limit, where the hopping modulation is spatially smoothed. High-order harmonics thus resolve a real-space characteristic of the coplanar CSL, even in the absence of scalar chirality and an emergent magnetic field.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文研究了在二维无能隙体系中模交换子$J(A,B,C)$的性质，发现其在狄拉克节点临界点处呈现半量子化取值，并与手性中心电荷和宇称反常相关；该结果推广了模交换子的应用范围，并提供了宇称反常的信息论测量手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索当二维体系统从有能隙变为无能隙时，模交换子是否仍可定义并具有物理意义，特别是其对手性中心电荷和宇称反常的探测能力。

Method: 基于Haldane蜂窝模型，计算不同类型的无能隙情况（如孤立狄拉克节点、二次节点和费米面）下的模交换子，利用涌现的反射对称性证明半量子化现象，并分析其对系统参数变化的鲁棒性。

Result: $J$在孤立狄拉克节点处收敛到半量子化值，修正项随子系统尺寸呈幂律衰减；存在保护的手性边缘态时，$3J/π = c_- + 1/2$；半量子化对分区形变、狄拉克速度和各向异性具有鲁棒性；但在非狄拉克型无能隙情况下（如二次节点或费米面），此性质消失。

Conclusion: 模交换子可推广至某些无能隙体系，其半量子化是狄拉克型临界行为和宇称反常的信息论标志，为理解拓扑相变和反常现象提供了新工具。

Abstract: The modular commutator $J(A,B,C) = i\langle[K_{AB},K_{BC}]\rangle$ extracts the chiral central charge $c_-$ from a single bulk wavefunction of a \emph{gapped} 2d state, where $3J/π=c_-$. Inspired by the recent developments in the field of gapless symmetry-protected topological phases, we ask: what does the modular commutator measure, if it is well-defined at all, when the 2d bulk becomes \textit{gapless}? Several interesting new insights can already be obtained using the simple Haldane honeycomb model. For the critical point hosting an isolated Dirac node we find that $J$ remains sharp: it converges to a \textit{half-quantized} value, with corrections that decay as a power law in the subsystem size rather than exponentially, mirroring the power-law correlations in gapless systems. We prove the half-quantization using an emergent reflection symmetry of the massless Dirac cone, and show that the half-quantized contribution comes from the other gapped cone (the massive partner of the massless one). This massive partner can be interpreted as the physical incarnation of the Pauli-Villars regulator, which is the origin of the parity-breaking level-$\frac{1}{2}$ Chern-Simons term (with half-quantized Hall conductance) and the parity anomaly. When protected chiral edge modes coexist with a bulk Dirac node we obtain $3J/π= c_-+\frac{1}{2}$. The half-quantization is also shown to be robust against tripartition deformation, tuning Dirac velocity and Dirac cone anisotropy. We further investigate other types of gaplessness---quadratic nodes (in contrast to linear Dirac) and the case with Fermi surface---and show that the robust half-quantization of $J$ is lost in such non-Dirac cases. These results generalize the modular commutator beyond gapped phases, and at the same time provide an information-theoretic measurement of the parity anomaly.

</details>

Abstract: We predict and classify interaction-driven quantum spin Hall crystals (QSHCs), a class of states emerging at fractional filling through an interplay of topology and spontaneous translation-symmetry breaking. QSHCs form nearly degenerate manifolds whose members can realize distinct topological phases protected by time-reversal or valley $U(1)_v$ symmetry, with time-reversal acting nontrivially within the manifold. As a representative of this broad class of states we provide evidence for 9-fold quasi-degenerate $\sqrt{3}\times\sqrt{3}$ charge ordered QSHCs at $ν= -8/3$ of twisted bilayer MoTe$_2$ near a $5^\circ$ twist. Here a $\mathbb{Z}_3$ index organizes states related by lattice translation into three time-reversal invariant states with nontrivial $\mathbb{Z}_2$ topology and three time-reversal related doublets whose individual members spontaneously break time-reversal and carry a $U(1)_v$ protected spin-Chern number. Finally, we determine the conditions that favor QSHCs over closely competing intervalley-coherent crystals.

</details>

Abstract: Despite being arguably the simplest and best characterized quasi-2D charge-density-wave (CDW) systems, the rare-earth tritellurides ($R$Te$_3$) continue to yield surprising experimental results, including recent evidence suggestive of mirror-symmetry breaking associated with the onset of CDW order. Motivated by this, we consider a 2D electron-phonon model for a single Te square-net plane, which we analyze using mean-field theory. For an appropriate region of parameter space, we find a finite-temperature continuous transition from the normal state to a unidirectional CDW state with an ordering vector matching that observed experimentally. At lower temperatures, we find a second translation-symmetry-breaking transition, similar to what occurs in $R$Te$_3$ compounds with heavier rare-earth elements. In certain parameter regimes, we also find an intervening mirror-symmetry-breaking transition occurring between the two transitions described above. These results reveal an intrinsic susceptibility to mirror-symmetry breaking in the unidirectional CDW phase, which is relevant to understanding recent experiments on the $R$Te$_3$ compounds.

</details>

Abstract: We analyze the criticality of an Ising Gross-Neveu-Yukawa (GNY) theory of $N_ψ$ Dirac fermion valleys with intra- and inter-valley interactions, using a renormalization-group analysis in $4-ε$ space-time dimensions to one-loop order. The conventional GNY fixed point of decoupled valleys is unstable against inter-valley fluctuations which are naturally present if the symmetry breaking is driven by short-ranged interactions. At the new fixed point, the critical exponents differ from the conventional GNY universality. Most importantly, Lorentz invariance is broken due to interference effects resulting from the relative rotation between valley coordinate frames. Method: 引入‘动力学分裂’结构实现哈密顿量的代数对偶映射，结合精确对角化方法与子系统对称性分析，在大尺度三角晶格上研究chiral XYZ模型的能谱、纠缠熵及相变行为。

Result: 发现了具有L log L 纠缠标度和弦标度行为的无能隙基态，证实了大N极限下节点结构的延续性；识别出由非可缩线对称性导致的拓扑简并；并通过调控形变发现通往Z2×Z2拓扑有序相的有限耦合相变。

Conclusion: 动力学分裂与子系统对称性为研究强关联阻挫系统提供了新路径，chiral XYZ模型展现出丰富的量子临界与拓扑现象，包括受保护的节点线、异常纠缠标度以及可实现的拓扑有序相变。

Abstract: We study a class of Hamiltonians with a structure that we call "dynamical splitting": the Hamiltonian terms can be divided into two sets acting on the same degrees of freedom such that every term in one set commutes with every term in the other, although terms within either set do not all commute. This structure yields an algebraic duality to effective degrees of freedom on which the two parts of the Hamiltonian act disjointly, enabling exact diagonalization on lattices with approximately twice as many spins as usually accessible. We exploit it in the "chiral XYZ model", a geometrically frustrated spin-$1/2$ model on the triangular lattice which was previously introduced as a special limit of a Majorana-Hubbard model. This model also possesses anticommuting noncontractible line symmetries, which enforce an exact, topology-dependent degeneracy between locally indistinguishable states. We first study a $\mathbb{Z}_N$ clock generalization and find, at large $N$, a gapless ground state with three subsystem-symmetry-protected nodal lines. Exploiting dynamical splitting and the subsystem symmetries, we carry out exact diagonalization of the $N=2$ model on lattices up to $9\times9$ spins. The many-body gap and bipartite entanglement provide strong evidence for a gapless state consistent with the large-$N$ nodal structure: the entanglement scales as $L\log L$ and exhibits $1+1$-dimensional CFT-like chord scaling on cylinders. Finally, we study instabilities and proximate phases. In particular, we find evidence that a subsystem-symmetry-preserving deformation drives a finite coupling transition to a gapped phase with $\mathbb Z_2 \times \mathbb Z_2$ topological order.

</details>

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

Result: 发现当频率高于下限阈值ω_− = 2H_⊥时，会出现明显的自旋共振峰，且可在欧姆吸收背景上清晰观测。

Conclusion: EDSR可作为识别交替磁性的一种有效手段，尤其适用于区分其他类型的磁序。

Abstract: We predict electric-dipole spin resonance (EDSR) in altermagnetic conductors: in a magnetic field ${\mathbf H}_\perp$, perpendicular to the magnetization axis, an AC electric field will induce a \textit{spin} resonance peak above the lower threshold frequency $ω_- = 2H_\perp$. In sufficiently clean samples, this peak shall be clearly visible on the background of ohmic absorption. EDSR can thus serve as a diagnostic of altermagnetism in conducting materials and as a means to distinguish it from higher-symmetry antiferromagnetic order.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确提取电子能带拓扑信息需要精确标定量子振荡的相位；然而，交流磁致伸缩系数中的相位偏移尚未被实验校准。

Method: 在同一样品上同时测量磁化强度和交流磁致伸缩系数的量子振荡，并进行直接相位比较，利用单一频率避免多频干扰。

Result: 观测到约$π/2$的相对相位差，与磁致伸缩系数与磁化强度应力导数之间由麦克斯韦关系预测的结果一致。

Conclusion: 建立了从交流磁致伸缩量子振荡中提取回旋轨道相位所需的关键相位校准，验证了其作为热力学探针的可靠性。

Abstract: The Berry phase accumulated along a cyclotron orbit encodes important information about electronic band topology and is commonly inferred from the phase of quantum oscillations. Measurements of the ac magnetostrictive coefficient have recently emerged as a sensitive thermodynamic probe of quantum oscillations, but the phase offset has not been experimentally calibrated. Here, using the topological antiferromagnet YbMnBi$_2$, we calibrate this offset by directly comparing quantum oscillations in magnetization with those in the ac magnetostrictive coefficient. Measurements of both responses on the same single crystal reveal a single fundamental frequency of approximately 160 T in fields up to 14 T, enabling a direct phase comparison free from ambiguities associated with multiple frequencies. We observe an approximately $π/2$ relative phase shift between the two oscillatory responses, consistent with the Maxwell relation linking the magnetostrictive coefficient to the stress derivative of magnetization. Our results establish the appropriate phase needed to extract cyclotron-orbit phase information from quantum oscillations in the ac magnetostrictive coefficient.

</details>


### [78] [Quasi two dimensional magnetic structure of the triclinic double perovskite Ca$_2$CuWO$_6$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25778)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解三斜双钙钛矿Ca$_2$CuWO$_6$的磁基态及其与四方类似物Sr$_2$CuWO$_6$的差异，探索对称性降低对磁结构的影响。

Method: 利用中子粉末衍射解析Ca$_2$CuWO$_6$的磁结构，并通过Rietveld精修和建立共同超晶胞进行比较分析；结合平均场计算研究交换路径的对称性分裂效应。

Result: 发现Ca$_2$CuWO$_6$在TN ≃ 32 K以下具有共线反铁磁结构，传播矢量为(1/2, 1/2, 0)，有序磁矩为0.66(3) μB/Cu；通过对称性分析揭示其与Sr$_2$CuWO$_6$具有相同的磁结构基元，但三斜对称性导致交换路径分裂，选择单一k域。

Conclusion: Ca$_2$CuWO$_6$的磁基态由钨介导的次近邻相互作用稳定，对称性破缺虽未改变磁性基序，但通过分裂交换路径选择特定有序态。

Abstract: We present the antiferromagnetic ground state of the triclinic double perovskite Ca$_2$CuWO$_6$solved by neutron powder diffraction, and analyze it in direct comparison with tetragonal Sr$_2$CuWO$_6$. Below $T_\mathrm{N} \simeq 32$ K, the magnetic Bragg reflections of Ca$_2$CuWO$_6$ are indexed by the commensurate propagation vector $\mathbf{k}=(\tfrac{1}{2},\tfrac{1}{2},0)$ in its native $P\bar{1}$ cell. Rietveld refinement yields a collinear structure with equal-magnitude, antiparallel moments on the crystallographically inequivalent Cu1 and Cu2 sites and an ordered moment of $0.66(3)~μ_{\mathrm B}$ per Cu at 1.5 K. A direct comparison with Sr$_2$CuWO$_6$ is obscured by different crystallographic settings and orientations of the cooperative Jahn-Teller elongation axes. Hence, we introduce a common crystallographic supercell through which effects of symmetry lowering from tetragonal to triclinic in the double perovskite are explored: Apparently different magnetic propagation vectors map onto the same supercell wave vector, revealing a common magnetic structure stabilized by tungsten-mediated, second-neighbor interactions. Mean field calculations further show that tetragonal symmetry preserves the degeneracy of four magnetic $\mathbf{k}$-domains in Sr$_2$CuWO$_6$, whereas the triclinic splitting of symmetry-related exchange pathways in Ca$_2$CuWO$_6$, most strongly within the Cu2 network, selects a single $\mathbf{k}$-domain. These results establish the magnetic ground state of Ca$_2$CuWO$_6$ and show how symmetry breaking selects a given ordered state without changing the underlying magnetic motif of the Sr analogue.

</details>


### [79] [Fate of the non-Abelian Moore-Read manifold under the non-Hermitian skin effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25816)
*Jiaxuan Guo,Simin Nie,Xiting Zhang,Fritz B. Prinz*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 研究了非厄米变形下的非阿贝尔Moore-Read分数量子陈绝缘体，发现其在有限非互易性范围内保持稳定的粒子纠缠计数特征，且在开放边界下左右态局域于相反边缘，而双正交纠缠谱在虚规范相似变换下不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非厄米系统中非阿贝尔拓扑序在非互易变形下的稳定性及其纠缠特性。

Method: 结合Hatano-Nelson虚规范变形与kagome晶格三体相互作用，采用双正交(2,4)-容许粒子纠缠计数作为主要诊断工具。

Result: 在不同系统尺寸下，Moore-Read流形的纠缠计数在有限非互易强度窗口内保持锁定，且具有正的参考秩纠缠能隙；开放边界下出现皮肤效应，但纠缠谱不变；Abelian ν=1/3 Laughlin态表现出更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 非厄米变形下Moore-Read态的拓扑纠缠特征在一定参数范围内稳定存在，表明其可用于非厄米拓扑量子计算的潜在应用。

Abstract: We study a non-Abelian Moore-Read fractional Chern insulator under a translation-preserving, nonreciprocal deformation that generates the non-Hermitian skin effect under open boundaries. The model combines the imaginary-gauge Hatano-Nelson deformation with a kagome-lattice three-body interaction designed to stabilize Moore-Read order at $ν=1/2$. Our primary diagnostic is the biorthogonal $(2,4)$-admissible particle-entanglement counting of the sixfold Moore-Read manifold, the standard Moore-Read fingerprint. Across three sizes ($N=16,20,24$), the counting locks to the clean references 1308, 3965, and 9282 over finite nonreciprocity windows through $γ\le0.55$, $0.65$, and $0.74$, respectively, with positive reference-rank entanglement gaps. Within every reported window the count is unchanged by the spectral readings tested; at $N=16$ it is also unchanged across three reduced density operators, with all 15 combinations returning 1308. The sixfold pattern for even $N_f$ and the adiabatically tracked Ising-odd doublet remain separated over the tested range $γ\le0.6$. Beyond a geometry-dependent threshold the instantaneous-lowest-six reference-rank gap drops sharply and its counting destabilizes. At $N=24$ a sector-0 branch pair becomes complex conjugate over a narrow interval inside the delocking bracket; both continuations through the interval are delocked at the tested PES points $γ=0.76$, $0.77$, and $0.80$. A same-lattice Abelian $ν=1/3$ Laughlin realization retains its counting to $γ=1.0$, so its counting is the more robust. On the torus the eigenstates remain extended; under open boundaries the right and left states skin-localize at opposite edges while the biorthogonal particle-entanglement spectrum is invariant under the imaginary-gauge similarity, so the torus and the open cylinder probe the same deformation under periodic and open boundaries.

</details>


### [80] [Rare-earth oxysulfides RE$_2$O$_2$S as model mixed-anion frustrated magnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26023)
*Austin M. Ferrenti,Ksenia Khoroshun,Mohamed Oudah,Graham King,Jonathan Gaudet,Alannah M. Hallas*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本研究报道了一类具有三角双层板晶格结构的稀土氧硫化物（RE₂O₂S）反铁磁体的合成与表征，揭示了混合阴离子材料中结构无序对磁性的影响，并强调了结构表征在发现新型磁性材料中的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索低维和各向异性磁性中的新奇量子现象，利用混合阴离子材料的本征各向异性调控结构、磁性和电子性质。

Result: 多数成分表现出长程反铁磁有序，其中Ce、Pr、Nd、Sm的反铁磁性为首次报道；PDF分析显示存在合成依赖的层间结构无序（RE₂O_{2+x}S_{1-x}），导致Nd样品的宏观磁响应显著变化。

Conclusion: 稀土基混合阴离子材料中的受挫磁基态具有可调性，且精确的结构表征对理解其磁性行为至关重要。

Abstract: The study of low-dimensional and anisotropic magnetism is a promising avenue for the discovery of novel quantum phenomena. Mixed-anion materials, defined by the coordination of metal cations by two or more distinct anionic species, provide an intrinsically anisotropic platform for the tuning of structural, magnetic, and electronic properties. In this work, we report the synthesis and characterization of a family of rare-earth oxysulfide (RE$_2$O$_2$S) antiferromagnets (AFM) possessing a triangular-bilayer slab lattice geometry. Long-range AFM order is observed for the majority of compositions, with several (RE = Ce, Pr, Nd, Sm) having been previously unreported. Although assumed to form stoichiometrically, pair distribution function (PDF) analysis provides evidence for significant, synthesis-dependent interslab structural disorder as RE$_2$O$_{2+x}$S$_{1-x}$, resulting in significant variability in the bulk magnetic response of the Nd member. This work highlights the tunability of frustrated magnetic ground states in rare-earth-based mixed-anion materials, and the importance of thorough structural characterization in the discovery of new mixed-anion magnets.

</details>


### [81] [Incommensurate spin fluctuations in one-dimensional Kondo metal CeCo2Ga8](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26071)
*Yixuan Huang,P. Murgatroyd,Yi Wu,M. Cook,P. F. S. Rosa,E. D. Bauer,Asish K. Kundu,A. Rajapitamahuni,E. Vescovo,S. Zhang,V. Anil,P. Piyawongwatthana,N. Murai,M. Kofu,K. M. Shen,F. Ronning,Jian-Xin Zhu,A. Scheie*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究复杂材料CeCo$_2$Ga$_8$是否具有一维Kondo系统的特征，并理解其低能自旋涨落行为。

Method: 采用非弹性中子散射和角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）进行实验测量，并结合密度矩阵重整化群（DMRG）对一维Kondo晶格模型进行数值模拟以解释实验结果。

Result: 观测到低能非公度短程磁涨落和高度一维的电子结构；DMRG模拟成功复现实验谱特征，确认该材料处于一维Kondo晶格相图中的新兴非公度相。

Conclusion: CeCo$_2$Ga$_8$虽具复杂性但仍表现为一维Kondo金属，其非弹性谱中同时存在低能Kondo和磁性特征，表明非微扰模拟的Kondo晶格模型可准确描述重费米子物理。

Abstract: We present an experimental and numerical study of the spin fluctuations in 1D Kondo metal CeCo$_2$Ga$_8$. Using inelastic neutron spectroscopy, we measure highly one-dimensional magnetism with low-energy incommensurate short-ranged magnetic fluctuations. ARPES similarly shows a highly one-dimensional electronic band structure, confirming the one-dimensional nature of the system. We use density matrix renormalization group (DMRG) simulations of the 1D Kondo lattice model to interpret the measured spectrum, which successfully reproduce the neutron scattering features. We are thus able to place CeCo$_2$Ga$_8$ within the emergent incommensurate phase of the 1D Kondo lattice phase diagram, and demonstrate that the Kondo lattice simulated non-perturbatively is an accurate microscopic model for heavy fermion physics. This shows CeCo$_2$Ga$_8$ to be one-dimensional despite its complexities, and reveals a coexistence of low-energy Kondo and magnetic features in its inelastic spectrum.

</details>

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索三维光子晶体中缺陷态的存在条件及其在光子带隙中的行为，为设计具有特定光学特性的材料提供理论基础。

Method: 采用理论上基于片上格林函数的方法分析缺陷态，并结合无限晶体极限与耦合偶极子方法的数值模拟研究有限尺寸效应。

Result: 缺陷会在光子带隙内引入局域态，其本征频率由理想晶体的片上格林函数决定；离片格林函数在约两个晶胞距离内消失；有限晶体中缺陷态的寿命随晶体尺寸指数增长；此外，缺陷还在带隙外产生强失谐态。

Abstract: We perform a theoretical study of defect states within the photonic band gap of three-dimensional diamond crystals composed of point scatterers and doped with substitutional defects. The defects introduce localized states inside the photonic band gap, whose existence conditions and eigenfrequencies are expressed in terms of the on-site Green's function of the ideal defect-free crystal. Off-site Green's functions are also calculated as function of distance and are shown to vanish within approximately two unit cells. Finite-size effects are analyzed by comparing the results obtained in the infinite-crystal limit with numerical simulations based on the coupled-dipole method. The latter not only reproduce the eigenfrequencies of the defect states within the band gap, but also provide their lifetimes originating from the finite crystal size. The lifetimes of the defect states increase exponentially with crystal size, becoming very long for large crystals. In addition to defect states in the three-dimensional photonic band gap, the defects also give rise to strongly detuned states outside the gap, which decouple from the spectrum of the ideal defect-free crystal.

</details>


### [83] [Semi-localized ground state in a 1D system with long-range hopping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25806)
*Murod S. Bahovadinov,Faridun N. Jalolov,Vladimir E. Kravtsov,Boris L. Altshuler,Georgy V. Shlyapnikov*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 研究了一维长程跃迁无序系统中量子粒子的局域化，发现1<a<3/2时在能带边缘存在无序驱动的相变，基态呈现半局域化而非传统局域化，表现出波函数的半分形特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索长程跃迁对一维无序系统中量子局域化行为的影响，特别是能带边缘的局域化转变。

Method: 在动量空间中研究基态，利用微扰理论推导波函数特征函数、矩和分形维数的表达式。

Result: 发现在1<a<3/2时，系统在能带边缘发生无序驱动的转变，高能态始终局域化；弱无序下基态呈现半局域化和半分形特性。

Conclusion: 长程跃迁导致一维无序系统中出现新的半局域化相，扩展了具有半分形波函数的模型类别。

Abstract: We study the localization of a quantum particle in a one-dimensional disordered system with long-range hopping amplitudes $t(r)\propto r^{-a}$. In contrast to the standard one-dimensional Anderson model ($a\to\infty$), in which all states are localized and the localization length is minimal at the band edge, the long-range model with $1<a<3/2$ exhibits a disorder-driven transition at the band edge, while high-energy states remain localized at arbitrary disorder strength. We investigate this transition for the ground state in momentum space. In the weak-disorder regime, we derive perturbative expressions for the characteristic functions and moments of the momentum-space wave function, as well as for its fractal dimensions. Our results demonstrate that the ground state exhibits $\it semilocalization$ rather than conventional localization, thereby extending the class of models displaying the unusual $\it semifractality$ of wave functions.

</details>

### [84] [Scalable Self-Supervised Learning for Multiphase AC-OPF in Distribution Systems with Topology Reconfiguration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25095)
*Hoang T. Nguyen,Shaohui Liu,Reetam Sen Biswas,Varsha Pendyala,Nurali Virani,Deepjyoti Deka,Priya L. Donti*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为Penalty+SLFS的自监督学习框架，用于在开关引起的拓扑变化下求解多相配电网交流最优潮流（AC-OPF），具有毫秒级推理、无需标签训练、高效处理拓扑变化和高鲁棒性，在大型配电网中实现了接近最优的可行解和显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式能源资源（DERs）在配电网中的广泛应用需要快速、大规模地求解多相不平衡交流最优潮流（AC-OPF），尤其是在动态拓扑重构场景下，传统非线性求解器速度慢，现有学习方法难以适应实际配电网的复杂性和不平衡特性。

Method: 提出Penalty + Sequential Linearized Feasibility Seeking (SLFS)算法，采用自监督学习框架，通过可微分的定点潮流求解器直接从AC-OPF目标函数和约束中训练，无需标注的最优解；利用Sherman-Morrison-Woodbury公式高效处理开关引起的拓扑变化，并采用M步雅可比近似加速反向传播；推理阶段通过SLFS修复不可行预测，确保可行性。

Result: 在IEEE 13节点到8500节点的配电网测试中，Penalty+SLFS实现了可忽略的最优性差距和近乎为零的约束违反，相比IPOPT求解器提速高达三个数量级，并在大分布偏移下保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: Penalty+SLFS为大规模、多相、可重构配电网提供了实现实时、拓扑感知的AC-OPF的可行路径，兼具高精度、高速度和强鲁棒性，适用于实际电力系统应用。

Abstract: The proliferation of distributed energy resources (DERs) in distribution grids enables the active coordination of these assets to reduce costs and enable cleaner operations. Realizing this potential requires solving multiphase AC optimal power flow (AC-OPF) quickly across varying loads, DER availabilities, and topology reconfigurations, at much greater speed and scale than conventional nonlinear solvers. Learning-based surrogates can offer millisecond inference, yet existing methods target largely balanced transmission systems and do not scale to the multiphase, unbalanced, and reconfigurable nature of distribution feeders at utility scale. We present the Penalty + Sequential Linearized Feasibility Seeking (SLFS) algorithm, a self-supervised learning framework for multiphase distribution AC-OPF under switch-induced topology changes. Penalty+SLFS requires no labeled optimal solutions and trains directly from the AC-OPF objective and constraints through a differentiable fixed-point power flow solver, avoiding expensive label generation and admitting robust training procedures. Topology changes are handled efficiently using Sherman-Morrison-Woodbury updates of the admittance-matrix inverse, while an M-step Jacobian approximation accelerates differentiation through the power flow solver. At inference, SLFS repairs any infeasible predictions, providing feasibility guarantees with low computational overhead. On IEEE feeders ranging from 13 to 8,500 nodes, Penalty+SLFS achieves negligible optimality gaps and near-zero constraint violations, delivers up to three orders of magnitude speedups over IPOPT, and remains robust under large distributional shifts, demonstrating a viable path toward real-time, topology-aware AC-OPF for large-scale distribution grids.

</details>


### [85] [Compiling Spatial Certificates into Temporal Contracts for Latency-Aware Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25228)
*Avinash malik*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: CIPS是一种面向安全关键型信息物理系统的合约驱动执行抽象，通过离线将空间安全证书转换为标准化的时间合约，以应对计算延迟和采样数据更新问题，确保在有界延迟下的全局混合安全性，并在自动驾驶制动场景中显著降低评估开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实时控制中，物理安全证书是空间定义的，但其有效性预测需考虑非零计算与交接延迟，依赖对系统动态的在线数值积分，导致实现复杂且效率低下。

Method: 提出CIPS框架，将异构的空间安全证书离线编译为归一化、单位速率的时间合约，从而抽象出复杂的系统动力学，提供确定性的O(1)时间预算给通用的采样数据调度器。

Result: 该方法实现了在有界计算延迟和异步执行下对全局混合安全不变性的形式化保证，并在自动驾驶车辆制动基准测试中验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: CIPS通过合约驱动的方式有效解决了安全关键型CPS中的时空一致性难题，在保持安全边界的同时大幅降低了微架构评估开销，相比LA-ETC减少了60.5倍的开销。

Abstract: We introduce CIPS, a contract-driven execution abstraction for
  managing computational latency and sampled-data updates in
  safety-critical cyber-physical systems (CPS). A fundamental challenge
  in real-time control is that physical safety certificates are defined
  spatially, yet predicting their validity under non-zero computation
  and handoff latency requires online numerical integration of plant
  dynamics. CIPS resolves this operational dichotomy by systematically
  compiling heterogeneous spatial safety certificates into normalized,
  unit-rate temporal contracts entirely offline. This transformation
  abstracts complex plant dynamics, exposing a deterministic, O(1)
  temporal budget to a generic sampled-data scheduler. We formally prove
  that this architecture guarantees global hybrid safety invariance
  under bounded computational latency and asynchronous execution.
  Finally, we validate the framework via an autonomous vehicle braking
  benchmark, demonstrating a 60.5 times reduction in micro-architectural
  evaluation overhead compared to latency-aware event-triggered control
  (LA-ETC) while preserving safety bounds.

</details>


### [86] [Multi-View Trust Evaluation for Collaborator Selection via Evidential Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25235)
*Botao Zhu,Xianbin Wang*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于多视图证据学习（MVE）的信任评估方法，通过建模多源异构数据、利用Mamba捕捉时序模式，并结合证据深度学习与动态融合策略，实现更准确的协作者选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于分布式系统中协作者的信任数据来自不同设备和场景，具有多源、异构且质量不均的特点，导致难以准确评估其信任度，因此需要一种能处理这些复杂特性的方法。

Method: 将每个任务拥有者视为一个独立观测视图，建模协作者的视图特定信任；采用Mamba模型捕捉各视图内信任状态的时间演化模式；引入证据深度学习量化评估中的不确定性；并通过动态证据融合策略自适应地整合多视图证据。

Result: 实验表明，MVE方法在信任评估准确性和任务成功率方面均优于基线方法。

Conclusion: MVE能够有效应对多源异构和动态变化环境下的信任评估挑战，显著提升分布式系统中协作者选择的可靠性与效率。

Abstract: Selection of trustworthy collaborators in distributed systems is critical for efficient task completion, necessitating the inference of trustworthiness from their past collaboration experience. However, as a collaborator serves distinct devices across diverse scenarios in past collaborations, its trust-related data, observed from different device-specific views, is inherently multi-source, heterogeneous, and uneven in quality. Consequently, achieving accurate trust evaluations for collaborator selection remains a major challenge. To tackle these issues, we propose a novel multi-view evidential learning (MVE) based trust evaluation method. First, to accommodate the multi-source heterogeneity of observed trust-related data, we model each task owner who has interacted with a potential collaborator as an independent observational view, enabling the evaluation of the collaborator's view-specific trust. Second, to address the dynamic evolution of trust under changing conditions, we leverage the powerful long-sequence modeling capability of the Mamba model to capture the deep temporal patterns of a collaborator's trust state within each view. Furthermore, to quantify the certainty levels of view-specific trust assessments, we incorporate an evidential deep learning mechanism in MVE, which outputs trust evaluation results while quantifying the subjective uncertainty underlying them. Finally, we employ a dynamic evidential fusion strategy to adaptively integrate the multi-view evidence based on their respective quantified uncertainties, thereby yielding a final trust evaluation for the collaborator. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the proposed MVE method outperforms baselines in both trust evaluation accuracy and task success rate.

</details>


### [87] [TrustFormer: Cross-Temporal and Cross- Dimensional Transformer for Task-Specific Multi-Dimensional Trust Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25238)
*Botao Zhu,Xianbin Wang*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种任务特定的多维信任评估框架TrustFormer，通过跨时间和跨维度注意力机制解决动态协作系统中多维信任评估的异步性和复杂依赖问题，显著提升了信任评估准确性和合作者选择可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的信任评估方法多采用单维或标量表示，难以准确反映合作者的真实可信度，且多维信任相关数据存在异步性及复杂的跨维度依赖关系，导致多维信任评估面临挑战。

Method: 提出TrustFormer框架，利用任务标识符和设备生成的时间戳同步历史协作中的异构信任数据，并引入跨时间与跨维度注意力机制，联合建模时间动态和跨维度关联，进而从历史性能数据中学习多维信任演化；同时根据任务的多维资源需求评估潜在合作者的多维资源信任。

Result: 实验结果表明，TrustFormer在信任评估准确性上比现有方法提升了40.8%，并能实现更可靠的合作者选择。

Conclusion: TrustFormer有效解决了多维信任评估中的数据异步和复杂依赖问题，通过融合多维信任特征实现了更精准的信任评估与最优合作者选择。

Abstract: In dynamic collaborative systems, the selection of reliable collaborators is critical to ensuring effective task execution. Existing trust evaluation methods often rely on unidimensional or scalar representations, which fail to faithfully capture a collaborator's true trustworthiness, thereby motivating a shift toward multi-dimensional trust modeling. However, due to the asynchrony of collected trust-related data across different dimensions, as well as the complex intra- and inter-dimensional dependencies embedded within these data, multi-dimensional trust evaluation remains challenging. To address these challenges, we propose TrustFormer, a task-specific multi-dimensional trust evaluation framework. Specifically, TrustFormer leverages task identifiers and device-generated timestamps to synchronize heterogeneous trust-related data across historical collaborations. It further employs cross-temporal and cross-dimensional attention mechanisms to jointly model temporal dynamics and inter-dimensional correlations, thereby effectively learning the multi-dimensional trust evolution of potential collaborators from historical performance data. In addition, according to the multi-dimensional resource requirements of tasks, potential collaborators' multi-dimensional resource trust is evaluated. Finally, by synthesizing these multi-dimensional trust profiles, the framework enables the optimal collaborator selection. Experimental results demonstrate that TrustFormer outperforms existing methods by yielding a 40.8% improvement in trust evaluation accuracy and enabling more reliable collaborator selection.

</details>


### [88] [Integrating Fast-response Capability into Virtual Power Plant Operation for Ancillary Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25247)
*Qixing Liu,Ruike Lyu,Zhe Zhai,Yan Shen,Xue Liu,Hongye Guo*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将快速响应能力纳入虚拟电厂（VPP）辅助服务运营框架的方法，通过历史控制指令构建机会约束，确保VPP满足辅助服务的响应要求，从而提升其在性能导向市场中的收益和运行性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虚拟电厂在提供辅助服务时需具备足够的快速响应能力以跟踪频繁变化的控制指令。若忽视该需求，可能导致VPP无法准确衡量调节能力，影响收益甚至被市场淘汰。因此，有必要在投标和运营模型中显式考虑响应能力约束。

Method: 利用历史控制指令数据，在VPP的投标模型中引入机会约束，要求VPP的快速响应能力以一定概率满足辅助服务的技术要求，并将其整合到运行优化框架中进行求解。

Result: 案例研究表明，考虑快速响应能力的机会约束能够有效提高VPP对辅助服务需求的满足程度，改善其市场出清结果和运行经济性。

Conclusion: 将快速响应能力纳入VPP运营模型有助于提升其在基于性能的辅助服务市场中的竞争力和盈利能力，确保其长期稳定参与。

Abstract: Virtual power plants (VPPs) can aggregate distributed energy resources (DERs) to provide ancillary services for power systems, creating new profit opportunities. Ancillary services such as secondary frequency regulation require providers to have sufficient response capability to follow rapidly changing control commands. If overlooking the response requirement, the VPP will not be able to accurately measure its regulation capability, reducing its earnings in performance-based markets or risking disqualification. This paper integrates the requirement for fast-response capability into the operational framework of VPPs providing ancillary services. We leverage historical control commands to formulate chance constraints in the bidding model, mandating that the VPP's fast-response capability meets the requirement of ancillary services with a specified probability. Case studies verify that considering fast-response capabilities can enhance VPP operation.

</details>


### [89] [LSTN: A Linear Model of Industrial Production Process for Demand Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25249)
*Ruike Lyu,Hongye Guo,Yuanjie Zheng,Yunlong Bai,Qixin Chen*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于评估需求响应应用的工业生产过程线性模型，兼顾了计算效率和建模精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统建模方法引入二进制变量导致在大规模需求响应应用中计算不可行，需要一种更高效的建模方式。

Method: 开发了一种线性化的工业生产过程模型，避免使用二进制变量以提升计算效率。

Result: 数值结果验证了所提模型在建模准确性方面的有效性，并显著提高了计算效率。

Conclusion: 该线性模型能够在保证建模精度的同时，大幅提升工业生产建模在需求响应应用中的计算效率。

Abstract: Industrial production modeling provides operational constraints for industrial users participating in demand response (DR) programs. Conventional modeling of the production process introduces binary variables to model the discrete operating points of industrial equipment, which can be computationally infeasible in large-scale DR applications. To reasonably model industrial users' operational constraints while balancing computational complexity and modeling accuracy, we developed a linear model of the industrial production process for evaluating DR applications. Numerical results verify the accuracy of the proposed model and its great improvement in computational efficiency over competing approaches.

</details>


### [90] [Dynamic droop specifications for Grid-Forming Inverter-Based Resources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25250)
*Jennifer T. Bui,Dominic Groß,Deepak Ramasubramanian*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出了一种数据驱动的动态模型来描述基于逆变器资源（IBRs）的小信号动态特性，并引入动态下垂系数以表征其在次同步频率下的响应，进而为构网型（GFM）IBR制定规范，促进互操作性并减少不利交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着同步发电机的大规模退役，需明确IBR在电网中的动态行为，尤其是异构控制下的稳定性贡献，以确保电网可靠运行。

Method: 提出一种数据使能的动态下垂模型，将传统的稳态下垂系数扩展为动态下垂系数，用于捕捉IBR在低于额定频率范围内的小信号动态，并设定增益与相位的边界条件作为GFM IBR的最低要求。

Result: 建立了可用于GFM IBR和频率辅助服务（如惯量响应）的动态下垂规范；通过GFL、GFM控制及OEM模型验证了该方法在识别潜在不良动态交互方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 动态下垂模型为IBR特别是GFM IBR提供了量化的小信号动态表征手段，有助于统一GFM认证标准，提升系统兼容性与稳定性。

Abstract: The large-scale retirement of synchronous generators requires additional capabilities from inverter-based resources (IBRs) to ensure the stability and reliability of power grids. With the heterogeneous controls of IBRs, it is especially important to understand their behavior on the grid. This work proposes a simple data-enabled dynamic model to capture the small-signal dynamics of IBRs and formulate specifications for grid-forming (GFM) IBRs. The dynamic droop model is complementary to well-studied impedance models and extends the common definition of steady-state droop coefficients to dynamic droop coefficients that fully characterize the IBR small-signal response below the nominal line frequency (e.g., subsynchronous oscillations). We propose bounds on the gain and phase of the dynamic droop coefficients to encode minimum requirements for GFM IBRs to promote interoperability and minimize adverse interactions. The resulting specifications also provide some insights into the much-debated question of how to certify an IBR as GFM. Moreover, we also provide dynamic droop specifications for frequency control ancillary services that, e.g., clarify and generalize the notion of an IBR inertia response. Finally, common grid-following (GFL) and GFM controls as well as original equipment manufacturer (OEM) models are used to illustrate the results and showcase the use of dynamic droop coefficients as a tool to screen IBR dynamics for potential adverse interactions.

</details>


### [91] [Analysis of Dynamic-Key LWE-Based Encrypted Control Systems for Asymptotic Stability and Numerical Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25255)
*Jungjin Park,Kiminao Kogiso*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于动态密钥LWE的加密状态反馈控制系统，通过时变编码器和解码器设计，利用李雅普诺夫方法推导出保证系统渐近稳定性和数值安全性的条件，并通过数值例子验证了所提方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在保证控制系统安全性的同时实现良好的动态性能，研究者希望将同态加密技术应用于反馈控制中，但需解决参数时变情况下的稳定性与数值溢出问题。

Method: 采用基于李雅普诺夫的方法，分析动态密钥LWE加密控制系统的稳定性，推导出对时变编码器和解码器参数的设计条件，以确保系统稳定并防止数值溢出。

Result: 得出了确保系统渐近稳定和数值安全的充分条件，并通过数值仿真验证了这些条件的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的时变编码-解码参数设计方法能够有效保障加密反馈控制系统的稳定性和计算安全性，适用于动态密钥环境。

Abstract: This study analyzes dynamic-key Learning-with-Errors (LWE)-based encrypted state-feedback control systems with time-varying encoders and decoders. Using a Lyapunov-based approach, we derive conditions on the time-varying encoder and decoder parameters that ensure both asymptotic stability and numerical safety by preventing overflow. The validity of the derived conditions is confirmed through numerical examples.

</details>


### [92] [Observability Blocking in a Linear Synchronization Network with Partial State Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25259)
*Alexis Moreno,Abdullah Al Maruf*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在部分状态测量下阻断大型网络系统中对手推断系统动态的方法，通过输出反馈和基于观测器的控制策略实现可观测性阻断，并保留部分或全部开环特征值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的全状态反馈方法需要完全的状态访问，在大规模网络中不实用，因此需要在有限状态信息下实现可观测性阻断的方法。

Method: 提出了两种控制策略：一是采用输出反馈实现可观测性阻断并保留部分开环特征值；二是利用观测器重构系统状态，实现全状态反馈控制并保留所有特征值，还扩展到了分布式框架。

Result: 数值例子验证了所提方法的有效性和适用范围，能够在部分状态测量条件下成功阻断系统可观测性。

Conclusion: 所提出的控制策略能够在仅有部分状态信息的情况下有效阻止对手对系统动态的推断，同时保持系统的重要动态特性，适用于大规模网络系统。

Abstract: Large-scale networked systems are increasingly vulnerable to adversaries that can infer system dynamics from a small set of compromised nodes. This paper addresses the problem of blocking such inference using limited state information. While existing state-feedback methods achieve observability blocking with eigenvalue preservation, they require full state access and are impractical for large networks. We propose multiple control strategies that operate under partial state measurements. The first approach employs output feedback to achieve observability blocking while preserving a subset of open-loop eigenvalues. The second approach leverages an observer to reconstruct the system state and enables full-state feedback control, preserving all eigenvalues and providing greater flexibility in sensor placement. We further extend the observer-based design to a distributed framework. Numerical examples demonstrate the scope and validity of the proposed methods.

</details>


### [93] [SonicNudge: Controlled Displacement of Hovering UAVs via Estimator-Controller Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25319)
*Shaocheng Luo,Ashir Raza,Haocheng Meng,David Hunt,Miroslav Pajic*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: SonicNudge是一种新型无人机攻击方法，通过超声波共振扰动陀螺仪，利用估计器与控制器的耦合关系，将低层惯性误差转化为对无人机位置的可控偏移，威胁多种悬停或慢速任务的安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统无人机位移攻击主要针对直接提供位置或平移运动信息的传感器（如GNSS和光流），而本文旨在探索仅通过干扰陀螺仪这类惯性传感器是否也能实现有效攻击，揭示被忽视的低层攻击面。

Method: 提出SonicNudge攻击原语，利用超声波引发陀螺仪谐振，产生持续的姿态估计偏差；通过分析PX4飞控架构中估计器与控制器的耦合机制，结合81次仿真和10余次室内外实验证明该偏差可导致无人机悬停点偏移。

Result: 实验证明，即使微小的陀螺仪扰动也能在特定条件下（如低速或悬停）引发米级的位置偏移；位移程度受估计器权重、偏差可观测性和闭环位置校正能力影响。

Conclusion: 研究表明，无人机安全防护不应局限于导航信号欺骗防御，还需重视低层惯性传感器错误及估计-控制耦合带来的隐蔽攻击风险，应加强对此类攻击面的防护设计。

Abstract: UAV displacement attacks have traditionally relied on spoofing sensors that directly report position or translational motion, such as GNSS and optical flow. In this work, we introduce SonicNudge, a new attack primitive that instead targets the gyroscope and shows that low-level inertial errors can be transformed into controlled displacement of hovering or slow-moving UAVs. The attack exploits estimator--controller coupling: a small gyroscope perturbation by ultrasonic resonance can persist as an attitude-estimation bias, and the flight controller can convert this biased estimate into a shifted hover point. This behavior is especially relevant to UAV tasks that require hovering, station-keeping, slow approach, or precise final alignment, such as perimeter denial, inspection, docking, landing alignment, and close-proximity operation, where meter-scale position errors can be operationally meaningful. We analyze this attack primitive in a PX4-style flight stack and validate it through 81 simulation runs and more than 10 indoor/outdoor physical experiments, showing that displacement is governed by estimator weighting, bias observability, and closed-loop position correction. Our study suggests that UAV and vehicle-system security should look beyond direct navigation spoofing and pay closer attention to low-level inertial errors and estimator--controller coupling as a subtle but important attack surface.

</details>


### [94] [Scalable Tube-Tightened Multi-Agent Safety via Certified Constraint Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25323)
*Armel Koulong*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于指数控制屏障函数（eCBF）的分布式模型预测控制约束缩减方法，通过几何自适应选择和Farkas证书验证，在保证安全性的前提下显著降低计算复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多智能体系统中，传统基于tube-tightened eCBF的安全控制方法会引入大量约束，导致计算负担随规模增长而急剧上升，限制了其在实时分布式控制中的应用。

Method: 在每个预测步中，仅保留部分关键的agent-agent和agent-obstacle eCBF半空间约束，并利用Farkas引理生成证书，验证缩减后的可行集仍包含于完整紧致集内；针对平面输入，通过最大角度间隙判断锥覆盖情况，结合闭式求解的圆锥乘子与标称感知偏移实现高效约束筛选。

Result: 在10个跟随者、4个障碍物的仿真中，该方法平均保留更少的安全约束，完全复现了完整滤波器对名义控制的接受/拒绝决策，未出现真实安全违规，且随着约束数量和预测时域增加，计算效率提升显著。

Conclusion: 所提约束缩减方法在严格保持tube-eCBF鲁棒安全保证的同时，大幅降低了分布式MPC的在线计算开销，适用于大规模多智能体系统的实时安全控制。

Abstract: This paper develops a certified constraint-reduction method for distributed model predictive control with tube-tightened exponential control barrier functions (eCBFs) in multi-agent systems. At each prediction stage, pairwise agent--agent and agent--obstacle eCBF conditions define halfspaces in the local control space. Rather than enforcing all such halfspaces, a geometry-adaptive subset is retained and a Farkas certificate verifies that the reduced admissible set is contained in the full tightened set. For planar inputs, cone coverage is characterized through the largest angular gap: two extreme directions suffice in the strict half-plane regime, while other geometries initialize with three retained constraints and escalate only when certification fails. Conic multipliers and nominal-aware offsets are obtained in closed form, without an auxiliary optimization, and the resulting construction preserves any nominal control already admissible for the full tightened set. Consequently, the reduced controller inherits the robust safety guarantee of the underlying tube-eCBF formulation. In a ten-follower, four-obstacle study, the method retained fewer safety constraints on average, reproduced the full filter's nominal accept/reject decisions with no true safety violations, and achieved increasing computational gains as the constraint count and prediction horizon grew.

</details>


### [95] [Goodput Maximization for Large Language Model Edge Inference: A Two-Phase Maskable PPO Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25543)
*Xiaojing Chen,Qi Zhang,Wei Ni,Shunqing Zhang,Yanzan Sun*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出了一种新型的两阶段可屏蔽近端策略优化算法（TP-MPPO），用于无线边缘网络中大语言模型推理服务的高吞吐量与严格服务等级目标保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在无线边缘网络中提升大语言模型推理服务的系统吞吐量并确保服务等级目标（SLO）合规，需要有效优化任务卸载和带宽分配。

Method: 第一阶段采用带有动作屏蔽机制的MPPO优化任务卸载决策，避免无效动作探索；第二阶段推导上行带宽分配的闭式解，并设计贪心算法进行下行带宽分配，以提供即时奖励，两阶段交替迭代直至收敛。

Result: 仿真结果表明，TP-MPPO相比基准方法可将系统奖励提高33.3%至87.5%，并实现最高的系统goodput。

Conclusion: TP-MPPO通过两阶段协同优化显著提升了无线边缘网络中LLM推理服务的性能，在保证SLO的同时最大化了系统吞吐量。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel two-phase maskable proximal policy optimization (TP-MPPO) algorithm, which maximizes the system goodput counting request throughput with strict service level objective (SLO) compliance for large language model (LLM) inference services in wireless edge networks. In the first phase of TP-MPPO, we optimize the task offloading decisions by MPPO with action masking mechanism, effectively avoiding exploring invalid actions and reducing the action space. In the second phase, closed-form solutions are derived for uplink bandwidth allocation; a greedy algorithm is designed for downlink bandwidth allocation to provide immediate rewards for the MPPO in the next round. The two stages alternate till convergence. Simulation results demonstrate that TP-MPPO can improve the system reward by 33.3%--87.5% compared to its benchmarks and achieve the highest goodput.

</details>


### [96] [Throughput Maximization for MapReduce-Based Collaborative Computing over Energy-Harvesting Wireless Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25549)
*Yuhang Li,Siqi Sun,Hongen Zheng,Xiaojing Chen,Shunqing Zhang,Yanzan Sun*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于MapReduce的协作计算在异构无线设备上的资源分配问题，这些设备由可再生能源供电。提出了一种结合深度确定性策略梯度（DDPG）和凸规划的DDPG-CVX算法，在无需信道状态或能量到达先验知识的情况下在线优化资源分配。仿真结果表明，该算法显著提高了吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提高可再生能源驱动的异构无线设备上MapReduce协作计算的长期平均吞吐量，需要解决计算负载、时间分配、发射功率及能耗之间的联合优化问题。

Method: 提出了DDPG-CVX算法，将DDPG用于决定每个时隙下各设备的能量预算，再通过嵌入式凸优化求解器全局最优地解析其余资源分配变量。这种方法降低了DDPG动作空间的维度，同时保持了每时隙解的质量。

Result: 仿真结果显示，与代表性基准相比，DDPG-CVX算法实现了1.25倍到32.36倍的吞吐量提升。

Conclusion: DDPG-CVX算法有效解决了无线设备上基于MapReduce的协作计算资源分配问题，能够在未知环境条件下实现高效的能量利用和高吞吐量性能。

Abstract: This paper studies resource allocation for MapReduce-based collaborative computing over heterogeneous wireless devices powered by renewable energy harvesting. We formulate a long-run average throughput maximization problem that jointly optimizes computing load, phase time allocations, transmit power, and per-device energy consumption, subject to battery evolution, CPU frequency, and latency constraints. To solve this problem online without prior knowledge of channel states or energy arrivals, we propose a DDPG-CVX algorithm that couples Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient (DDPG) with convex programming. DDPG determines the per-slot energy budget for each device from observed battery and channel states; the remaining resource allocation variables are then resolved to global optimality by an embedded convex solver. This two-phase decomposition reduces the action-space dimensionality of DDPG while preserving per-slot solution quality. Simulations show that DDPG-CVX achieves 1.25$\times$$\sim$32.36$\times$ the throughput of representative benchmarks.

</details>


### [97] [The Impact of PV Generation Forecast and Multi-Objective Control Policy on Optimal Operation of Grid Connected PV-BESS Microgrid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25703)
*Berhane Darsene Dimd,Steve Voller,Ole-Morten Midtgård*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于LSTM的光伏功率预测模型，并结合多目标调度框架，用于提升并网光伏-电池储能系统的运行性能。通过比较三种预测情景，结果表明LSTM模型能显著提高光伏自消纳率、降低电网注入和预测误差，但也带来电池使用强度增加的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光伏出力的波动性给并网微电网的可靠高效运行带来挑战，因此需要高精度的预测模型和优化的调度策略来提升系统整体性能。

Method: 采用长短期记忆网络（LSTM）构建光伏功率预测模型，并集成多目标能量调度框架；对比完美预测、持续模型和LSTM预测三种情景，评估其对自消纳率、电网成本、电网注入和电池利用等指标的影响。

Result: LSTM预测相比持续模型将均方根误差（RMSE）降低了6%，光伏自消纳率从78.1%提升至84.5%，电网注入减少82%，但电池通量增加，可能加速老化。

Conclusion: 精确的光伏功率预测对提升系统性能和运行可靠性至关重要；未来工作将聚焦于概率预测、负荷预测及支持电网的智能控制策略。

Abstract: The variability of photovoltaic (PV) generation poses significant challenges to the reliable and efficient operation of grid-connected microgrids. Accurate PV output power forecasting and efficient energy scheduling strategies are essential not only for optimizing PV system operation but also for improving the overall performance and reliability of the system. This study proposes a long short-term memory (LSTM)-based PV power forecasting model integrated with a multi-objective scheduling framework for a grid-connected PV-battery energy storage system (BESS). The proposed approach enables detailed performance monitoring and assessment by quantifying how PV forecast accuracy influences key operational metrics, including PV self-consumption ratio, grid energy cost, grid injection, and battery utilization. Three forecasting scenarios (perfect forecast, persistence model, and LSTM-based forecast) are compared to evaluate their impact on system performance and operational reliability. Results show that the LSTM-based forecast reduces root mean squared error (RMSE) by 6% compared with the persistence model, increases the PV self-consumption ratio from 78.1% to 84.5%, and reduces grid injections by 82%. The analysis also highlights trade-offs, as higher battery throughput associated with improved performance may contribute to accelerated aging. These findings demonstrate the importance of accurate PV forecasting in improving system performance and ensuring reliable operation. Future work will focus on probabilistic forecasting to properly quantify uncertainties, incorporate load prediction, and develop smart control strategies that allow grid-support functionalities from the PV side.

</details>


### [98] [Model predictive traction control system based on the Koopman operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25753)
*Josip Kir Hromatko,Šandor Ileš*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Koopman算子理论的模型预测控制方法，用于车辆牵引力控制，能够在保持良好跟踪性能的同时降低计算时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于轮胎动力学模型难以精确获取且具有强非线性，传统控制方法面临挑战，因此需要一种能够有效处理非线性的控制策略。

Method: 采用Koopman算子理论将非线性系统通过状态空间变换近似为线性系统，并设计基于该线性化模型的模型预测控制器，与标准非线性MPC进行对比。

Result: 在高保真车辆动力学仿真环境中，Koopman-based MPC与非线性MPC具有相当的参考跟踪性能，但在标准PC和嵌入式硬件上均表现出更短的执行时间。

Conclusion: 基于Koopman算子的线性模型预测控制是一种有效且计算效率更高的牵引力控制方案，适用于实际车载硬件平台。

Abstract: Due to their importance, traction control and anti-lock braking systems have become standard equipment in modern vehicles. However, accurate models of tire dynamics are often difficult to obtain and usually include nonlinearities, making their use in control systems challenging. This paper describes a traction control system based on model predictive control and Koopman operator theory, which aims to approximate nonlinear systems with linear ones through a state space transformation. A linear model predictive controller based on the Koopman predictor is compared to a standard nonlinear model predictive controller. Experiments in a high-fidelity vehicle dynamics simulation environment show a comparable reference tracking performance of the two controllers, with a reduced execution time for the proposed Koopman operator-based algorithm, both on a standard PC and embedded hardware.

</details>


### [99] [UNION: A Unified AC-OPF Framework for Topology-Varying Real-Time Grid Operation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25784)
*Kyungnam Park,Keunju Song,Yeji Lim,Suho Park,Kibaek Kim,Hongseok Kim*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为UNION的统一图基AC最优潮流（AC-OPF）框架，适用于异构系统和拓扑变化运行，通过共享图编码器、显式一致性校正和稀疏感知可微隐层，在多个系统上联合训练后表现出高效性与准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为应对实时电网运行中因工况和拓扑变化带来的挑战，需要快速且准确的AC-OPF工具；现有学习方法多局限于单一系统或固定拓扑，难以保证所有运行约束的满足。

Method: 提出UNION框架，包含共享图编码器、标量门控聚合与显式一致性校正机制，嵌入交流潮流方程的稀疏感知可微隐层，并通过原始-对偶训练和确定性恢复层处理不等式约束。

Result: 在一个涵盖七个系统的联合训练模型中，包括4,492节点的韩国实际电网，测试实例平均目标差距为1.23%，99.56%的实例满足所有运行限制；在N-1故障和时变拓扑下仍保持良好性能，轻量在线微调后五天内覆盖率仍达100%且间隙为2.51%；推理时间在最大三个系统上为55-58毫秒（预恢复），含恢复共108-114毫秒。

Conclusion: UNION表明，一个联合训练、物理一致的模型可有效支持跨异构系统和动态拓扑的实时AC-OPF，具备良好的泛化性、鲁棒性和实用性。

Abstract: Secure real-time grid operation requires fast AC optimal power flow (AC-OPF) tools that stay accurate and feasible as operating conditions and topology change. Learning-based methods have advanced, but most are trained per system or per topology, and delivering an operating point that satisfies every operational limit remains challenging. This paper proposes UNION, a unified graph-based AC-OPF framework for heterogeneous systems and topology-varying operation. UNION proposes a shared graph encoder, a scalar-gated aggregation with explicit consensus correction, and a sparse-aware differentiable implicit layer embedding the AC power-flow equations. The remaining inequalities are handled by primal-dual training and the deterministic restoration layer. A single model trained jointly across seven systems, including a real-world 4,492-bus Korean transmission grid, attains a 1.23% mean objective gap and satisfies every operational limit on 99.56% of test instances. It sustains this under zero-shot $N-1$ contingencies, i.e., line and generator outages, and over five days of time-varying Korean topologies; it retains full snapshot coverage at a 2.51% gap under lightweight online fine-tuning. UNION pre-restoration inference takes 55$-$58 ms per instance on the three largest systems, and 108$-$114 ms including restoration. These results indicate that one jointly trained, physics-consistent model can support real-time AC-OPF across heterogeneous systems and evolving topologies.

</details>


### [100] [Hard-Constrained Sampling on Embedded Riemannian Manifolds via Adjoint Schrödinger Bridges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25838)
*Mattia Mosso,Jaemoo Choi,Heng Yang*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于随机最优控制理论的、在光滑紧致连通黎曼流形上对非归一化玻尔兹曼分布进行采样的新方法，通过在状态空间层面保持可行性，实现了内在的流形约束控制扩散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多任务需要在流形上的非归一化玻尔兹曼分布中采样，现有方法难以有效处理弯曲空间中的约束问题。

Method: 基于伴随匹配和伴随薛定谔桥采样，结合随机最优控制理论，在内蕴几何结构上构建受控扩散过程，确保状态始终位于流形上。

Result: 该方法在多个物理应用中得到了实证验证，能够在复杂流形上高效准确地进行采样。

Conclusion: 所提方法为流形上的概率采样提供了理论严谨且实用的新框架，拓展了现有采样技术的适用范围。

Abstract: A variety of tasks require sampling from unnormalized Boltzmann distributions supported on manifolds. Building upon the foundations of adjoint matching and adjoint Schrödinger bridge sampling, this paper provides a theoretically justified method, through the lens of stochastic optimal control, to address this problem on smooth, compact, path-connected embedded Riemannian manifolds. As an element of novelty compared to existing literature, feasibility is enforced at the level of the state space, meaning the controlled diffusion is defined intrinsically on the curved space. Empirical validations are provided for several physics applications.

</details>


<div id='cs.ET'></div>

# cs.ET [[Back]](#toc)

### [101] [Simulating Cognitive Smart Freight Corridors with Agent-Based Models and Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25193)
*Madelaine Martinez-Ferguson,Chun Wang,Mustafa Can Camur,Xueping Li*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合物理基础设施、V2X通信和基于强化学习的决策层的智能货运走廊多智能体建模框架，用于货车编队与充电协调，实验表明认知场景可提升通行量并减少拥堵，辅助场景可显著节能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有货运中连网自动驾驶车辆（CAV）部署依赖昂贵的物理实验和无法适应动态行为的预设控制策略，亟需更灵活高效的仿真方法。

Method: 采用耦合物理基础设施层、V2X通信层和集成强化学习（RL）与多智能体强化学习（MARL）决策层的基于智能体建模（ABM）框架，评估基线、辅助与认知三种场景。

Result: 认知场景相比基线显著提高通行量并降低拥堵；辅助场景通过编队实现明显的每公里能耗节约；敏感性分析显示高需求下智能走廊优势更明显，且MARL比规则方法更高效利用固定充电能力。

Conclusion: 所提出的ABM与RL/MARL结合框架能有效模拟智能货运走廊中自适应车辆行为，支持在复杂动态环境下优化交通流、能源使用与系统鲁棒性，为未来CAV部署提供可行路径。

Abstract: Smart freight corridors offer a practical pathway for connected and automated vehicle (CAV) deployment in freight transportation, but physical experimentation is expensive and existing approaches rely on predefined control policies that cannot capture adaptive behaviors. This paper presents an agent-based modeling (ABM) framework coupling a physical infrastructure layer, a connectivity layer (V2X), and a decision layer integrating reinforcement learning (RL) and multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) for platoon formation and charging coordination. We evaluate three scenarios (Baseline, Assisted, and Cognitive) using throughput, congestion, energy, emissions, and robustness metrics. Preliminary results indicate that the Cognitive scenario achieves higher throughput and lower congestion than the baseline, while the Assisted scenario delivers meaningful energy savings per kilometer through platooning. Sensitivity analysis shows that the throughput advantage of the smart corridor widens under conditions with high demand and that MARL coordination extracts greater utilization from fixed charging capacity than rule-based assignment.

</details>


### [102] [GPU-Accelerated Quantum Annealing-Inspired UAV Path Planning for Smart Agriculture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25376)
*Maho Hirahara,Aohan Li*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于GPU并行化Ising求解器的新型路径规划优化框架，通过将问题转化为QUBO模型并在Fixstars Amplify平台上实现并行退火，显著提升了大规模路径规划的计算效率和求解质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统路径规划方法受限于局部最优、可扩展性差和收敛速度慢，难以有效应对大规模问题。为此，本文旨在通过计算架构的并行化而非算法改进来突破这些限制。

Method: 提出一种基于GPU的并行化Ising求解框架，模拟量子退火原理，在Fixstars Amplify平台上实现并行退火；将路径规划问题建模为QUBO形式，转化为伊辛模型的能量最小化问题，并利用GPU进行大规模并行状态搜索。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在飞行路径搜索中始终优于遗传算法和模拟退火，能够在问题规模增大时保持稳定的计算性能，并显著缩短计算时间。

Conclusion: 所提方法具有强健性和良好可扩展性，适用于下一代大规模智能农业等需要实时路径规划的应用场景。

Abstract: Traditional path planning methods are often constrained by local optima, limited scalability, and slow convergence, which significantly restrict their effectiveness in solving large-scale problems. To address these limitations, this paper shifts the problem-solving paradigm from algorithmic refinement to parallelization of computational architecture. We propose a novel optimization framework utilizing a Graphics Processing Unit (GPU)-parallelized Ising solver. Our method mimics the operational principles of quantum annealing on GPU hardware, enabling rapid search for the minimum-energy state of Ising models. Unlike physical quantum devices, which are often constrained by the number of qubits, our approach leverages the Fixstars Amplify (FA) platform to perform parallel annealing on highly parallelized GPUs. This enables the simultaneous evaluation of thousands of potential path candidates and vast state transitions. By leveraging large-scale parallel processing, the core strength of this framework lies in minimizing computation time even as the problem scale increases. Furthermore, to solve the path planning problem using the FA platform, we formulate the problem as a Quadratic Unconstrained Binary Optimization (QUBO) model. This formulation converts the objectives of flight constraints and operational time minimization into an energy state, enabling problem processing via the Ising-based architecture. Simulation results demonstrate that our proposed method consistently identifies superior flight paths while maintaining stable computational performance compared with the genetic algorithm and simulated annealing method. These findings highlight its potential as a robust, scalable real- time solution for next-generation large-scale smart agriculture.

</details>


### [103] [Study of Resistive Switching Dynamics and Memory States Equilibria in Analog Filamentary Conductive-Metal-Oxide/HfOx ReRAM via Compact Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25767)
*Matteo Galetta,Donato Francesco Falcone,Victoria Clerico,Wooseok Choi,Stephan Menzel,Antonio La Porta,Tommaso Stecconi,Folkert Horst,Bert Jan Offrein,Valeria Bragaglia*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种先进的基于物理机制的模拟型导电金属氧化物/氧化铪（CMO/HfOx）ReRAM器件紧凑模型，能够准确再现多种操作条件下的电阻开关特性，并揭示了脉冲条件下记忆状态在对称点附近的稳定机制，支持神经形态计算与非易失性存储器的设计优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ReRAM器件的紧凑模型无法准确描述其模拟电阻开关行为，尤其是在宽范围操作条件下的动态特性，限制了其在类脑计算和非易失性存储中的应用。

Method: 该研究发展了一个基于物理机制的紧凑模型，扩展了离子迁移与电子跃迁之间的动态相互作用，并考虑了寄生电阻元件的影响，通过多种电压输入下的仿真验证模型性能。

Result: 模型成功再现了准静态I-V曲线、单脉冲下的SET切换动力学以及双极性脉冲序列下的累积电导调制；发现了脉冲条件下记忆状态在对称点附近稳定的物理准则，并建立了可视化映射平衡态的方法。

Conclusion: 该模型为CMO/HfOx基ReRAM器件在模拟神经形态系统和非易失性存储架构中的电路设计与优化提供了准确且可靠的仿真基础。

Abstract: Resistive Random Access Memory (ReRAM) devices offer a promising solution for next-generation non-volatile memory and neuromorphic computing systems. Yet, existing compact models fail to capture analog resistive switching behavior of ReRAM devices. This work presents an advanced physics-based compact model for analog filamentary Conductive-Metal-Oxide (CMO)/HfOx ReRAM, capable of reproducing switching characteristics over a broad range of operating conditions. Compared to the state-of-the-art, the model extends the dynamic interplay between ion migration and electron hopping, while also accounting for parasitic resistive elements. Simulations of various voltage inputs are tested to reproduce quasi-static I-V curves, SET switching kinetics under single-pulse programming conditions, and analog accumulative conductance modulation upon bipolar identical pulse streams. Additional simulations reveal the physical criterion underlying the stabilization of the CMO/HfOx-based ReRAM memory state around the equilibrium point, namely symmetry point, under pulsing conditions when a fading memory mechanism emerges. Building upon the evidence of such equilibrium stabilization under pulsing and quasi-static conditions, a procedure is established to visualize and map equilibrium memory states across different input domains. The physical model supports design optimization of switching behavior for analog neuromorphic systems and non-volatile memory architectures. It also enables accurate integrated circuit simulations with CMO/HfOx-based ReRAM technology.

</details>


### [104] [Update Disturbance-Resilient Analog ReRAM Crossbar Arrays for In-Memory Deep Learning Accelerators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25781)
*Wooseok Choi,Tommaso Stecconi,Donato Francesco Falcone,Matteo Galetta,Victoria Clerico,Elisa Zaccaria,Mamidala Saketh Ram,Antonio La Porta,Folkert Horst,Daniel Jubin,Matias Senger,Marilyne Sousa,Steffen Reidt,Ralph Heller,Bernabe Linares-Barranco,Valeria Bragaglia,Bert Jan Offrein*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于HfOx层中纳米级导电细丝形成的电阻开关导电金属氧化物（CMO）的ReRAM器件，实现了快速、非易失性模拟切换，并具有优异的抗更新干扰能力，适用于存内训练的深度学习加速器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为实现更可持续和节能的人工智能，需要在存储器内加速训练算法，但目前仍处于研究初期，特别是在全并行权重更新过程中存在权重值扰动的问题。

Method: 采用350 nm硅技术开发ReRAM器件，利用HfOx层中的纳米级导电细丝形成CMO结构，并通过COMSOL Multiphysics仿真分析其抗干扰机制，同时在后端集成的ReRAM阵列芯片上验证并行权重映射。

Result: 器件展现出60 ns的快速非易失性模拟切换，可承受超过10万次脉冲的更新干扰；仿真表明细丝诱导的热电能量集中导致对输入电压幅度的高度非线性响应；实验验证了无干扰的并行权重映射。

Conclusion: 该ReRAM器件具备高抗扰性和高速性能，结合硬件感知神经网络仿真，证明其在支持全并行权重更新的存内深度学习加速器中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Resistive memory (ReRAM) technologies with crossbar array architectures hold significant potential for analog AI accelerator hardware, enabling both in-memory inference and training. Recent developments have successfully demonstrated inference acceleration by offloading compute-heavy training workloads to off-chip digital processors. However, in-memory acceleration of training algorithms is crucial for more sustainable and power-efficient AI, but still in an early stage of research. This study addresses in-memory training acceleration using analog ReRAM arrays, focusing on a key challenge during fully parallel weight updates: disturbances of the weight values in cross-point devices. A ReRAM device solution is presented on 350 nm silicon technology, utilizing a resistive switching conductive metal oxide (CMO) formed on a nanoscale conductive filament within a HfOx layer. The devices not only exhibit 60 ns fast, non-volatile analog switching, but also demonstrates outstanding resilience to update disturbances, enduring over 100k pulses. The disturbance tolerance of the ReRAM is analyzed using COMSOL Multiphysics simulations, modeling the filament-induced thermoelectric energy concentration that results in a highly nonlinear device responses to input voltage amplitudes. Disturbance-free parallel weight mapping is also demonstrated on the back-end-of-line integrated ReRAM array chip. Finally, comprehensive hardware-aware neural network simulations validate the potential of our ReRAM for in-memory deep learning accelerators capable of fully parallel weight updates.

</details>


### [105] [Simultaneous Digital Communication and Deformation Sensing over a Single Stretchable Interconnect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25801)
*Yuji Isano,Hiroki Ota*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 提出了一种通信集成的变形感知架构，通过拉伸液态金属互连的应变诱导电阻变化对数字信号进行幅度调制，实现可拉伸混合器件的自变形感知与数字通信同步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可拉伸电子系统通常将拉伸性仅视为机械属性，未利用器件变形来编码其力学状态，且传统刚性电路架构难以兼容应变传感需求。

Method: 利用可拉伸液态金属互连在应变下的电阻变化，对标准UART数字信号进行幅度调制，同时读取幅度变化和数字模式，实现通信与自感知同步。

Result: 在多节点系统中验证了该架构，并应用于可穿戴传感和自变形映射设备，实现了无需额外传感器的变形感知。

Conclusion: 该研究从系统层面提升了刚性电路与柔性元件的融合，提供了一种新型可拉伸电子系统设计范式，能内在利用自身变形作为功能信息。

Abstract: Stretchable hybrid electronics integrate rigid solid-state electronics with stretchable materials and structures to achieve both high deformability and stable electronic performance. However, most existing systems treat stretchability only as a mechanical attribute without exploiting device deformation to encode its own mechanical state. This problem arises from adapting conventional rigid circuit architectures to stretchable substrates, affording a loss in compatibility with the sensors required for strain measurement. This study addresses this issue by proposing a communication-integrated deformation sensing architecture for stretchable hybrid devices. In the proposed approach, standard universal asynchronous receiver-transmitter digital signals transmitted between rigid nodes are amplitude-modulated by strain-induced resistance changes in stretchable liquid metal interconnects. By reading both amplitude changes and digital patterns, the system enables simultaneous digital communication and self-deformation sensing without requiring additional stretchable sensing elements. The architecture is demonstrated in a multi-node system and applied to wearable sensing and self-deformation mapping devices. By extending the integration of rigid circuits and soft elements from the hardware level to the system level, this study provides a novel design paradigm for stretchable electronic systems that inherently utilize their own deformation as functional information.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [106] [The Sampling Distribution of the Log-Euclidean Distance Between Sample Correlation Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25288)
*Argyn Kuketayev*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文推导了在零假设下两个独立估计的相关矩阵之间内在对数欧氏距离的渐近分布，提出了用于检测相关结构是否发生真实变化的统计方法，并提供了计算尾部概率和检验功效的实用工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量化金融和多元统计中，比较不同时间或压力情景下的相关矩阵至关重要，但样本估计噪声常掩盖真实结构变化，因此需要一种能够区分估计误差与真实差异的统计方法。

Method: 基于广义Fisher变换（GFT）坐标，推导出在总体相关矩阵相同的情况下，两个独立估计的相关矩阵之间的缩放平方距离的渐近分布；在高斯独立采样下简化为参数自由的4χ²_d分布，并提供闭式累积生成函数、鞍点近似和切尔诺夫包络等工具进行尾部概率校准。

Result: 得到了渐近分布形式及其在特定条件下的简化版本，提出了期望距离的经验法则（E[d_LE] ≲ 2√(d/n)），并实现了插件一致性、显式协方差分解、与坐标Wald检验的比较以及局部功效刻画。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效量化由估计误差引起的平均距离，为判断相关矩阵是否发生结构性变化提供了可靠的统计基础和实用计算工具。

Abstract: Comparing correlation matrices across time or stress scenarios is critical in quantitative finance and multivariate statistics, yet sample estimation noise often obscures whether an observed distance reflects a true structural shift. We derive the asymptotic sampling distribution of the intrinsic off-log (log-Euclidean) distance between two independently estimated full-rank correlation matrices under the null hypothesis that their population correlation matrices coincide. Under general sampling with finite fourth moments, the scaled squared distance converges to a weighted sum of independent $χ_1^2$ variables, with weights determined by the asymptotic covariance of the Generalized Fisher Transformation (GFT) coordinates. Under Gaussian sampling at independence, this simplifies to a parameter-free $4χ_d^2$ law. To calibrate tail probabilities, we provide closed-form cumulant generating functions, Lugannani--Rice saddlepoint quantiles, and an explicit Chernoff envelope requiring no root-finding. The first moment of the limiting law establishes a simple rule of thumb for the baseline expected distance under the null hypothesis ($\operatorname E[d_{\mathrm{LE}}] \lesssim 2\sqrt{d/n}$ near independence), quantifying the average separation induced strictly by estimation error. We establish plug-in consistency, present an explicit Gaussian covariance factorization, compare the distance statistic with coordinate Wald tests, and characterize its local power.

</details>


### [107] [Berry--Esseen bounds and bootstrap approximations for the Hilbert-space norm of $U$-statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25463)
*Nilanjan Chakraborty,Sayan Das*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文建立了非渐近的Berry-Esseen界和适用于希尔伯特空间中U-统计量范数的自助逼近方法，适用于核取值于随样本量变化的可分希尔伯特空间的三角阵列框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在更一般的函数空间（如随样本大小变化的希尔伯特空间）中对U-统计量进行精确的分布逼近，并处理高维和无限维情形下的统计推断问题。

Method: 结合希尔伯特空间中的Stein方法与Hoeffding分解中高阶项的精细控制，分析误差来源；同时研究经验、高斯加权和jackknife乘子自助法的逼近误差。

Result: 得到了依赖于核的四阶矩和Hájek投影协方差算子谱几何的逼近误差界；证明了自助法具有渐近正确的检验水平，并在协方差相关的分离速率下保持对备择假设的一致性；针对Kendall's tau系数向量检验，建立了匹配的极小极大下界。

Conclusion: 所提出的理论能够适应奇异或近低秩的协方差算子，只要足够多的谱质量保留在主导方向之外，且在密集高斯相关备择下达到极小极大最优的分离速率。

Abstract: We establish non-asymptotic Berry--Esseen bounds and bootstrap approximations for the Hilbert-space norm of nondegenerate $U$-statistics. Our triangular-array framework allows the kernel to take values in a separable Hilbert space $H_n$ that may vary with the sample size, thereby covering both infinite-dimensional spaces and Euclidean spaces of increasing dimension. The Gaussian approximation combines a Hilbert-space version of Stein's method with refined control of the higher-order terms in the Hoeffding decomposition. Its error depends on fourth moments of the kernel and on the spectral geometry of the covariance operator of the Hájek projection, particularly the proportion of squared spectral mass lying outside the leading eigendirection. Consequently, the theory accommodates singular and approximately low-rank covariance operators, provided that sufficient spectral mass remains beyond the leading direction. For $H_n=\mathbb{R}^{d_n}$, we obtain dimension-explicit bounds under coordinatewise fourth-moment conditions. We also establish approximation bounds for the empirical, Gaussian-weighted, and jackknife multiplier bootstraps, yielding tests with asymptotically correct size and consistency against alternatives at covariance-dependent separation rates. For testing the vector of pairwise Kendall's tau coefficients, a matching minimax lower bound shows that the resulting separation rate is minimax rate-optimal over dense Gaussian correlation alternatives.

</details>


### [108] [Estimation of a regression function from dependent data by over-parametrized deep neural networks learned by gradient descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25718)
*Michael Kohler,Adam Krzyżak,Vincent Molinero Römer*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了从指数β混合数据中估计回归函数的问题，采用L2误差作为评价标准。通过梯度下降法训练具有逻辑激活函数的深度神经网络模型，并分析了(p,C)光滑回归函数下的期望L2误差收敛速率。特别地，当设计分布在d*维流形上时，证明了估计的期望L2误差的收敛速率仅依赖于d*而非设计的原始维度d。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对非独立同分布的指数β混合数据，如何有效估计回归函数并获得良好的收敛速率是一个重要问题。现有方法可能未充分考虑数据依赖性及高维带来的“维度灾难”。本文旨在利用深度神经网络处理此类复杂依赖结构的数据，并在低维流形假设下实现更快的收敛速率。

Method: 定义基于逻辑激活函数的深度神经网络回归估计量，使用梯度下降法学习所有参数；采用L2误差（关于设计分布积分）作为评估准则；理论分析集中在(p,C)光滑函数类和设计分布位于低维流形上的情况。

Result: 得到了深度神经网络估计量在(p,C)光滑回归函数下的期望L2误差的收敛速率；特别地，当设计分布集中在d*维流形上时，收敛速率仅依赖于内在维度d*，从而缓解了高维带来的影响。

Conclusion: 深度神经网络能够有效处理指数β混合数据的回归估计问题，在低维流形假设下可实现与内在维度相关的最优收敛速率，表明其在处理高维但结构简单数据时的良好性能。

Abstract: Estimation of a regression function from exponentially $β$-mixing data is considered. The $L_2$ error with integration with
  respect to the design is used as the error criterion.
  Deep neural network estimates with logistic activation function
  are defined, where all parameters are learned by gradient descent.
  The rate of convergence of the expected $L_2$ error is analyzed for $(p,C)$-smooth regression functions. In the special case that the design is concentrated
  on a $d^*$-dimensional manifold, it is shown that the expected $L_2$ error
  of the estimate achieves a rate of convergence which depends on $d^*$
  and not on the dimension $d$ of the design.

</details>


### [109] [Learning of deep neural network regression estimates using gradient descent with pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25743)
*Michael Kohler,Vincent Molinero Römer,Adam Krzyżak*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于随机剪枝的深度神经网络回归方法，使用梯度下降和数据依赖步长，在L2误差下达到近似最优的收敛速率，并能避免高维带来的维度灾难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在回归函数估计中实现最优收敛速度并克服维度灾难，特别是在高维数据集中在低维流形附近的情况下。

Method: 采用初始随机剪枝的全连接深度神经网络，以logistic squasher为激活函数，通过带有数据依赖非恒定步长的梯度下降法拟合数据，并使用关于设计变量的L2误差作为评价标准。

Result: 该方法在回归函数(p,C)--光滑时，能达到（至多差一个对数因子）最优极小极大收敛速率；且当预测变量集中在低维流形邻域时，可避免维度灾难。模拟数据验证了其有限样本下的良好表现。

Conclusion: 所提出的深度神经网络估计方法在理论上具有优良的收敛性质，并能在低维结构存在时有效应对高维回归问题。

Abstract: Estimation of a regression function from independent and identically distributed data is considered. The $L_2$ error with integration with respect to the design variable is used as the error criterion. An initially randomly pruned fully connected deep neural network with logistic squasher as activation function is fitted to the data via gradient descent, using a data-dependent choice of non-constant stepsizes during gradient descent. It is shown that this network achieves (up to a logarithmic factor) the optimal minimax rate of convergence in case that the regression function is $(p,C)$--smooth. Here the estimate is able to circumvent the curse of dimensionality provided the predictors are concentrated in the neighborhood of a low dimensional manifold. The finite sample size performance of the estimate is illustrated by applying it to the simulated data.

</details>


### [110] [Frequency Domain Bootstrap for Functional Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25765)
*Daniel Rademacher,Jens-Peter Kreiss,Efstathios Paparoditis*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于函数时间序列的频域自助法，适用于谱均值算子类，能一致估计二阶和四阶结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了更好地估计函数时间序列中谱均值算子的分布特性，尤其是其二阶和四阶矩结构。

Method: 通过在正傅里叶频率上生成独立的伪周期图算子，并结合投影分解与重采样方法构造功能重复样本。

Result: 所提方法能够一致地估计谱均值算子的整个二阶及有限维四阶结构，并在m随样本量增大时实现对全部四阶结构的一致估计。

Conclusion: 该频域自助法在较弱条件下具有渐近有效性，适用于广泛的函数过程类别。

Abstract: A frequency domain bootstrap procedure for functional time series is proposed and applied to the class of spectral mean operators. The procedure works by first generating independent pseudo periodogram operators across the positive Fourier frequencies using an estimator of the spectral density operator involved. Functional replicates of the spectral mean operators of interest are then generated. Through an additive, projection-based decomposition of the bootstrapped spectral mean operator, its leading $m$-dimensional part is properly complemented to also capture the relevant fourth order characteristics of the process. The complementation is achieved by means of a resampling procedure based on convolved periodogram operators of subsamples. The resulting bootstrap spectral mean operator consistently estimates the entire second order as well as the $m$-dimensional fourth order structure of the distribution of spectral mean operators. By allowing for the decomposition parameter $m$ to increase to infinity as the sample size increases to infinity, consistency in estimating the entire fourth order structure of the process also is achieved. The asymptotic theory developed investigates properties of the procedure for fixed and for increasing $m$ and establishes validity of the frequency domain bootstrap proposal under rather weak conditions on the underlying functional process class.

</details>


### [111] [Pooling Mobility Obscures Epidemic Invasion Routes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25766)
*Tiandong Wang,Wei Yang*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 该论文研究了在流行病模型中将多种交通方式（如航空、通勤）合并为单一网络所带来的信息损失问题，提出多层流动的极值统计方法，揭示合并过程会保留输入感染总量但改变或丢失传播路径与模式的组成信息，并给出了恢复路径组成的条件及其对干预策略的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现有流行病模型中，不同交通方式常被合并为单一加权网络，虽保留总输入感染数，却丢失了具体的传播路径与模式信息，导致难以实施针对性干预。本文旨在量化这种信息损失，明确何时能恢复真实传播路径，并评估其对公共卫生决策的影响。

Method: 基于具有重尾变异的有向多层流模型，采用极值统计理论分析合并过程对输入感染事件的径向（规模）和角向（组成）特征的影响；提出可恢复性条件，并引入监测决策成本来量化信息损失；通过美国流感和意大利新冠疫情的对比案例进行实证校准与验证。

Result: 发现输入感染的规模（径向尾指数）在合并中保持不变，但其跨层与路径的组成（角向部分）会被重加权甚至不可逆地融合；提出了恢复路径组成的充要条件；在美国流感案例中，航空运输提供了通勤之外的重要信息，分层排序比合并排序更能识别高风险路径；在早期意大利新冠疫情中，通勤数据有助于疫情起点重建，但航空传播归属仍不明确。

Conclusion: 合并多层流动性网络虽可用于总体输入感染监测，但通常无法识别具体传播模式或路径，可能误导精准干预；因此，在设计监测系统和干预策略时，应保留并分析多层流动结构，避免因信息丢失而导致决策失效。

Abstract: Epidemic models often pool air travel, commuting, and other mobility layers into a single weighted network. Pooling keeps the total imported infections into a region but discards the transport mode and route that delivered them, the information a mode-specific intervention needs. We make this precise for directed multilayer flows with heavy-tailed variation. Pooling acts as an asymmetric filter. The magnitude of an extreme importation, its radial tail index, is an exact invariant of sampling and nonnegative aggregation, whereas its composition across layers and routes, the angular part, is reweighted by layer-specific sampling and can be irrecoverably merged. We give a necessary and sufficient condition for recovering route composition, attach a surveillance decision cost to the loss, and show that the first established route need not be the busiest. A controlled metapopulation study calibrates the cost, and two contrasting reconstructions show where it bites. In US pandemic influenza, air adds fitted information beyond commuting, and a layer-resolved ranking targets more air-import burden than a pooled one. In the early Italian COVID-19 wave, commuting improves onset reconstruction while air attribution stays unresolved. Pooling can therefore support total-importation surveillance but cannot in general identify the mode or route behind an importation.

</details>


### [112] [Limiting properties of monotone rearrangements of estimators when the truth is flat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25811)
*Holger Dette,Marius Kroll,Stanislav Volgushev*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了在平坦区域中单调重排估计量的分布性质，提出了在均匀密度和独立性下的重排Copula模型中的渐近行为，并证明了一类基于重排Copula的相依性度量的渐近正态性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单调重排估计量的分布理论无法覆盖目标函数无局部排序的平坦区域，本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 通过研究两种典型平坦情形下的重排估计量：一是均匀密度的直方图估计量的非递减重排；二是基于经验Copula增量和棋盘逼近的独立性下重排Copula估计量，并结合中心化与重缩放方法分析其弱收敛性。

Result: 1. 在(0,1)区间紧子集上，均匀密度的重排直方图估计量经中心化后以参数速率实现函数弱收敛；2. 两类重排Copula估计量在[0,1]^2上弱收敛于一个此前未知的积分高斯过程；3. 证明了一类重排Copula相依性度量的渐近正态性。

Conclusion: 重排估计量在平坦区域仍具有良好的渐近性质，且表现出与严格单调情形显著不同的行为，拓展了单调重排理论的应用范围。

Abstract: Monotone rearrangements provide a simple way to enforce shape constraints of an estimator, but existing distributional theory does not cover flat regions, where the target induces no local ordering. We study rearranged estimators in two canonical flat settings. First, for a histogram estimator of the uniform density, we establish functional weak convergence of its non-decreasing rearrangement at the parametric rate on compact subsets of the interval $(0,1)$ after an additional deterministic centering. This result is strikingly different from what is known for strictly monotone densities. Second, we consider two rearranged estimators of rearranged copulas under independence, based on empirical-copula increments and on a checkerboard approximation. After appropriate centering and rescaling, both estimators converge weakly on $[0,1]^2$ to an integrated Gaussian process which was not known before. We further use these results to prove asymptotic normality for a broad class of rearranged copula-based dependence measures, which were recently discussed in Strothmann et al. (2024).

</details>


### [113] [Weighted Estimation by Discrete-time Sparse Domination on Martingale Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25892)
*Wei Chen,Chaoyue Zhang,Gege Zhang*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文引入了条件稀疏性概念，建立了离散时间鞅变换的稀疏控制理论，推导出 sharper 的加权估计和混合范数估计，并应用于Doob极大算子及双权定量估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了改进离散时间鞅变换和Doob极大算子的加权范数估计，已有方法在sharp估计方面存在局限，需发展新的稀疏控制框架。

Method: 引入条件稀疏性作为核心性质，在离散时间滤链空间中建立稀疏控制理论，并发展适用于Doob极大算子的专用稀疏控制方法。
