Result: 获得了sharp的ApαArβ型混合加权估计，改进了已知的Lp界；恢复了Doob极大算子的sharp界；并将稀疏理论成功应用于定量双权估计。

Conclusion: 条件稀疏性为离散时间鞅提供了有效的稀疏控制框架，显著改进了多种算子的加权估计结果，拓展了稀疏理论在概率分析中的应用范围。

Abstract: Lacey used sparse domination to study the sharp weighted norm estimate of the maximal function of predictable multipliers in discrete time filtration spaces. Domelevo, Petermichl, and Škreb developed the self similarity argument known as sparse domination in an abstract martingale setting with a continuous time parameter. In our investigation, we establish sparse domination for discrete-time martingale transforms, introducing the novel concept of conditional sparsity as a core property of our approach. The conditional sparsity framework enables derivation of sharp weighted estimates and a mixed-norm estimate \( A_p^αA_r^β\) that improves upon known sharp \( L^p \) bounds. Moreover, we develop dedicated sparse domination specifically for Doob's maximal operator, recovering the sharp bound as a direct application. Finally, we focus on the application of sparse theory to quantitative two-weight estimates.

</details>


### [114] [Statistical Properties of Nonparametric MLE under Laplace Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25997)
*Yifei Xiong,Nianqiao Phyllis Ju,Vinayak Rao*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了在局部差分隐私下使用拉普拉斯噪声机制的实值数据中，通过非参数最大似然估计（NPMLE）从扰动观测中恢复潜在数据分布的问题。提出了NPMLE的有限维重构，并分析其在1-Wasserstein距离下的收敛性，揭示了收敛速度与隐私噪声尺度的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在局部差分隐私框架下，如何有效估计原始数据的分布是一个关键问题，尤其是在添加拉普拉斯噪声后。研究动机在于提升对私有化数据的统计推断能力。

Method: 采用非参数最大似然估计（NPMLE），利用拉普拉斯卷积模型将无限维优化问题转化为n维凸优化问题，并在i.i.d.采样模型下分析其统计收敛性。

Result: 证明了NPMLE可转化为基于观测集的有限维问题；当拉普拉斯噪声增长慢于n^{3/16}时，NPMLE保持一致性；若噪声达到或超过√n量级，则无法实现一致估计。

Conclusion: NPMLE在适度隐私噪声下能有效恢复潜在分布，但过强的噪声会从根本上限制估计的一致性，明确了隐私代价与统计效率之间的权衡。

Abstract: Local differential privacy (LDP) protects individuals in a dataset by perturbing each measurement before release. For real-valued data, a widely used mechanism is additive Laplace noise. We study the problem of estimating the distribution of the latent confidential data from the privatized observations via the nonparametric maximum likelihood estimator (NPMLE) under an i.i.d. sampling model. We first show that under the Laplace convolution model, the NPMLE admits a finite-dimensional reformulation in which the support is restricted to the observation set. This reduces the original infinite-dimensional optimization over all mixing distributions to an $n$-dimensional convex optimization over mixture weights, where $n$ is the sample size. We then study the statistical convergence of the NPMLE under the 1-Wasserstein distance, and explicitly connect its convergence rate with the privacy noise scale. Allowing the privacy noise level to change with the sample size, our analysis shows that the NPMLE remains consistent when the Laplace noise grows at a rate slower than $n^{3/16}$. Conversely, when the Laplace noise is of the order $\sqrt n$ or larger, no estimator can achieve uniformly consistent recovery of the latent distribution.

</details>


<div id='quant-ph'></div>

# quant-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [115] [Comment on 'Supervised quantum machine learning models are kernel methods'](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24923)
*Rajiv Krishnakumar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文指出了M. Schuld关于监督量子机器学习模型是核方法的证明中的几个小错误，这些错误不影响定理的有效性，但在计算量子核的傅里叶系数时需要注意。作者提供了修正后的推导，并指出论文中的余弦核示例存在另一个独立错误，恰好抵消了第一个错误，从而偶然得到了正确结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 修正Schuld论文附录A中定理1证明的小错误，确保在实际计算量子核傅里叶系数时的准确性。

Method: 重新推导定理1的证明过程，识别并纠正其中的错误，并分析余弦核示例中的问题。

Result: 给出了正确的傅里叶系数推导方式，并发现原论文的余弦核例子中两个错误相互抵消，最终结果正确但推导过程存在问题。

Conclusion: 尽管原始定理依然成立，但在具体实现和计算中需注意推导细节的正确性，避免依赖巧合得出的结果。

Abstract: We identify a few small errors in the proof of Theorem 1 in M. Schuld, 'Supervised quantum machine learning models are kernel methods' (arXiv:2101.11020v2), Appendix A. These do not affect the validity of the theorem, but do matter if one wants to use the appendix to explicitly compute the Fourier coefficients of a quantum kernel. We give the corrected derivation, and additionally show that the paper's worked cosine-kernel example contains a second, unrelated error that happens to cancel the first, thereby coincidentally yielding the correct result.

</details>


### [116] [Observing Bell Inequality Violation Beyond the Qubit Bound in a Spinor Bose--Einstein Condensate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24981)
*Wenxin Xu,Junshuang Hu,Guillem Müller-Rigat,Xinwei Li,Qi Liu,Matteo Fadel,Li You*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 实现了在多体系统中高维（qutrit）贝尔非局域性的观测，利用自旋-1玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚态中的集体测量验证了超越qubit限制的贝尔关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索多体系统中超越二维（qubit）的高维贝尔非局域性，突破以往仅限于qubit系统的限制。

Method: 基于集体自旋观测量构建贝尔 witness，并利用自旋交换碰撞在约3.1万原子的自旋-1 ^87Rb玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚态中产生自旋向列型压缩，实现qutrit贝尔关联的检验。

Result: 观测到-11.8(7) dB的自旋向列压缩，并显著违反了qubit系统所能达到的贝尔 witness 下限，证实了 genuine multipartite qutrit Bell correlations 的存在。

Conclusion: 自旋-1玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚态是研究多体高维贝尔非局域性的可行平台，且粗粒化的集体测量足以在宏观尺度上认证量子关联的维度。

Abstract: Correlations allowed by quantum mechanics can defy any classical explanation, with Bell nonlocality standing as their most profound and operationally powerful manifestation. While nonlocality has been demonstrated across a wide range of platforms, in many-body systems it has remained limited to ensembles of qubits, leaving the observation of higher-dimensional multipartite Bell correlations an open challenge. Here we report the observation of qutrit Bell correlations in a spin-1 $^{87}$Rb Bose-Einstein condensate via the violation of a Bell witness based on collective spin observables only. Exploiting spin-exchange collisions in an ensemble of $N \simeq 3.1 \times 10^{4}$ atoms, we generate spin-nematic squeezing of $-11.8(7)$ dB and observe a violation that surpasses the minimum bound achievable by a collection of $N$ qubits, providing direct evidence of genuine multipartite qutrit Bell correlations. Our results establish spinor Bose-Einstein condensates as a viable platform for investigating high-dimensional Bell correlations in the many-body regime, and demonstrate that coarse-grained collective measurements suffice to certify the dimensionality of quantum correlations at macroscopic scales.

</details>


### [117] [Mixed-State Symmetry-Protected Topology and Strong-to-Weak Spontaneous Symmetry Breaking in a Superconducting Qubit Array](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24993)
*Qian Cao,Zhen Bi,Kater W. Murch*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 实验研究了在五量子比特超导阵列中，通过测量和退相干如何改变一维簇态中的对称性保护拓扑序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索对称性保护拓扑序在测量和退相干下的演化行为，并揭示混合态下对称性和拓扑的特征。

Method: 利用五量子比特超导电路制备一维簇态，通过可控去相位和可调基矢测量，表征非局域弦序、长程纠缠态转变及不同关联函数的行为。

Result: 发现受控退相干选择性抑制某一对称性分支而保留另一分支；测量导致剩余量子比特在GHZ态与顺磁态间切换；丢弃测量记录后线性关联消失但非线性保真度关联仍有限，显示强-弱自发对称性破缺的有限尺寸特征。

Conclusion: 测量、平均和退相干揭示了同一簇态中不同形式的序，验证了超导电路作为研究混合态对称性与拓扑平台的可行性。

Abstract: We experimentally investigate how symmetry-protected topological order in a one-dimensional cluster state is transformed by measurement and decoherence in a five-qubit superconducting array. We first characterize the state's nonlocal string order and show that controlled dephasing selectively suppresses one symmetry sector while leaving the other robust, consistent with average symmetry-protected topological order. We then measure one sublattice in a tunable basis and show that the remaining qubits are driven between a long-range-entangled GHZ state and a paramagnetic state. When the measurement record is discarded, the conventional long-range correlator vanishes while a nonlinear fidelity correlator remains finite, providing a finite-size signature of strong-to-weak spontaneous symmetry breaking. These experiments demonstrate how conditioning, averaging, and decoherence reveal distinct manifestations of order encoded in the same underlying cluster state, and establish a superconducting-circuit setting for probing mixed-state symmetry and topology.

</details>


### [118] [Fluctuation-dissipation structure of quantum geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25006)
*Per Moosavi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子态几何结构，特别是混合态下的距离度量与曲率之间的关系，揭示其具有涨落-耗散物理意义，并推广到纯态已知的凯勒结构，为输运行为提供了几何刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于混合态在有限温度等情形下不可或缺，但其几何结构研究尚不充分，因此需要建立适用于一般量子态（纯态与混合态）的距离与曲率统一框架。

Method: 通过构建统一的距离度量与曲率之间的结构，将其与涨落和线性响应分别关联，分析其在纯态和混合态下的极限行为与几何性质。

Result: 发现该结构具有明确的涨落-耗散物理含义，混合态下的响应函数本质上探测的是混合态量子几何张量，并由此导出对一般输运行为的几何表征。

Conclusion: 建立了适用于一般量子态的几何结构理论，明确了其涨落-耗散解释，推广了纯态下的凯勒结构，并为理解量子输运提供了新的几何视角。

Abstract: The geometry of quantum states is a fundamental research area with applications ranging from band theory in condensed matter to variational algorithms in quantum information. Due to their relative simplicity, pure states are usually studied, while mixed ones are needed in general, for instance to allow for finite temperatures. The geometry of mixed states, however, is much more challenging, with important aspects yet to be explored. Here, we provide one missing piece by identifying the structure that relates distance measure and curvature for general (mixed or pure) quantum states. This structure is shown to carry the physical meaning of fluctuation-dissipation---by directly relating distance measure with fluctuations and curvature with linear response---and it turns into the corresponding well-known structure for pure states, where it is Kähler, and only then. We thus obtain a general, simple fluctuation-dissipation picture, which, among its consequences, implies that response functions inherently probe the mixed-state quantum geometric tensor. We end by using this picture to give geometric characterizations of generic transport behaviors.

</details>


### [119] [Superextensive learning in quantum reservoirs at the onset of information scrambling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25511)
*Jonas Freiheit,Francesco Campaioli*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究通过模拟二维Ising自旋网络作为量子储层进行时间序列预测，发现信息处理性能在信息开始散列（scrambling）时达到峰值，且储层规模越大，性能提升越显著，揭示了信息散列在量子储层计算中的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子多体系统中信息处理是否在有序与混沌边界处最优，验证信息散列对学习系统性能的影响。

Method: 数值模拟最多N=20个自旋的二维Ising网络，将其作为量子储层用于时间序列预测，并利用OTOC定位信息散列的 onset；分析不同输入强度下预测精度、基态占据数、记忆容量等指标的变化。

Result: 预测精度在信息开始散列时达到峰值；储层大小N增加时，精度呈超广延幂律增长（α>1），而在其他区域仅为次线性增长（α<1）；散列表现出增强非线性记忆但降低线性容量的特点；总记忆容量在具备集体弛豫机制时超广延增长。

Conclusion: 信息散列的 onset 是量子储层计算性能最优的工作点，不仅是一个操作状态，更支持一种可扩展的性能提升规律，强化了信息散列在学习系统中的核心地位。

Abstract: The idea that information processing is optimised near the boundary between order and chaos has emerged as a recurring principle across neuroscience, complex systems, and machine learning. Here we test this hypothesis in quantum many-body systems, numerically simulating two-dimensional Ising networks of up to $N=20$ spins, operated as quantum reservoirs for time-series forecasting. Using out-of-time-order correlators (OTOCs), we locate the onset of information scrambling as the input strength is swept, separating regimes where information is frozen and scrambled across the whole reservoir state. We show that prediction precision peaks at the onset of scrambling, along with the number of computational-basis states that the reservoir actively populates. We then show that prediction precision grows as a power law $\sim N^α$ in the reservoir size, superextensively $(α>1)$ at the onset of scrambling and only sublinearly $(α<1)$ in either neighbouring regime. Finally, we show that scrambling enhances the nonlinear components of the reservoir memory while reducing its linear capacity. At the onset, the total memory capacity grows superextensively, provided that the necessary ``forgetting'' mechanism is supplied by a collective relaxation channel. These results consolidate the role of information scrambling in learning systems, turning it from an operating point into a scaling law for the performance of quantum reservoirs.

</details>


### [120] [The bottleneck dimension of quantum operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25010)
*Pavel Sekatski*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种操作性的、与基无关的框架，用于衡量含噪声量子设备的有效相干量子维度，并定义了三种不等价的压缩性概念（$d$-compressibility, $d$-simulability, $d$-embeddability），形成严格层级关系，统一了多个已有概念，并通过噪声量子操作示例验证了随着标称维度增加，维持全希尔伯特空间相干性的难度增大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于噪声的存在，当前可编程量子设备难以在整个指数级增长的希尔伯特空间中保持相干性，因此需要一个操作性强且与基选择无关的框架来量化其有效相干维度。

Method: 引入基于信息压缩能力的操作性框架，通过限制量子变换中的信息流，定义三种不同因果结构下的维度瓶颈概念，并分析其层级关系。

Result: 识别出$d$-压缩性、$d$-可模拟性和$d$-可嵌入性三种不等价概念并建立严格层次；恢复了联合可测性、测量 simulability 和态集合绝对维数等已有概念作为特例；计算了在互补基下含噪qubit测量以及高维含噪通用量子处理器的白噪声阈值。

Conclusion: 随着标称维度$n$的增加，保持整个希尔伯特空间的相干性变得愈发困难，该框架为评估现实量子设备的有效量子资源提供了系统工具。

Abstract: Programmable quantum devices nominally act on a Hilbert space whose dimension grows exponentially with the number of constituents, but the presence of noise makes it unlikely that they remain coherent across all of this immense Hilbert space. Then, what is the effective coherent quantum dimension that should be associated with such imperfect devices? To answer this question we here introduce an operational basis-independent framework which imposes a dimension bottleneck on the programmable transformations. Concretely we ask how strongly the quantum information they process can be compressed. Formalizing this idea we identify three inequivalent notions, termed $d$-compressibility, $d$-simulability and $d$-embeddability, which differ in the causal structure used to impose the bottleneck and form a strict hierarchy. The framework unifies several existing notions: joint measurability and simulability of quantum measurements, and the absolute dimensionality of state ensembles, are recovered as special cases. We illustrate the hierarchy with noisy qubit measurements in complementary bases, and we determine the white-noise thresholds at which the set of all noisy unitary channels in dimension $n$, a noisy universal quantum processor, becomes $d$-compressible, $d$-simulable and $d$-embeddable. The thresholds confirm the expectation -- maintaining coherence across the full Hilbert space becomes increasingly demanding as the nominal dimension $n$ increases.

</details>


### [121] [Hardware-Aware Fermion-to-Qubit Mappings for Simulating the 2D Hubbard Model on Heavy-Hexagon Quantum Processors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25024)
*Alessio Esposito,Andrea Giachero,Zoltàn Zimboràs,Leonardo Banchi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本研究针对二维Hubbard模型在IBM超导量子处理器上的量子模拟，比较了多种费米子-量子比特映射方法，发现Bonsai映射在考虑路由开销后最具硬件效率，并通过模拟退火优化Majorana字符串分配，显著降低代价函数，成功实现了高达6x6晶格的硬件友好的大规模二维模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提升强关联费米系统在近期量子硬件上模拟的实用性，需解决费米子映射和硬件连通性带来的开销问题。

Method: 在重六边形连接结构的IBM量子处理器上，数值评估Jordan-Wigner、Bravyi-Kitaev和Bonsai变换的Pauli权重与SWAP开销；采用Bonsai映射构建2D Hubbard模型的量子比特哈密顿量，并引入模拟退火算法优化Majorana字符串到晶格点的分配。

Result: Bravyi-Kitaev虽初始Pauli权重较低，但考虑路由后优势消失；Bonsai映射成为最高效的基准方案；模拟退火使成本函数降低近50%；在真实量子硬件上实现了最大6x6晶格的费米态时间演化模拟。

Conclusion: Bonsai映射结合优化的Majorana分配策略，是面向重六边形架构进行大规模二维费米系统量子模拟的最优选择。

Abstract: Quantum simulation of strongly correlated fermionic systems is among the most promising near- term applications of quantum computing, but its practical efficiency depends critically on the choice of fermion-to-qubit mapping and on the connectivity of the underlying hardware. In this work we address this problem in the context of the two-dimensional Hubbard model, simulated on IBM superconducting quantum processors with heavy-hexagon connectivity. We numerically benchmark the Jordan-Wigner, Bravyi-Kitaev, and Bonsai transformations, evaluating their Pauli weight and SWAP overhead across seven heavy-hexagon chips of increasing size. We show that, while the Bravyi-Kitaev mapping initially exhibits a lower Pauli weight, this advantage is eliminated once routing costs are taken into account, confirming the Bonsai mapping as the most hardware-efficient baseline transformation for this architecture. We then use the Bonsai mapping to construct the qubit Hamiltonian of the 2D spinful Fermi-Hubbard model, introducing a simulated annealing algorithm that optimizes the assignment of Majorana strings to lattice sites, reducing the cost function by nearly 50% percent. Finally, we simulate on quantum hardware the time evolution of fermionic states up to 6x6 lattices, confirming the viability of the Bonsai encoding for hardware-aware large-scale two-dimensional simulations.

</details>


### [122] [Real-time decoder for a MegaQuOp quantum computer using a single CPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25027)
*Min Ye,Andrii Maksymov,Nicolas Delfosse*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文展示了一个适用于大规模容错离子阱量子计算机的端到端实时解码系统，可在单个CPU上实现实时错误校正，支持包含数百万逻辑门和数百个逻辑量子比特的实际工作负载。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着量子计算机向执行数百万门操作的兆级量子操作（MegaQuOp）时代发展，实时纠错解码系统成为实现容错计算的关键需求。现有研究多集中于小规模逻辑操作或存储器纠错，缺乏对大规模通用量子计算工作负载的支持。

Method: 提出并实现了一个完整的实时解码栈，包括动态生成探测器误差模型、全逻辑量子比特解码、逻辑操作处理和魔法态工厂管理，并在单个CPU上运行整个流水线。

Result: 系统在多达408个逻辑量子比特和最多一百万个T门的工作负载上进行了基准测试，在CNOT错误率为10^{-4}至5×10^{-4}、周期时间为1-5毫秒的离子阱架构假设下，解码延迟仅使计算延长不到0.3%到12%。

Conclusion: 该解码系统能够在单个传统CPU上实现兆级量子操作规模的实时解码，展示了迈向实用化大规模容错量子计算的重要进展。

Abstract: As quantum computers advance toward the regime of MegaQuOp machines executing millions of gates, a decoding system capable of real-time error correction in such a device will be crucial. Recent efforts have been focused on decoding an error-corrected memory or a small number of logical operations. Here we demonstrate an end-to-end real-time decoding stack for a universal fault-tolerant trapped-ion quantum computer architecture capable of decoding real workloads with millions of logical gates over hundreds of logical qubits. The complete pipeline, including on the fly detector error model generation, decoding of all logical qubits, logical operations, and magic-state factories, runs on a single CPU. We benchmark the decoder on practically relevant quantum applications spanning up to 408 logical qubits, and up to one million $T$ gates. Assuming a trapped-ion architecture with 1 to 5 ms cycle time, the decoding delay stretches the computation by less than $0.3\%$ at $p_{\mathrm{CNOT}}=10^{-4}$ and less than $12\%$ at $p_{\mathrm{CNOT}}=5\times 10^{-4}$ for all workloads studied. These results demonstrate real-time decoding at MegaQuOp scale on a single conventional CPU.

</details>


### [123] [A Unified Framework for Operator Backpropagation and Observable Measurement in Quantum Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25042)
*Kevin Dougherty,Ji Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种综合框架，通过优化基于算子反向传播生成的测量可观测量的测量协议，减少量子电路深度与测量误差，结合QWC分组和截断策略以提高精度并降低测量次数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子电路受退相干影响严重限制了电路深度，需要降低深度的方法；算子反向传播虽能降低深度但增加了需测量的可观测量数量，且引入多种累积误差，缺乏与先进测量协议结合的研究。

Method: 提出一个综合框架，基于反向传播产生的可观测量的QWC（qubit-wise commuting）分组数量来确定最优测量协议；分析并分类反向传播过程中的主要误差来源（如算法误差、截断误差、shot noise），研究截断策略以减少可观测量数量，并刻画其结构特征。

Result: 建立了根据QWC分组选择测量协议的准则，量化了不同误差源的影响，验证了截断策略在减少可观测量方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 将算子反向传播与高效测量协议结合是可行且必要的，所提框架可实现高精度下最少测量次数，为深度压缩与误差控制提供了联合优化路径。

Abstract: Decoherence imposes severe limits on quantum circuit depth, motivating methods to reduce circuit depth. Operator backpropagation has recently been proposed as a way to lower circuit depth by classically backpropagating observables through subcircuits. While it lowers the circuit depth, it also significantly increases the number of backpropagated observables that must be measured. This calls for an efficient measurement protocol tailored to these backpropagated observables. Moreover, errors arise both during the backpropagation procedure and during measurement, and they accumulate in the final computation. No prior work has examined how to integrate operator backpropagation with advanced measurement protocols, and how to jointly optimize the combined process.
  In this paper, we introduce a comprehensive framework to determine the appropriate measurement protocol to achieve a desired level of accuracy with the fewest number of measurements based on the number of qubit-wise commuting (QWC) groups formed from operator backpropagation. We analyze and categorize the primary sources of error that arise through our workflow, including error incurred from the backpropagation algorithm, truncation error, and shot noise variation. We also examine truncation strategies to reduce the number of backpropagated observables by characterizing the structure of the set of backpropagated observables.

</details>


### [124] [Feedback-enabled magnomechanics in lithium ferrite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25101)
*Mehri Ebrahimi,Yunhu Huang,Ali Rashedi,John P. Davis*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 首次在单晶锂铁氧体中观测到腔磁声学相互作用，利用相干微波反馈抑制耗散，显著增强磁声耦合效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索锂铁氧体在腔磁声学中的潜力，克服传统测量中因耗散导致的弱信号难以探测的问题。

Method: 使用相干微波反馈技术降低腔-磁子极化激元的线宽，从而提升磁声合作性，并观察磁声诱导透明现象。

Result: 反馈使有效上极化激元线宽从3.53 MHz降至5.8 kHz，合作性从1.9×10⁻³提升至0.15，清晰观测到磁声诱导透明。

Conclusion: 实现了锂铁氧体中的腔磁声学调控，验证了相干反馈作为研究弱相互作用的有效手段。

Abstract: Lithium ferrite (LiFe) is a promising material for cavity magnonics because its large spin density enables strong coupling between magnons and microwave photons. Its potential for cavity magnomechanics, however, has remained unexplored. Here, we observe magnomechanical interactions in a single-crystal LiFe sphere using coherent microwave feedback to suppress dissipation of the cavity--magnon polariton. In the absence of sufficient feedback, the narrow mechanical response is difficult to resolve against the much broader polariton background. Increasing the feedback gain reduces the polariton linewidth and correspondingly increases the magnomechanical cooperativity, revealing a clear magnomechanically induced transparency feature. In the measurements presented here, the effective upper-polariton linewidth is reduced from $3.53~\mathrm{MHz}$ without feedback to $5.8~\mathrm{kHz}$ in the presence of feedback, while the measured cooperativity increases from $C=1.9\times10^{-3}$ to $C=0.15$. These measurements provide, to our knowledge, the first observation of cavity magnomechanics in LiFe and demonstrate coherent feedback as a practical route for accessing weak interactions that would otherwise be obscured by dissipation.

</details>


### [125] [Generalized Efficient Quantum Circuit Implementation of Discrete-Time Quantum Walks on Cayley Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25136)
*Seoyoon Kang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种高效的量子电路框架，用于在任意维度的Cayley图上实现离散时间量子行走，通过系统性分解移位算符，显著降低了门复杂度，尤其在生成集度数较小时具有明显优势，且效率增益对系统规模不敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在近期量子设备上高效实现离散时间量子行走，需要降低量子电路中移位算符的门复杂度，尤其是CNOT门的数量，以提升可扩展性和硬件兼容性。

Method: 基于Razzoli等人提出的边界QFT方案，引入了针对1D Cayley图三类生成集的多阶段系统性分解方法，并推广到d维环面图，将QFT对角化的移位算子分解为结构化块组件，用低控制度旋转门替代高控制度操作。

Result: 给出了8-Cayley图和Z16×Z8环面图的具体电路实现，CNOT复杂度分析显示，在k≤64（逆闭）和k≤16（非逆闭）时，分解后的实现相比朴素实现显著减少CNOT门数量，且效率增益几乎不受系统大小N影响。

Conclusion: 该框架为在近期量子硬件上可扩展、高效地实现DTQW提供了可行路径，揭示生成集的度数k是决定资源开销的关键参数。

Abstract: We present a generalized and efficient quantum circuit framework for implementing discrete-time quantum walks (DTQWs) on Cayley graphs of arbitrary dimension. Building on the Boundary QFT scheme of Razzoli et al., we introduce a systematic multi-stage decomposition of the shift operator for 1D Cayley graphs across three classes of generating sets: inverse-closed without involutions, inverse-closed with an involution, and non-inverse-closed. The decomposition hierarchically factorizes the QFT-diagonalized shift operator into structured block components, progressively reducing the control degree of the required rotation gates and replacing high-degree multi-qubit controlled operations with collections of lower-degree equivalents. We extend this construction to $d$-dimensional torus graphs and provide explicit circuit implementations for an 8-Cayley graph and a $\mathbb{Z}_{16} \times \mathbb{Z}_8$ torus graph as concrete illustrations. Gate complexity analysis using the linear CNOT scaling of Rosa et al. demonstrates that the decomposed implementation achieves a substantial reduction in upper-bound CNOT cost relative to the naive implementation within the regime $k \leq 64$ for inverse-closed graphs and $k \leq 16$ for non-inverse-closed graphs, where $k$ denotes the degree of the generating set. Benchmarking further reveals that this efficiency gain is largely insensitive to the system size $N$, identifying $k$ as the dominant resource parameter for the shift operator. These results provide a scalable and hardware-conscious pathway toward practical DTQW implementations on near-term quantum devices.

</details>


### [126] [No Free Compression in Quantum Relaxations for Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25151)
*Stuart Hadfield*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在量子优化中，通过将经典决策变量压缩到更少的量子比特上所带来的资源权衡。重点分析了完全二次Majorana编码下的关联子表示能力及其限制，指出尽管这种压缩能显著减少所需量子比特数，但会带来期望值几何受限、期望值幅度减小或信息恢复难度增加等代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子松弛方法在压缩经典变量时的资源效率与局限性，理解qubit压缩对量子优化性能的实际影响。

Method: 利用完全二次Majorana编码分析n个量子比特如何表示m=Θ(n²)个二进制变量，引入“通用边界”概念，并结合费米高斯态与任意密度算符的协方差体比较，以及量子随机访问码的信息论下界进行分析。

Result: 证明了Majorana编码的通用边界为Δ_Maj(n)=tan(π/(4n))=Θ(1/n)，而随机符号分配的目标特定边界为Θ(1/√n)；任意非高斯态无法扩展该两点松弛范围；并给出了任意固定可观测量族的通用边界上限及信息恢复所需的拷贝数下限。

Conclusion: 虽然qubit压缩可大幅节省量子资源，但它并未消除成本，而是将其转移至更小的期望值幅度、受限的相关结构或更高的信息恢复要求上，揭示了量子压缩方法的根本性权衡。

Abstract: Qubit-efficient quantum relaxations compress classical decision variables into expectation values on substantially fewer qubits. We ask what resource tradeoffs this compression entails for quantum optimization. For the complete quadratic-Majorana encoding on $n$ qubits, pairwise correlators can represent $m=Θ(n^2)$ binary variables. We define the universal margin as the smallest correlator magnitude that can be guaranteed with prescribed signs for every target sign assignment. We show that it is exactly $Δ_{\rm Maj}(n)=\tan\!\left(\fracπ{4n}\right)=Θ(1/n)$, whereas uniformly random sign assignments retain $Θ(1/\sqrt n)$ target-specific margins. The stronger $1/n$ worst-case scaling is Majorana-specific. Moreover, arbitrary density operators and fermionic Gaussian states generate the same quadratic-Majorana covariance body, so non-Gaussian state resources cannot enlarge this two-point relaxation. Beyond Majoranas, standard quantum random access code bounds provide general information-theoretic baselines. For any fixed family of $m$ designated binary observables on $n$ qubits, the universal margin is at most $\sqrt{(2\ln2\;n/m)}$, while arbitrary random access decoding from $N$ copies with constant success probability above $1/2$ requires $nN=Ω(m)$. For a fixed Pauli correlation encoding required to work uniformly over all targets, maintaining a fixed nonzero decoded magnitude under smooth sign decoding therefore requires a rescaling parameter that grows as the available margin shrinks. Thus, while providing substantial qubit savings, compression can shift cost into restricted expectation value geometry, smaller expectation value magnitudes, or more demanding information recovery rather than eliminate it.

</details>


### [127] [Optical centrifuge as a probe of strong dissipative coupling between a molecular rotor and superfluid helium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25161)
*Ian MacPhail-Bartley,Sören E. Mahr,Cameron E. Peters,Baptiste Coquinot,Volker Karle,Giacomo Bighin,Ragheed Alhyder,Mikhail Lemeshko,Henrik Stapelfeldt,Valery Milner*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 通过使用超慢光学离心机连续驱动，使分子进入摆动态，从而突破溶剂化层限制，实现分子与超流体之间的强耗散耦合，并直接测量量子流体中的分子-浴耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在纳米尺度下超流体中摩擦力消失现象的破坏机制，特别是在强耗散耦合 regime 下的能量传递主导动力学过程的问题。

Method: 利用短激光脉冲诱导分子旋转，并采用超慢光学离心机进行连续驱动，使分子进入摆动态，进而探测其与超流体之间的能量交换和热化过程。

Result: 实现了分子旋转能量进入超流体集体激发谱范围，观察到快速热化导致分子锁定于旋转场，直至旋转引起的能级分裂超过热化速率后分子取向逐渐丧失。

Conclusion: 该方法成功进入了强耗散耦合 regime，为在量子流体中直接测量分子-浴耦合提供了新途径。

Abstract: A macroscopic manifestation of superfluidity is that objects moving through liquid helium experience negligible friction below the Landau critical velocity. How this frictionless motion breaks down at the nanoscale remains an open question. Molecules embedded in helium nanodroplets represent a well-controlled system for studying this breakdown, yet none has reached the regime of strong dissipative coupling, when energy transfer from the molecule to the superfluid dominates the observed dynamics. Molecular rotation, induced by short laser pulses, offer a suitable probe to reach rotational energies in the range of the roton gap, where superfluid helium supports a large number of elementary excitations. However, the solvation shell around a rotating molecule caps the energy reachable by a free rotor after impulsive excitation well below the roton excitation energy. Here we show that continuous driving with an ultraslow optical centrifuge overcomes this limitation: the strong field dresses the molecule into pendular states whose energies fall within the spectrum of the collective excitations of the superfluid, placing the system in the strong-dissipation regime. The resulting rapid thermalization locks the molecule to the rotating field until the rotation-induced level splittings overtake the thermalization rate, beyond which the molecular alignment is progressively lost. Our approach offers a direct measurement of the molecule-bath coupling in a quantum fluid.

</details>


### [128] [Separating Quantum Indistinguishability Obfuscation from Falsifiable Assumptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25195)
*Mohammed Barhoush,Tomoyuki Morimae,Ramis Movassagh*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了通过一类受限的量子黑盒规约，基于任何可证伪密码假设来构建QMA问题的密钥加密（WE）是不可行的。由于qIO蕴含WE，这一结果也表明null-qIO无法从大多数标准密码假设出发构造，揭示了构建量子不可区分混淆的障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子不可区分混淆（qIO）的安全性基础，特别是其是否能基于标准密码假设构造，是当前量子密码学的重要开放问题。由于qIO在诸多高级应用中的核心地位，理解其理论极限至关重要。

Method: 通过分析一类受限的量子黑盒规约（要求规约以经典、非自适应方式查询敌手，且仅使用相同安全参数下的合法密文），结合QMA-QCIP[2]间隙问题的存在性（一种平均情况下的复杂性假设），证明WE for QMA不能基于可证伪假设通过此类规约实现。

Result: 证明了对于具有经典密文的WE for QMA，其安全性不能基于任何可证伪假设通过所限定类别的量子黑盒规约来实现；因此，null-qIO也无法通过此类方式从可证伪假设导出。该结果对当前主流的标准密码假设构成理论障碍。

Conclusion: 构建量子不可区分混淆面临根本性挑战：至少在当前主流的规约方法和经典密文设定下，无法将其安全性归约到大多数标准（即可证伪）密码假设上，提示未来需探索更广义的规约方式或量子化输出的新型构造。

Abstract: Quantum indistinguishability obfuscation (qIO) aims to make a quantum circuit unintelligible while preserving its functionality. It serves as a foundational primitive for advanced applications, such as witness encryption (WE) for QMA, non-interactive zero-knowledge arguments for QMA, and attribute-based encryption for BQP. Despite its importance, constructing qIO from standard assumptions remains a major open problem.
  In this work, we prove that the security of WE for QMA cannot be based on any falsifiable cryptographic assumption via a restricted class of quantum black-box reductions. Because qIO for null quantum circuits implies WE for QMA, this also separates null-qIO from falsifiable assumptions. Since almost all standard cryptographic assumptions are falsifiable, our result presents a barrier to basing qIO on standard cryptographic assumptions.
  The reductions we rule out are restricted: the reduction must query the adversary classically, non-adaptively, at the same security parameter, and only on honestly generated ciphertexts. Moreover, our impossibility applies only to WE with classical ciphertexts, and therefore does not rule out qIO with obfuscators whose output is a quantum state. Ruling out more general reductions, as well as more general forms of WE and qIO, remains open.
  Our impossibility relies on the existence of a QMA-QCIP[2] gap problem, an average-case assumption postulating a QMA language that cannot be verified with two messages of classical communication.

</details>


### [129] [Multipair-resilient entanglement swapping with complementary linear-optical measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25211)
*Siavash Mirzaei Ghormish,Ryan M. Camacho*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种级联网络结构，通过结合不同类型的测量（如直接基BSM和四模Green Machine分析器），有效区分并抑制自发参量下转换源在纠缠交换中的多光子噪声，从而提升贝尔态制备的保真度与分发效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统贝尔态测量无法区分单光子对与同一光源产生的双光子发射，导致纠缠交换中出现虚假信号，影响量子通信的可靠性。因此需要一种能够消除此类干扰的新型测量方案。

Method: 设计级联网络，在不同节点采用不同测量方式：直接基BSM用于抑制同偏振双光子发射，四模Green Machine利用双光子干涉抑制混合偏振双光子发射，并回收被BSM误判的有用事件；使用全阶高斯态分析评估多源网络在损耗环境下的性能。

Result: 三源网络可抑制主导失真项，四源及以上交替结构在无损情况下可实现精确贝尔态制备；相比全BSM网络，新方案在所有损耗水平下均具有更高的保真度，且无需复用即具备单通道速率优势，结合谱复用可逼近确定性贝尔对分发极限。

Conclusion: 交替结构的BSM--GM级联网络能完全覆盖单源双光子噪声类型，同时保留有用的跨源事件，显著提升纠缠交换的性能，是构建高效、高保真量子中继的理想方案。

Abstract: Multipair emission is a principal source of false heralds in entanglement swapping with spontaneous parametric down-conversion sources. A conventional Bell-state measurement (BSM) cannot distinguish the desired arrival of one photon from each neighboring source from a mixed-polarization double emission by one source accompanied by vacuum from the other. We show that a cascaded network can resolve this ambiguity by allowing its successive swapping stations to perform different measurements. A direct-basis BSM rejects same-polarization double emissions, whereas a balanced equatorial analyzer, implemented by a four-mode Green Machine (GM), uses two-photon interference to reject mixed-polarization double emissions. At the same time, it recovers same-polarization inter-source events rejected by the BSM as resolved $φ$-type Bell heralds. Their rejection sets jointly cover both single-source two-photon classes while retaining contributions from both useful inter-source classes. We classify the passive four-mode, number-resolving analyzers satisfying this condition and identify the BSM and GM as canonical balanced representatives. Using a Gaussian-state analysis exact to all orders of multipair emission, we evaluate three-source BSM--GM and four-source BSM--GM--BSM Pure Bell Pair sources under coupling, detector, and channel loss. The three-source network suppresses the leading infidelity, while the four-source network heralds an exact Bell state in the lossless limit, a property that holds for every alternating BSM--GM chain with four or more sources. The fidelity advantage over source-count-matched all-BSM networks persists at every loss level studied, a single-channel rate advantage appears without multiplexing, and spectral multiplexing raises the Bell-pair delivery probability toward the asymptotically deterministic limit.

</details>


### [130] [Affine-Profile Stabilizer Thresholds for Magic in Codeword-Stabilized Quantum Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25414)
*Li-Yi Hsu,En-Jui Kuo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种适用于码字稳定化（CWS）量子码的魔法见证框架，将量子态的魔性鲁棒性下界转化为经典的仿射关联问题，实现了从量子优化到经典几何问题的精确转化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了统一描述稳定子与非加性量子纠错码中的魔性，并量化由CWS码字相干叠加所产生的非稳定子特性所带来的魔法鲁棒性。

Method: 发展了一种适配于CWS码的魔法见证框架，利用有限几何方法分析码字集合上的仿射交集与二次相位结构，在等权和加权叠加情形下分别将稳定子阈值问题转化为经典组合几何问题。

Result: 得到了稳定子阈值与经典CWS码字集上仿射子空间包含关系之间的精确等价性；对等权重叠加情形，阈值完全由各维仿射平坦中可容纳的CWS词数决定；给出了固定参数算法、无穷并稳定子族的解析下界及多个非加性CWS码的精确有理证书。

Conclusion: 该框架揭示了非线性CWS码字集通过仿射交集结构生成可验证魔性的几何机制，为理解非稳定子态的资源性提供了新的经典组合视角。

Abstract: Codeword-stabilized quantum codes give a unified graph-state description of stabilizer and nonadditive quantum error-correcting codes. Although each individual CWS word state is stabilizer, coherent superpositions of different word states can be nonstabilizer. We develop a CWS-adapted magic-witness framework that isolates this codeword coherence and converts it into certified lower bounds on robustness of magic.
  The main result is an exact reduction of the stabilizer threshold of a natural CWS coherence witness to a finite-geometric problem over the classical CWS word set. For general weighted superpositions, the threshold is computed by enumerating affine intersections and affine-quadratic phases. For equal-weight superpositions, the phase optimization collapses, and the threshold is determined entirely by how many CWS words can lie in affine flats of each dimension. Thus a quantum optimization over stabilizer states becomes a classical affine-incidence problem.
  This reduction yields a fixed-parameter algorithm, an analytic lower bound for an infinite union-stabilizer family, and exact rational certificates for several standard nonadditive CWS examples. The framework also clarifies why exact enumeration fails for large structured families and identifies the remaining task as an affine-intersection problem. The result provides a geometric mechanism by which nonlinear CWS word sets generate certifiable magic.

</details>


### [131] [Quantum skyrmion parallelism via metasurface-tailored high-dimensional entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25415)
*Pedro Ornelas,Ramona Bedford,Fazilah Nothlawala,Chi Li,Haoyi Yu,Isaac Nape,Stefan A. Maier,Haoran Ren,Andrew Forbes*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究通过将高维光子纠缠与超表面结合，引入非局域相互作用，实现了多维度拓扑量子态的生成。利用轨道角动量投影调控纠缠光子，单个超表面器件可产生多种不同拓扑类别的非局域偏振-轨道角动量纠缠态，揭示了复杂量子态紧凑生成的新路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子skyrmion生成方法局限于二维、单一拓扑结构，缺乏灵活性和扩展性，亟需实现多维、可调控的拓扑量子态以拓展其在量子信息中的应用。

Method: 通过将高维光子纠缠态与具有结构化光学响应的超表面耦合，利用纠缠态的概率特性使超表面的拓扑变换具有非确定性，从而实现多拓扑共存的量子态；通过测量和轨道角动量（OAM）投影选择特定输出拓扑。

Result: 成功生成了多个共存拓扑结构的量子态，并可在不同空间模式通道中并行传输；实验验证了修改后的希尔伯特空间中丰富的拓扑图景，实现了由单一超表面产生多个不同拓扑类别的非局部偏振-OAM纠缠态。

Conclusion: 高维结构化纠缠与结构化物质的耦合为复杂量子态的紧凑生成提供了新范式，拓展了量子拓扑结构的可编程性和应用潜力。

Abstract: Quantum skyrmions are topological structures that have garnered significant interest due to their demonstrated robustness and versatility across diverse optical platforms. However, existing approaches for their generation are limited to producing pre-determined two dimensional qubit states with a single topology. Here we create multi-dimensional topological states by introducing a non-local interaction between high-dimensional photonic entanglement and a metasurface, where the topological transformation induced by the metasurface is made non-deterministic by the probabilistic nature of the interfacing entangled state. Within this framework, we demonstrate that individual quantum states can host multiple co-existing topologies that are only revealed upon measurement, allowing for their parallel transport within distinct spatial mode channels. We confirm this by revealing the rich topological landscape within our modified Hilbert space while controlling the desired output topology by orbital angular momentum (OAM) projections on one of the entangled photons, producing multiple non-local polarization-OAM entangled states characterized by distinct topological classes, all from a single metasurface device. Our results reveal new capability when structured high-dimensional entanglement is interfaced with structured matter capable of coupling photonic degrees of freedom, establishing a new pathway for the compact generation of complex quantum states.

</details>


### [132] [Magic of Kitaev spin liquids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25929)
*Eleonora Lamma,Tim Bauer,Marcello Dalmonte,Mario Collura*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究了量子自旋液体（QSLs）中的“魔法”（非稳定子性）作为多体复杂性的诊断工具，利用Kitaev蜂窝模型计算稳定子Rényi熵（SRE），发现魔法在无能隙相中最大，并在拓扑相变时表现出由临界指数决定的普适标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子关联如何产生量子复杂性，并将‘魔法’作为超越纠缠的多体复杂性诊断工具。

Method: 通过建立泡利字符串与流动马约拉纳算符乘积之间的对应关系，使用针对高斯态的优化算法采样SRE。

Result: 无能隙相中魔法最大，具有非局域贡献；有能隙相中SRE下降并与各向异性极限下的微扰展开定量一致；在拓扑相变点，SRE表现出由临界指数决定的普适标度。

Conclusion: SRE可作为探测量子自旋液体中非局域量子复杂性的有效工具，揭示了不同相中魔法的行为及其与拓扑相变的关系。

Abstract: Quantum spin liquids (QSLs) are long-range entangled phases of matter and a natural setting for exploring how quantum correlations generate quantum complexity. Motivated by the emergence of magic, or nonstabilizerness, as a diagnostic of many-body complexity beyond entanglement, we study magic of QSLs by computing the stabilizer Rényi entropy (SRE) of the Kitaev honeycomb model. We derive a correspondence between Pauli strings and products of itinerant Majorana operators in the model's free-fermion description; this enables the sampling of SRE using an optimized algorithm for Gaussian states in systems with thousands of spins. Our results show that magic is largest in the gapless phase, where subleading volume-law corrections indicate nonlocal contributions, while in the gapped phases, SRE decreases in quantitative agreement with a perturbative expansion that we develop in the anisotropic limit. At the topological phase transition, SRE exhibits universal scaling dictated by critical exponents.

</details>


### [133] [Computing key rates for one-sided device-independent quantum key distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25915)
*Andreas Bluhm,Gereon Koßmann,Martin Sandfuchs,René Schwonnek,Giuseppe Viola,Ramona Wolf*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于单边设备无关量子密钥分发（QKD）的新工具和方法，通过扩展NPA层次结构并结合有限尺寸安全性分析，评估了多种协议的密钥生成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在完全设备依赖与全设备无关QKD之间，单边设备无关QKD提供了一个折中方案，但缺乏有效的密钥率计算工具，因此需要发展新的理论方法以支持其实用化。

Method: 开发并比较了两种NPA层次结构的扩展形式，利用广义熵累积定理推导出针对一般攻击和任意设备记忆的有限尺寸安全边界，并应用于多种QKD协议的性能分析。

Result: 成功计算了BB84（有无损耗）、qutrit MUB、CHSH和I3322等协议的密钥率；发现被刻画方的选择显著影响密钥率，且不存在普适的优劣排序。

Conclusion: 本工作为单边设备无关QKD协议提供了通用的密钥率计算工具箱，增强了该类协议在现实条件下的安全性和实用性评估能力。

Abstract: The defining feature of one-sided device-independent quantum key distribution is its asymmetric trust model in which only one party is characterized. This scenario sets an interesting middle ground between high key rates achievable by characterizing devices and the security of full device-independence. Here, we provide new tools, methods, and benchmarks for calculating key rates in this setting. To achieve this, we develop and compare two extensions of the NPA hierarchy and derive finite-size security bounds against general attacks with arbitrary device memory. The latter is based on the Generalized Entropy Accumulation Theorem. We then investigate the performance of various protocols: the BB84 protocol both with and without losses, a qutrit mutually unbiased bases protocol, and protocols based on Bell inequalties such as CHSH and $I_{3322}$. We find that the choice of which party is characterized can strongly affects the key rate, surprisingly without a universal ordering. Our work thus offers a general toolbox for calculating key rates for one-sided device-independent QKD protocols.

</details>


### [134] [The temperature-dependent non-Abelian gauge potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25437)
*Zheng-Chuan Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该研究发现时间演化算符可作为U(1)规范变换作用于初始波函数，在考虑自旋自由度时对应非阿贝尔SU(2)或SU(3)规范变换；在铁磁系统中引入绝热近似后，会出现温度依赖的非阿贝尔热规范势，并给出了半经典极限下温度依赖Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert方程的线性解析解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索时间演化算符在不同自旋系统中的规范对称性表现，并研究在自旋极化电流驱动下铁磁系统磁化动力学中的非阿贝尔规范势效应。

Method: 通过理论分析时间演化算符的作用，结合自旋自由度与绝热近似方法，推导出温度依赖的非阿贝尔热规范势，并建立相应的温度依赖Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert方程，给出其线性近似解析解。

Result: 揭示了时间演化算符具有U(1)、SU(2)和SU(3)规范变换特性；发现了温度依赖的非阿贝尔热规范势；得到了描述磁化动力学的温度依赖LLG方程的解析解。

Conclusion: 时间演化算符可体现为不同自旋系统的规范变换，在绝热近似下铁磁系统的磁化动力学会产生温度依赖的非阿贝尔规范势，可用推广的LLG方程描述。

Abstract: This study shows that the usual time evolution operator can act as a U(1) gauge transformation on the initial wavefuntion. When considering the spin freedom in the system, the time evolution operator corresponds to a non-Abelian SU(2) gauge transformation for spin-1/2 particles and a SU(3) gauge transformation for spin-1 particles. If we adopt the adiabatic approximation in the magnetization dynamics of a ferromagnetic system driven by a spin-polarized current, a temperature-dependent non-Abelian thermal gauge potential will appear. We can employ a temperature-dependent Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert (LLG) equation to describe the magnetization dynamics in the semi-classical limit, a linear approximated solution for this equation is given analytically.

</details>


### [135] [Yang-Lee Criticality as a Dissipative Dynamical Phase Transition: Quantum Simulation of non-Hermitian Physics without Post-selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26082)
*Stephen W. Yan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种在开放量子系统中无后选择实现Yang-Lee理论的方法，通过局域幺正和耗散动力学的競爭实现阻尼相变，并可用于测量自旋关联函数和Loschmidt回波，具有在近期量子模拟器上实现的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典Ising模型在虚磁场下的Yang-Lee理论映射到开放量子系统中，避免后选择，以实现在量子平台上直接研究非厄米统计力学。

Method: 利用具有局域幺正演化和工程耗散的(d-1)+1维开放量子系统，借助Lindbladian动力学中的弱对称性，精确实现Yang-Lee转移矩阵的非厄米生成元，并通过修改动力学局部测量相关物理量。

Result: 实现了Yang-Lee理论在开放量子系统中的有效嵌入，观测到线性观测量从欠阻尼到过阻尼的动力学相变，可直接测量自旋关联函数和Loschmidt回波；并推广至任意非厄米哈密顿量的嵌入。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了无后选择下非厄米统计力学与开放量子动力学之间的普适联系，为在量子模拟器上研究异常点物理和非幺正现象提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We show that the $d+0$-dimensional Yang-Lee theory describing classical Ising spins in an imaginary magnetic field can be realized, without post-selection, within a $(d-1)+1$ open quantum system whose dynamics consist of local unitaries and engineered dissipation. Competition between the coherent unitary and dissipative dynamics drives a transition wherein the time-dependence of a particular class of linear observables changes from damped oscillatory "underdamped" to purely exponential "overdamped" decay. Our construction relies on an extensive number of weak-symmetries of the Lindbladian fixed by the choice of observable but is otherwise exact. Consequently, we show that the dynamics are described by the non-Hermitian generator of the Yang-Lee transfer matrix, leading to an effective Yang-Lee theory defined on the spacetime history of the open system. By locally modifying the dynamics, we directly measure spin correlation functions of the Yang-Lee theory as well as a related "Loschmidt Echo" correlator which we detail. We explicitly show that the required dissipation channels can be obtained through local $2$-qubit gates and discuss potential realization on near-term quantum simulator devices. Finally, we generalize our construction to embed arbitrary non-Hermitian Hamiltonians within an open quantum system under Lindbladian time-evolution without post-selection, drawing connections between unconditional open quantum dynamics, exceptional point physics, and non-unitary statistical mechanics.

</details>


### [136] [Competing Soft Modes and Tunable Multicriticality in a Generalized Two-Mode Quantum Rabi Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25455)
*Xiufeng Cao,Ofri Adiv,Neil G. R. Broderick*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本研究揭示了强耦合光-物质系统中可调多临界现象的微观起源，通过推广的双模量子拉比模型，展示了各向异性如何通过相位锁定机制破坏U(1)对称性并导致多种多临界拓扑结构的出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索光-物质平台中多临界现象的微观起源，尤其是在缺乏完整对称性保护下的软模不稳定性与多临界点形成的机制。

Method: 采用推广的双模量子拉比模型，结合独立可调的旋转波和逆旋转波耦合，在统一的Bogoliubov框架下分析软模重分布与多临界相结构的关系。

Result: 发现各向异性通过相位锁定机制破坏U(1)对称性的超辐射流形，引发坐标型和动量型软模失稳，形成两重和四重点等多临界拓扑结构，并证明相变边界与平均场不稳定性条件精确一致。

Conclusion: 竞争的软模通道是强耦合光-物质系统中可调多临界性的共同微观起源，且多临界拓扑直接编码在基态能量及其响应函数中，建立了相拓扑、集体激发与热力学可观测量之间的统一联系。

Abstract: Multicritical phenomena play a central role in quantum many-body systems, yet their microscopic origin in light--matter platforms remains largely unexplored. Here we investigate a generalized two-mode quantum Rabi model with independently tunable rotating- and counter-rotating-wave couplings. We demonstrate that anisotropy lifts the parent U(1)-symmetric superradiant manifold with a gapless Goldstone-like mode by phase locking the complex superradiant order parameter. This phase-locking mechanism provides the common microscopic origin of the coordinate- and momentum-like soft-mode instabilities, the emergence of symmetry-related two- and four-triple-point multicritical topologies, and the corresponding thermodynamic responses. Within a unified Bogoliubov framework, we further show how the dominant soft mode is redistributed between the two symmetry-related instability channels, thereby determining the multicritical phase structure. The resulting phase boundaries are determined by collective soft-mode softening and coincide exactly with the mean-field instability conditions. We further show that the multicritical topology is directly encoded in the full quantum ground-state energy and its response functions, establishing a unified connection between phase topology, competing collective excitations, and thermodynamic observables. Our results identify competing soft-mode channels as the microscopic origin of tunable multicriticality in strongly coupled light--matter systems.

</details>


### [137] [Interpretable Activation-Selection Neural Networks for Symbolic Regression of Parameter-Dependent Hamiltonian Eigenvalues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25475)
*Alexander Yu. Kharin,Kirill F. Sheberstov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种可微分的符号回归架构——激活选择网络（ASN），用于从参数依赖哈密顿量中提取解析表达式，通过归一化和稀疏学习实现维度约简和物理一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了获得对参数依赖哈密顿量本征值的物理洞察，传统数值对角化方法难以提供直观的解析表达式，因此需要一种能自动生成简洁、物理合理表达式的符号回归方法。

Method: 设计激活选择网络（ASN），使用预定义函数库（如{0, x, x^2}）构建可微分符号回归模型；将哈密顿参数与本征值表示为无量纲比值以保证量纲一致性，并通过深层网络生成高阶多项式展开。

Result: 在三到四站点自旋链哈密顿量上验证了ASN的有效性，提取的表达式准确捕捉了常数项、线性和二次项结构；与简并微扰理论结果一致；固定基底最小二乘法在适当基底下表现相当或略优，加入径向特征可改善近退化点的局部逼近。

Conclusion: ASN为多种可能函数形式下选择紧凑符号表达式提供了可微分框架，但自适应激活选择并未相比合适预设基底带来本质精度优势。

Abstract: Analytical approximations to eigenvalues of parameter-dependent Hamiltonians can provide physical insight that is not readily apparent from numerical diagonalization alone. Here, we introduce an activation-selection network (ASN), a differentiable symbolic-regression architecture in which each input node learns a sparse combination of predefined analytical functions, and the trained network can be converted directly into an explicit expression. Before regression, the Hamiltonian parameters and eigenvalues are expressed as dimensionless ratios. This normalization enforces dimensional homogeneity, reduces the number of independent variables, and ensures that the extracted expressions do not depend on the choice of energy units. Using the library {0, x, x^2}, compositions across successive hidden layers generate polynomial expansions of progressively higher degree; polynomial expansions of arbitrary finite degree can therefore be obtained in principle by increasing the network depth. We apply the ASN to effective three- and four-site spin-chain Hamiltonians relevant to zero-quantum nuclear magnetic resonance. Comparisons with degenerate perturbation theory show that the extracted expressions capture the expected constant, linear, and quadratic structure. Fixed-basis least-squares models match or slightly outperform the ASN when an appropriate quadratic basis is specified in advance, while inclusion of a radial feature improves the local approximation near the degeneracy. These results establish the ASN as a differentiable framework for selecting compact symbolic representations when several functional forms are plausible, while showing that adaptive activation selection does not provide an intrinsic accuracy advantage over a suitable predefined basis.

</details>


### [138] [Low-leakage superconducting-qubit measurement with sub-100-ns total duration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25484)
*Peter A. Spring,Adrian L. Hesse,Shiyu Wang,Shuhei Tamate,Yasunobu Nakamura*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 实现了快速、高保真度且低泄漏的超导transmon量子比特测量，通过结合大的共振器衰减速率和接近最优信噪比每光子条件的色散位移，达到了97纳秒内的总测量时间及0.17%的分配误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速、准确且低泄漏的量子比特测量是实现量子纠错的关键需求。为了提高量子计算的可靠性，需要减少测量过程中引入的错误和状态泄漏。

Method: 结合了大的平均状态共振器衰减速率（κ_eff/2π = 30.8 MHz）与接近最优信噪比每光子条件的色散位移，并使用58纳秒的测量脉冲进行测量；利用重复测量序列和对泄漏敏感的测量来表征测量引起的状态跃迁。

Result: 实现了97(1)纳秒的总测量时间，分配误差为0.17(1)%，每测量泄漏率为2.7(2)×10^-5，仅为背景率的两倍，比测量引起的弛豫率低两个数量级。弗洛凯模拟表明，在工作点处存在的多光子共振弱耦合并以非绝热方式穿越，不会导致泄漏。

Conclusion: 大的共振器衰减速率结合接近最优信噪比每光子条件的色散位移，能够在小量子比特-共振器失谐下实现快速、高保真度、低泄漏的色散读出。

Abstract: Fast, accurate, and low-leakage qubit measurement is a key requirement for quantum error correction. Here, we demonstrate measurement of a superconducting transmon qubit with a total duration of 97(1) ns, defined as the time from the start of the measurement pulse until the measurement-induced error on a subsequent $π$-pulse operation falls below $10^{-4}$. By combining a large state-averaged resonator decay rate of $κ_\mathrm{eff}/2π$ = 30.8 MHz with a dispersive shift close to the optimal SNR-per-photon condition, we achieve an assignment error of 0.17(1)% using a 58-ns measurement pulse, with residual readout photons depleting passively in tens of nanoseconds without an active depletion pulse. Using a repeated-measurement sequence together with a leakage-sensitive measurement, we benchmark the measurement-induced state transitions, finding a per-measurement leakage rate of $2.7(2) \times 10^{-5}$, only twice the background rate and two orders of magnitude below the measurement-induced relaxation rate, which dominates the assignment error. Floquet simulations indicate that the multiphoton resonances present at the operating point are weakly coupled and traversed diabatically, without causing leakage. These results demonstrate that a large resonator decay rate, combined with a dispersive shift close to the optimal SNR-per-photon condition, can enable fast, high-fidelity, low-leakage dispersive readout at small qubit-resonator detuning.

</details>


### [139] [High-Fidelity Entangled States in a Connectivity-Four Fluxonium Quantum Processor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25503)
*J. Schirk,N. Bruckmoser,S. M. Taubenberger,F. Wallner,N. J. Glaser,M. Zetzl,L. Huang,I. Tsitsilin,M. Werninghaus,L. Södergren,K. Liegener,C. M. F. Schneider,Stefan Filipp*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于集总元件谐振器耦合器的fluxonium量子处理器，首次实现了具有抑制寄生相互作用的四连通单元结构，并展示了高保真度单比特和两比特门操作以及多比特纠缠态制备，为实现可扩展、兼容量子纠错的二维fluxonium处理器提供了可行路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展基于fluxonium的量子处理器的连接性至二维晶格以支持量子纠错是一个核心挑战，需要解决寄生相互作用和多比特操控中的误差问题。

Method: 采用集总元件谐振器作为耦合器，构建连接度为四的单元结构；使用谐振器诱导相位（RIP）门并引入重聚焦技术来抑制观测量子门操作中的旁观者误差。

Result: 实现了超过99.9%的并行单比特门保真度，所有耦合比特对之间的静态ZZ相互作用低于1 kHz；两比特门保真度超过99%；成功制备了最多五个比特的GHZ态，层析保真度达90%。

Conclusion: fluxonium-谐振器-fluxonium架构是实现高连通性、低串扰fluxonium量子处理器的有效途径，具备向支持量子纠错的大规模架构扩展的潜力。

Abstract: A central challenge in fluxonium-based quantum processors is the extension of the qubit connectivity to two-dimensional lattices compatible with quantum code-error correction. Here, we present a fluxonium quantum processor that employs lumped-element resonator couplers which realizes, for the first time, a connectivity-four unit cell with suppressed parasitic interactions. We achieve parallel single-qubit gate fidelities exceeding 99.9 % in simultaneous randomized benchmarking experiments, while maintaining residual static ZZ interactions below 1 kHz across all coupled qubit pairs. We implement resonator-induced phase (RIP) gates and benchmark two-qubit gate fidelities exceeding 99 % using interleaved randomized benchmarking. To cancel spectator errors observed in two-qubit operations, we implement a refocused RIP gate, recovering coherent control in the presence of multi-qubit connectivity. Furthermore, we prepare Greenberger-Horne-Zeilinger states of up to five qubits with a tomographic fidelity of 90 %, verifying multi-qubit entanglement within the unit cell. These results establish the fluxonium-resonator-fluxonium architecture as a viable approach to realizing densely connected fluxonium processors and provide a scalable path toward quantum error-correction-compatible processor architectures.

</details>


### [140] [A universal loss-limited optimum for fixed multi-pass quantum sensing per absorbed photon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25534)
*Christoph F. Wildfeuer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在样本稀缺的情况下，多通量方案中每个被吸收光子所获得的信息量。通过分析发现，信息增益与通过次数的平方成正比、每次通过的存活概率固定以及已损失光子不产生剂量三个特性决定了最优性能，并导出了统一的权衡函数 $h(x)=x^2/(e^x-1)$，其最大值为 0.648，出现在 $x_{\mathrm{opt}}=1.594$。结果表明，在考虑损耗限制时，最有效的测量方式是最简单的：单个光子循环使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在样本而非光资源稀缺的情况下，如何最大化每吸收一个光子所获取的信息是一个关键问题。传统的多通量方案虽能提升灵敏度，但可能增加样本损伤。因此，需要一种衡量标准来评估不同方案在单位吸收光子下的信息效率，并寻找最优策略以实现对脆弱样本的“温和”测量。

Method: 作者建立了一个通用模型，基于三个核心假设：信息随通过次数平方增长、每次通过具有固定的生存概率、已丢失光子不再造成剂量。利用这些假设推导出一个普适的权衡函数 $h(x)=x^2/(e^x-1)$，其中 $x$ 是每次通过的损耗与通过次数的乘积。通过对该函数进行优化，确定了在损耗受限情况下的最佳操作点。同时比较了N00N态及其他广义态的表现，并扩展分析到相位传感、吸收估计和微弱伴星探测等问题。

Result: 得出的关键结果是存在一个由函数 $h(x)=x^2/(e^x-1)$ 描述的性能上限，其最大值 0.648 出现在 $x_{\mathrm{opt}}=1.594$。这适用于包括交互自由 interrogation 和 Yu 等人的多通相位传感在内的多种方案。对于弱吸收物体的相位传感，最优通过次数为 $m_{\mathrm{opt}}=x_{\mathrm{opt}}/(ε+α)$，且损伤仅取决于吸收系数 $α$。N00N 态及其推广形式在此度量下表现更差，联合优化光子数和通过次数仍指向单光子循环最优。此外，吸收估计无法从多通方案中获益；而探测低于瑞利极限的微弱伴星时，多通方案对伴星的剂量恒定，不像直接成像那样随亮度降低而无限上升。

Conclusion: 在以吸收光子数为代价衡量信息获取效率的场景下，最高效的测量策略是使用单个光子并多次循环通过样本。这种最简单的方案达到了理论性能上限，且在面对脆弱样本时既是“最温和”的也是最优的。复杂量子态如N00N态在此指标下不如单光子循环有效，且多个问题（如相位估计、伴星探测）共享相同的优化结构。

Abstract: Multi-pass schemes send a photon through a sample several times to learn more about it. When the sample rather than the light is scarce, the natural figure of merit is the information gained per photon the object absorbs. We show that one constant fixes the loss-limited optimum of every fixed scheme in which a single photon passes repeatedly through the sample and is detected once at the end. Three properties suffice: information that grows as the square of the pass number, a fixed survival probability per pass, and no dose from a photon already lost. They force a single trade-off function $h(x)=x^2/(e^x-1)$, where $x$ is the number of passes times the loss per pass. Its maximum, 0.648, sits at $x_{\mathrm{opt}}=1.594$. The loss-limited ceiling of interaction-free interrogation and the multi-pass phase optimum of Yu et al. are two instances. Phase sensing of a weakly absorbing object is a third, optimal at $m_{\mathrm{opt}}=x_{\mathrm{opt}}/(ε+α)$ passes; the absorption $α$ and the parasitic loss $ε$ enter the optimum only through their sum, but the damage counts only $α$. N00N states and their loss-robust generalisations do worse per absorbed photon, and optimising over photon number and pass number together returns a single recycled photon. Two further problems are priced in the same measure. Absorption estimation gains nothing, for any scheme. Detecting a faint companion below the Rayleigh limit costs the companion a dose that does not depend on its faintness, while under direct imaging the dose grows without bound. For a fragile sample the gentlest measurement is also the simplest one: a single photon, recycled.

</details>


### [141] [Certified decoding of quantum LDPC codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25545)
*Ragavi Krishnamoorthy,Florian Gerhardt,Johannes Knaute,Thomas Klir,Stefan Raimund Maschek,Erik Schulze,Tomislav Maras,Alexander Dotterweich,Loong Kuan Lee,Christian Bauckhage,Nico Piatkowski*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于概率推断的量子低密度奇偶校验码（qLDPC）解码新方法，通过构建无向图模型来处理退化性问题，并设计了两种解码器，在多种噪声模型下性能优于现有方法且能为多数决策提供最优性证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于量子LDPC码中物理错误在稳定子下等价，传统最大似然解码需比较整个错误类的概率（即配分函数），而现有启发式方法如BP+OSD缺乏理论保证，因此需要一种能严格处理退化解码的通用框架。

Method: 将退化解码建模为无向图模型中的概率推断问题，定义基于校验变量的正定马尔可夫随机场，推广了表面码的随机键Ising模型至任意CSS码和含测量误差的时空解码；在此基础上构建两种解码器：一是基于退火重要性采样与共同随机数估计各类配分函数，并结合自举检验或WISH算法提供最优性证书；二是基于区域的方法，利用Bethe自由能在类别间的偏差抵消特性实现精确ML解码，扩展至消除簇后可处理大规模双变量自行车码。

Result: 在表面码及[[72,12,6]]、[[144,12,12]]双变量自行车码上，新解码器在码容量、现象学和电路级噪声模型下性能达到或超过BP+OSD，同时为大部分决策提供了最优性证书，且证书能准确标识出应怀疑快速解码器输出的综合征。

Conclusion: 该工作为qLDPC码的退化最大似然解码提供了严格的概率建模范式，所提解码器不仅性能优越，还能提供决策可信度验证，有助于构建可信赖的容错量子计算系统。

Abstract: Quantum low-density parity-check (qLDPC) codes reduce the qubit overhead of fault-tolerant quantum computation by an order of magnitude, but their decoding is harder than its classical counterpart: because many physical errors are equivalent up to stabilizers, the degenerate maximum-likelihood (ML) decoder must compare the probabilities of entire equivalence classes of errors, that is, partition functions, rather than single errors. The workhorse decoder BP+OSD sidesteps degeneracy heuristically and offers no guarantees. We treat degenerate decoding as probabilistic inference in an undirected graphical model: the probability of each logical class is the partition function of an unconstrained, strictly positive Markov random field over the code's check variables, a construction that generalizes the random-bond Ising mapping of the surface code to arbitrary CSS codes and to spacetime decoding with measurement errors and circuit-level noise. On this model we build two decoders. The first estimates all class partition functions by annealed importance sampling with common random numbers and attaches to every decision a certificate of optimality: a paired bootstrap test, or, composed with constant-factor estimators such as WISH, an exact optimality proof. The second is region-based: the Bethe free energy, whose bias cancels between classes, reproduces exact ML decoding on every tested surface-code instance at millisecond cost, and enlarging the regions to elimination clusters makes exact degenerate ML decoding of the [[72,12,6]] bivariate bicycle code feasible. Across surface codes and the bivariate bicycle codes [[72,12,6]] and [[144,12,12]], under code-capacity, phenomenological, and circuit-level noise, the sampling decoder matches or exceeds BP+OSD while certifying the bulk of its decisions, and the certificate flags exactly the syndromes on which any fast decoder should be distrusted.

</details>


### [142] [GNSS-free quantum gravity-aided navigation and fine-scale marine surveying with a strapdown quantum gravimeter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25563)
*Patrick J. Everitt,Donald H. White,Todd Lyon,Murat Muradoglu,Alessandro D'Ortenzio,Aaron J. Canciani,Malo Cadoret,Daniel D. Brown,David Adams,Yosri Ben-Aïcha,Suraj Bijjahalli,Mojtaba K. Farsani,Alexander Rischka,Karandeep S. Gill,Magdalena Meyer,Henry W. Orton,Nicholas P. Robins,Reuben Symon,Michael J. Biercuk,Michael R. Hush,Stuart S. Szigeti,Russell P. Anderson*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本研究首次实现了基于量子重力仪的完全独立于GNSS的海上导航与重力图匹配，验证了在无GNSS条件下通过量子传感进行高分辨率重力测量和惯性导航校正的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于海上GNSS信号易受干扰或不可用，而惯性导航系统（INS）存在漂移问题，亟需一种无需基础设施、被动式且可靠的导航辅助手段。量子传感结合重力图匹配被视为潜在解决方案，但此前尚无实际海上验证。

Method: 研究人员将一台混合式量子重力仪（结合原子传感器与经典加速度计）安装在29米长的水面船只上，在未控环境中运行，不依赖环境稳定或校准。系统以陀螺稳定（gimbaled）和捷联式（strapdown）两种模式运行，利用卫星-derived重力异常图进行重力图匹配，对83公里航程中的INS进行校正，并在另一模式下以GNSS参考方式进行沿海重力勘测。

Result: 在全程排除GNSS的情况下，重力辅助将惯性导航的漂移限制在nautical-mile级精度；在GNSS参考模式下，系统实现mGal级测量精度和亚mGal重复性，沿轨迹分辨率达到约300米，比卫星重力图半功率波长精细50倍；56小时静态测试显示原子参考使长期漂移降低约70倍。两种配置性能相当。

Conclusion: 该研究首次在同一仪器上对比了陀螺稳定与捷联式移动量子重力测量，并成功演示了完全独立于GNSS的量子重力图匹配导航，为未来小型化、适用于自主平台的量子传感在拒止环境中的海上导航与勘测应用铺平了道路。

Abstract: Global navigation satellite systems (GNSS) are often disrupted or unavailable at sea, and unaided inertial navigation systems (INS) drift without correction. Quantum-sensing-based gravity map matching offers a passive, infrastructure-free aid, but field demonstrations of GNSS-free quantum gravimetric navigation have not been reported. Here we perform gravity map matching and fine-resolution gravity survey with a mobile quantum gravimeter aboard a 29 m surface vessel. We hybridize an atomic sensor with a classical accelerometer for bias stabilization and independently mechanize a navigation-grade IMU, all installed in an uncontrolled cabin with no environmental stabilization or calibration. Operated in both gimbaled and strapdown configurations over identical traversals, the hybrid sensor corrected the inertial solution over an 83 km maritime trajectory by referencing locally measured gravity to a satellite-derived anomaly map. Gravity-aiding constrains INS drift and delivers bounded positioning at nautical-mile-level accuracy, with GNSS excluded throughout the measurement chain. In a separate GNSS-referenced mode, the same system surveyed coastal routes up to Sea State 4, achieving mGal-level agreement with gravimetric maps and sub-mGal repeatability and stability, with gimbaled and strapdown operation performing comparably. Resolved anomalies reach an along-track scale of ~300 m, 50X finer than the satellite map's half-power wavelength. A 56 h stationary test shows atom referencing lowers long-term drift ~70X versus the classical channel alone. These results provide the first same-instrument comparison of gimbaled and strapdown mobile quantum gravimetry and the first fully GNSS-independent gravity-map-matching navigation demonstration using a quantum gravimeter, pointing toward compact, autonomous-platform-ready quantum sensing for GNSS-denied maritime navigation and survey.

</details>


### [143] [Fast-forwarding quantum algorithms for weakly nonlinear dissipative differential equations and beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25822)
*Yixiang Li,Dong An*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合Carleman嵌入与线性哈密顿模拟的快速量子算法，用于求解弱非线性耗散常微分方程，复杂度不显含演化时间T，实现O(1)性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在提升现有量子算法在求解非线性微分方程时的时间复杂度，特别是消除对演化时间T的依赖，以实现更高效的计算。

Method: 结合Carleman嵌入技术与线性组合哈密顿量模拟算法，对线性化系统进行快速缩放，并简化了基于Carleman嵌入算法中的后选择步骤。

Result: 算法复杂度由先前的\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(\sqrt{T})提升至\mathcal{O}(1)，时间依赖仅通过输出范数和驱动参数隐式体现；给出了Carleman嵌入在时变系数矩阵下的收敛性保证，并进行了数值实验验证。

Conclusion: 该方法显著提高了求解弱非线性耗散ODE的效率，具备实现快速缩放的潜力，且可能推广至更强非线性或线性非共振系统。

Abstract: We study a fast-forwarded quantum algorithm for solving weakly nonlinear dissipative ordinary differential equations. Our approach is a combination of the Carleman embedding technique and the linear combination of Hamiltonian simulation algorithm for linearized systems with fast-forwarded scaling. The complexity of our algorithm does not explicitly depend on the evolution time $T$, thus greatly improving the previous state-of-the-art $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(\sqrt{T})$ to $\mathcal{O}(1)$, and any remaining time dependence enters through the output norm and forcing parameters. We rigorously analyze the performance of this approach by convergence guarantees of the Carleman embedding for time-dependent coefficient matrices and detailed complexity estimates, and improve the realization of the Carleman-embedding-based algorithms by simplifying the post-selection step. In addition, we perform a numerical study on differential equations beyond the weakly nonlinear case, and identify possibility of achieving fast-forwarding scaling for systems with stronger nonlinearity or linear non-resonant effect.

</details>


### [144] [Phase-sensitive cascade quantum amplifier with nearly noiseless operation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25645)
*Ilari Lilja,Ekaterina Mukhanova,Michael Perelshtein,Kirill Petrovnin,Stanislav Khaldeev,Visa Vesterinen,Gheorghe Sorin Paraoanu,Pertti Hakonen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本研究利用SQUID基约瑟夫森参量放大器（JPA）在相位保持和相位敏感模式下的性能对比，结合行波参量放大器（TWPA）实现低噪声级联放大，在毫开尔文温度下实现了低于标准量子极限的系统噪声温度，展示了高保真探测量子器件的新方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索相敏参量器件在亚量子极限噪声性能方面的潜力，提升对量子器件的高保真度探测能力。

Method: 采用VTT SWAPS技术制备基于SQUID的JPA，在三波混频配置下以反射式测量系统进行表征，并使用TWPA作为低噪声前置放大器构建级联放大方案，直接探测低增益JPA的噪声性能。

Result: 在相位保持模式下，系统噪声温度为351±53 mK；在相位敏感模式下，最低系统噪声温度达到94±12 mK，显著低于288 mK的标准量子极限。

Conclusion: JPA-TWPA级联放大方案可实现无需背景噪声扣除的直接、高保真量子器件探测，为量子测量提供了一种高性能放大新途径。

Abstract: Phase-sensitive parametric devices enable quadrature-selective amplification with the potential for sub-quantum-limited noise performance. In this work, we investigate the operation of a SQUID-based Josephson Parametric Amplifier (JPA), comparing its performance in the phase-preserving and phase-sensitive regimes. The device, fabricated using VTT SWAPS technology, is driven in a three-wave mixing configuration and characterized in a reflection-based measurement setup at millikelvin temperatures. To directly probe the noise performance at low JPA gains, we employ a cascaded amplification scheme in which a Traveling-Wave Parametric Amplifier (TWPA) provides low-noise pre-amplification of the JPA output. In a phase-preserving operation, the JPA exhibits near-quantum-limited performance with a system noise temperature of $351\pm53$ mK at 6 GHz. In contrast, phase-sensitive operation yields a minimum system noise temperature of $94\pm12$ mK, well below the standard quantum limit of 288 mK. Our results demonstrate that a JPA-TWPA amplifier cascade opens the door to direct, high-fidelity probing of quantum devices without the need for background noise subtraction.

</details>


### [145] [Quantum motility-induced phase separation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25649)
*Laurin Brunner,Ricard Alert,Reyhaneh Khasseh,Markus Heyl*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了主动硬核玻色子系统中是否存在真正的量子MIPS，并探讨了量子相干性与耗散自推进之间的相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索主动物质概念在量子层面的扩展，以及量子相干性如何与耗散自推进竞争或协作。

Method: 使用一维主动硬核玻色子模型进行理论分析和数值模拟。

Result: 发现量子MIPS可以在该模型中发生，表现出超线性数量波动特征，导致微相分离；加入近邻排斥相互作用后，恢复了具有发散相关长度的真实相分离；聚类稳态保持长距离量子相干性。

Conclusion: 结果为探索量子MIPS提供了基础，并表明相干性-活性相互作用可以产生新型非平衡量子态。

Abstract: Motility-induced phase separation (MIPS) describes a central clustering phenomenon in active matter systems where particles spontaneously separate into dense and dilute phases even in the absence of interparticle attractive forces. Recent theoretical and experimental efforts have taken the first steps to extend active matter concepts to the quantum level. However, whether a genuine quantum analog of MIPS exists and how quantum coherence would compete or cooperate with the dissipative self-propulsion has remained open so far. Here, we provide evidence that quantum MIPS can occur in a model of active hard-core bosons in one dimension. Our model yields superlinear number fluctuations characteristic of MIPS, leading to microphase separation with large but finite cluster size. Adding nearest-neighbor repulsive interactions, we find numerical evidence for restoring genuine phase separation with a divergent correlation length. Crucially, the clustered steady states maintain a long-distance quantum coherence, revealing a genuinely quantum feature with no classical counterpart. These results provide a foundation for exploring quantum MIPS and suggest that the coherence-activity interplay can generate new types of nonequilibrium quantum states.

</details>


### [146] [The Geometric Phase as a Diagnostic for Driven-Dissipative Oscillators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25742)
*Zeen Sun,Yuan Shen,Haitao Ding,Yuancheng Zhan,Leong-Chuan Kwek*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于混合态几何相位的统一框架，用于描述受驱耗散量子振荡器在非平衡稳态下的相位锁定、极限环变形和耗散相变等行为。该几何相位来源于驱动相位扫描所形成的稳态闭合路径，并因李维尔算符的数旋转协变性而简化为单个稳态本征结构的泛函。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同现象（如相位同步与相变）通常需用不同物理量分析，缺乏统一描述。作者旨在寻找一个能同时刻画相位锁定、极限环变化和耗散相变的普适几何量。

Method: 利用李维尔动力学中数旋转对称性，将非平衡稳态环路的运动学混合态几何相位约化为单一稳态的本征系统泛函，并结合弱驱动、近霍普夫阈值及克尔谐振腔模型进行分析。

Result: 几何相位在弱驱动下由最近邻相干性主导，重现同步的阿诺德舌；在霍普夫分岔附近反映稳态本征向量的非微扰重组；在压缩驱动的克尔谐振腔中，对一阶和二阶耗散相变表现出明确区分的信号。

Conclusion: 混合态几何相位为耗散量子系统中的稳态重构提供了一个统一且实验可测的表征工具，连接了同步、极限环形变与相变等不同物理现象。

Abstract: Driven-dissipative quantum oscillators lock their phase, deform their limit cycles, and undergo dissipative phase transitions, yet these behaviors are read from unrelated quantities defined on the same steady-state density matrix. We show that a single geometric quantity organizes them. Winding the phase of the drive generates a closed loop of nonequilibrium steady states, and because the Liouvillian is covariant under number rotations, the kinematic mixed-state geometric phase of this loop reduces exactly to an eigensystem functional of a single steady state. Under weak driving, it is governed by the same nearest-neighbor coherences that produce phase locking and inherits the Arnold tongue of synchronization. Near the Hopf threshold, it registers the nonperturbative reorganization of the steady-state eigenvectors. And in the squeezing-driven Kerr resonator, it develops distinct signatures at the first and second order dissipative phase transitions. The geometric phase thus provides a unified and experimentally accessible characterization of steady-state reorganization.

</details>


### [147] [Quantum sampling in hybrid light-matter systems with mixed statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25788)
*Franziska Barkhausen,Laura Ares,Stefan Schumacher,Jan Sperling*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了光-物质相互作用系统中的混合玻色-费米采样，提出输出概率涉及非纯行列式或永久式的广义不变量（immanants），并证明其独立性，为超越传统量子采样的应用提供了新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索超越传统玻色采样和费米采样框架的量子采样方法，利用光-物质相互作用系统的复杂性实现更强大的量子优势验证。

Method: 结合玻色子和费米子激发的混合网络输入，分析输出模式的概率分布，引入并研究广义immanants在量子采样中的作用，并探讨非玻色非费米统计下的扩展表达形式。

Result: 证明了immanant项在线性意义上独立于行列式和永久式，表明混合采样网络具有独特计算特性；发现非标准统计下的采样可超出immanants，拓展了量子采样器的功能。

Conclusion: 混合光-物质量子采样系统因其独特的数学结构和超越传统采样模型的能力，是实现复杂量子计算和验证量子优势的理想平台。

Abstract: Boson sampling is one of the most prominent methods to verify quantum advantage, harnessing the computational complexity of the permanent of a network matrix. Similar quantum sampling problems utilize other expressions for the output probabilities, such as the fermion-based analog applying determinants. In this work, we study sampling setups of interacting light-matter systems, combining fermion and boson sampling. The mixed network input consists of fermionic and bosonic excitations. Sampling the output probability of the modes then yields contributions that are neither purely determinant nor permanent but general immanants. Specifically, we prove that these immanant contributions are linearly independent from determinants and permanents, rendering our mixed sampling network an ideal candidate for light-matter quantum application beyond pure fermion and boson sampling. Furthermore, quantum sampling with statistics that are neither bosonic nor fermionic results in expressions which even exceed immanants, further extending the functionality of quantum samplers.

</details>


### [148] [Fault-tolerant $|\sqrt{ \mathrm{T} }\rangle$ state preparation and injection for more efficient fine-grained quantum circuit synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25797)
*Berat Yenilen,Markus Müller,Manuel Rispler*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在三维四面体色码及其变形变体上实现逻辑$|\sqrt{\mathrm{T}} \rangle$魔态制备的旗标容错协议，通过电路级噪声下的仿真验证了其容错性，并证明使用$\sqrt{\mathrm{T}}$门可将单量子比特酉操作的空间时间开销降低20-30%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了降低通用容错量子计算中的编译开销，需要扩展可用的非Clifford基元集合，前提是这些新基元能够以较低的资源成本和逻辑噪声率实现容错制备。

Method: 提出了基于3D四面体色码和其变形变体的旗标容错协议，用于制备逻辑$|\sqrt{\mathrm{T}} \rangle$魔态，并在电路级噪声下进行仿真，通过逻辑过程层析重建有效逻辑通道。

Result: 验证了所提协议的容错性，量化了接受率和逻辑错误率，发现使用$\sqrt{\mathrm{T}}$门相比Clifford+T门集可将Haar随机单量子比特酉合成的平均时空成本降低20-30%。

Conclusion: 扩展容错非Clifford基元集合可以提升计算效率，为早期容错量子计算提供更多设计空间。

Abstract: Magic-state injection is a standard route to realize universal fault-tolerant quantum computation. Whereas the set of Clifford gates in combination with the non-Clifford T gate is a widely used universal gate set, extending the available set of non-Clifford primitives can reduce compilation overhead, provided that the additional primitives can be prepared fault-tolerantly with competitive resource costs and at sufficiently low logical noise rates. In this work, we introduce flag fault-tolerant protocols for preparing logical $|\sqrt{\mathrm{T}} \rangle $ magic states on the 3D tetrahedral color code and its smaller morphed variant. Our simulations under circuit-level noise verify fault tolerance, quantify acceptance and logical error rates, and we reconstruct the effective logical channels of the corresponding circuits for gate injection via logical process tomography. We find that access to $\sqrt{\mathrm{T}}$ reduces the average space-time cost of synthesizing Haar-random single-qubit unitaries by approximately $20$-$30$% relative to the Clifford+T gate set across practically relevant approximation regimes. These results demonstrate how expanding the set of fault-tolerant non-Clifford primitives can improve computational efficiency and broaden the design space for universal quantum computation in the early fault-tolerant era.

</details>


### [149] [The View from Within: What Can Embedded Observers (Not) Learn?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25800)
*Tomáš Gonda,Johannes Fankhauser,Gemma De les Coves*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过一个经典粒子的玩具模型，研究了作为世界内部物理系统的观察者（主体）如何从第一人称视角认识世界，并揭示了即使在经典确定性系统中，主体也存在认知极限（认知视界），例如只能获知对象一半变量的‘知识平衡原理’，且预测比追溯更受限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决物理学通常采用第三人称外部视角描述世界，而实际观察者是处于世界之中的物理系统这一张力问题，探讨嵌入式观察者的认知局限。

Method: 构建一个经典粒子的玩具模型，将观察者定义为具有显变量（经验记录）和预备态（初始信息）的物理系统，分析主体通过与客体建立关联的相互作用，在不同学习类型（关于过去、未来或两者）、显变量和预备态组合下能获得何种知识。

Result: 发现许多主体存在认知视界：即使模型是经典且确定性的，主体的认知仍受限制；具有完全显变量但部分初始信息的主体最多只能获知客体一半的变量，重现了Spekkens的知识平衡原理；预测所面临的认知限制比追溯更严重；可重复测量的学习方式可能比单独预测或追溯更受限。

Conclusion: 观察者的内在视角（第一人称）与其所处世界的外在描述（第三人称）之间存在根本差异，这种差异导致了即使在经典系统中也会出现类似量子不确定性原理的认知限制，为从嵌入式观察者角度解释量子理论中的内在不确定性提供了语言和工具。

Abstract: Physics is usually done from a third-person perspective, as if the world were described from outside. Yet, observers are themselves physical systems within the world they observe. Here, we investigate this tension using a toy model of classical particles. Observers are physical systems characterised by a choice of (i) a manifest variable, whose value constitutes their empirical record, and (ii) ready states, which provide initial information. We ask what such observers (subjects) can learn about the world through interactions that establish a correlation with another system (the object). For each combination of the three types of learning (about the past, the future, or both), manifest variables, and ready states, we determine what the subject can learn. This shows that many subjects face an epistemic horizon---a limitation to what they can learn about the world---even though the model is classical and deterministic. For example, a subject with a complete manifest variable---one whose ontic state is the empirical record---and partial initial information can learn at most half the object's variables, recovering Spekkens' knowledge-balance principle, i.e. an analogue of Heisenberg's uncertainty principle. More generally, we find that subjects face more severe epistemic horizons when predicting than when retrodicting, and that learning by repeatable measurements can be more limited than either prediction or retrodiction alone. Our work provides language and tools to study how the first-person perspective can differ from the third-person perspective beyond the toy model studied here, and invites explanations of the inherent uncertainty in quantum theory from the standpoint of embedded observers.

</details>


### [150] [Enhanced quantum metrology with robust multipass interferometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25842)
*Sayak Mukherjee,José Afonso Oliveira,Sean William Moore,Jacob A. Dunningham*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合小规模纠缠态与多通干涉测量的混合量子计量策略，在保持抗损耗鲁棒性的同时显著提升测量精度，并可通过现有技术实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子计量依赖大规模纠缠态以超越标准量子极限，但其制备和维持困难且对损耗敏感；多通协议虽避免大规模纠缠，但累积损耗仍限制其性能。

Method: 将小规模、抗损耗的纠缠态与多通干涉技术相结合，利用重复相互作用增强测量精度，并设计基于现有技术的实验实现方案。

Result: 该混合策略在存在损耗的情况下仍能保持量子优势，显著提高测量精度，且理论预测的性能增益可在当前实验条件下实现。

Conclusion: 结合小纠缠态与多通干涉是一种实用且高效的量子计量方法，为实现高精度测量提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Quantum metrology typically uses entangled states to achieve measurement precisions beyond the standard quantum limit. The advantage increases with the size of the entangled state, however generating and preserving large entangled states remains a major experimental challenge. Multipass protocols offer an alternative approach by allowing a single probe to interact repeatedly with the parameter of interest, but their performance is highly susceptible to loss, which accumulates over successive passes and rapidly erodes the quantum advantage. Here we introduce a hybrid strategy that combines small, loss-resilient entangled states with multipass interferometry. We show that this approach retains the robustness of small entangled probes while exploiting repeated interactions to achieve substantial enhancements in measurement precision. Furthermore, we propose a concrete implementation using currently available technologies, demonstrating that the predicted performance gains should be experimentally accessible with existing capabilities.

</details>


### [151] [Surpassing Gaussian optimality in multiparameter estimation with indefinite causal order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25870)
*Sudipta Das,Rivu Gupta,Aditi Sen De,Himadri Shekhar Dhar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在连续变量量子系统中同时估计位移和压缩操作的最优单模高斯探针，发现通过将部分能量用于位移而非实验成本较高的压缩资源，可在固定能量下实现最佳精度。引入不定因果序（ICO）可突破高斯精度极限，且该优势不能仅归因于非高斯性，表明ICO是一种独立的增强多参数估计性能的资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提高连续变量量子系统中多参数估计的精度并降低实验成本，探索超越传统高斯探针性能限制的方法。

Method: 利用位移和压缩操作生成单模高斯探针，并分析其在不同能量分配下的估计精度；引入不定因果序（ICO）到探针制备或参数编码过程中，评估其对估计精度的影响。
