Result: 发现最优精度并非依赖于高成本的压缩资源，而是通过将更多能量分配到位移操作来实现；引入ICO可在特定参数范围内超越最优高斯探针的精度极限，且该优势与非高斯性无直接关联。

Conclusion: 不定因果序（ICO）是一种独立于非高斯性的资源，可用于提升多参数量子估计的精度，为更高效的量子传感提供了新途径。

Abstract: We identify single-mode Gaussian probes, generated by displacement and squeezing operations on the vacuum state, which are optimal for the simultaneous estimation of displacement and squeezing operations in continuous-variable quantum systems. Importantly, our results reveal that the best precision at a fixed energy is achieved not by an experimentally costly squeezing resource, but rather by redirecting some of the energy towards displacement, thus allowing for more resource-effective operations. Furthermore, introducing indefinite causal order (ICO) in either the probe preparation or parameter encoding step can surpass the Gaussian precision bound, even though the optimal Gaussian probe state is agnostic to the ordering of the operations. Specifically, we observe that odd-parity superpositions of the two definite orders can enhance precision over optimal Gaussian probes in specific parameter regimes. Further, the observed advantage cannot be attributed solely to non-Gaussianity, as quantified by the relative entropy of non-Gaussianity, highlighting ICO as an independent resource for enhancing multiparameter estimation.

</details>


### [152] [How much randomness in a quantum process can be explained using memory?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25878)
*Derek D. C. Chang,Graeme D. Berk,Mile Gu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于Choi态表示的平稳量子随机过程中不可约随机性的收敛度量方法，并可用于量化时间关联和估计实现该过程所需的最小记忆资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子系统中的复杂性科学缺乏对信息分解的有效工具，尤其是在处理侵入性测量和量子关联时，因此需要发展量子类比方法来识别不可约随机性和可学习结构。

Method: 利用编码多时间输入-输出关系的过程张量的Choi态表示，构建平稳量子随机过程中不可约随机性的收敛度量，并由此导出对时间关联的度量。

Result: 提出了一个收敛的不可约随机性度量，能够下界定复制该量子过程所需的记忆资源，并为基于循环量子电路的实现提供理论依据。

Conclusion: 该方法为分析和预测量子动力学系统提供了新的工具，推动了量子复杂性科学的发展。

Abstract: For a stochastic process describing observations of a dynamical system, complexity science provides systematic methods to decompose the information produced into true irreducible randomness, and that which corresponds to structure superficially disguised as random but can in fact be learned and predicted. Such methods then equip vital tools for prediction, control and inference of the system's internal structure. However, quantum analogs remain underdeveloped due to the inherent complications of invasive measurements and quantum correlations. By harnessing the Choi state representation of process tensors which encode multi-time input-output relations, we formulate a convergent measure of irreducible randomness in stationary quantum stochastic processes. This directly enables a measure of temporal correlations which lower bounds the memory resources required to replicate the process via a recurrent quantum circuit.

</details>


### [153] [Distributed Trotterization with optimal time-scaling entanglement cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25896)
*Tianfeng Feng,Jinzhao Sun,Yunlong Xiao,Qi Zhao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种自适应纠缠消耗的重复直到成功协议，用于分布式量子模拟，其纠缠成本随演化时间线性增长且与 Trotter 误差无关，达到了最优时间标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于量子隐形传态的分布式量子模拟在高精度下纠缠成本发散，效率低下，亟需一种更高效的非局域操作实现方式。

Method: 引入一种简单的重复直到成功的协议，使纠缠消耗适应相互作用强度，并将其集成到分布式乘积公式中。

Result: 总纠缠成本随演化时间线性缩放，且独立于 Trotter 误差；通过量子通信复杂性得出匹配下界，证明该时间标度是最优的。

Conclusion: 该协议为网络化处理器上的高精度量子模拟建立了资源高效的实现基础。

Abstract: Distributed architectures extend quantum simulation of many-body dynamics beyond the reach of any single processor, with shared entanglement mediating interactions between spatially separated devices. Conventional implementations rely on quantum teleportation, which provides a universal realization of nonlocal operations but incurs a fixed entanglement cost per gate, irrespective of its strength. This becomes increasingly inefficient in product formula simulation, where higher accuracy requires ever more numerous, yet progressively weaker, nonlocal rotations, causing the entanglement cost to diverge in the high-accuracy limit. Here we introduce a simple repeat-until-success protocol that makes entanglement consumption adaptive to interaction strength. Incorporating this primitive into distributed product formulas yields a total entanglement cost that scales linearly with evolution time and remains independent of Trotter error. A matching lower bound from quantum communication complexity proves this time scaling to be optimal, establishing a resource-efficient foundation for high-accuracy quantum simulation across networked processors.

</details>


### [154] [Structured Non-Locality and Emergent Locality in Cavity-QED Many-Body Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25908)
*Mark A. Oehlgrien,Błażej Jaworowski,Darrick E. Chang,Charlie-Ray Mann*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究了腔量子电动力学（QED）中集体耦合主导情况下，多体系统在不同子空间中的有效动力学行为，提出局域积态子空间与纠缠子空间的不同演化机制，并展示了其在生成无局域或全集体对应的新类型多体动力学中的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在强集体耦合下，腔QED系统中多体动力学的组织原则，超越传统的局域性或全集性描述框架。

Method: 通过分析各孤立能量子空间是否存在局域积态基，结合角动量选择规则，推导出两类代表性腔QED自旋模型的有效哈密顿量。

Result: 发现积态子空间保留微观相互作用的空间结构，而纠缠子空间则引入非局域但结构化的动力学；腔隔离子空间可作为实现竞争性短程相互作用或全局调控局域过程的资源。

Conclusion: 腔QED中孤立子空间的结构性质决定了系统动力学的本质特征，为设计新型多体量子态和动力学提供了新思路。

Abstract: Cavity quantum electrodynamics (QED) modifies many-body systems by combining cavity-mediated collective interactions with microscopic short-range interactions. The resulting dynamics lies between the local and fully collective limits, such that neither locality nor collectivity alone provides a complete organizing principle. In the clean conceptual limit of dominant collective coupling, we show that the effective dynamics within each energetically isolated subspace is generically controlled by whether that subspace admits a local product-state basis, and identify exceptions imposed by angular-momentum selection rules. Product-state subspaces generically retain the spatial structure of the microscopic interaction. Entangled subspaces instead generically dress local processes with global operators, generating non-local but highly structured dynamics. We illustrate this by deriving the corresponding effective Hamiltonians in two representative cavity-QED spin models. The cavity-isolated subspace thus becomes a resource for generating competing short-range interactions or globally conditioned local processes, opening a class of many-body dynamics without local or fully collective counterparts.

</details>


### [155] [Entirely nonlocal quantum magic without entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25950)
*Fuchuan Wei,Ruixia Wang,Yujia Zhang,Huihui Li,Junfeng Li,Fei Yan,Zi-Wen Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文发现了一种与量子特性看似矛盾的现象：完全非局域的“魔法”（ENM）——即仅存在于关联中而各子系统局部不存在的非稳定子性——可以在无纠缠的情况下存在。作者系统地研究了这种分离现象，并证明其具有普适性和操作可逆性：任何魔法态或通道都可以通过局域稳定子操作和经典通信编码到并恢复自可分的ENM实现中。基于此机制，提出了激活密钥协议和魔法秘密共享方案，并在超导量子处理器上实验验证了相关协议，展示了无需纠缠即可实现魔法的经典激活、定位和秘密共享，为分布式量子计算提供了新的资源控制原语。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非稳定子性（magic）这一关键量子资源是否必须依赖于纠缠，以及能否在无纠缠系统中实现其功能，从而拓展对量子优势资源的理解与控制手段。

Method: 通过理论构建证明完全非局域魔法（ENM）可在可分态中存在，并提出将任意魔法态或通道编码至可分ENM态中的通用可逆协议；结合激活密钥与魔法秘密共享机制，在超导量子处理器上实验实现了ENM态制备、提取及网络计算原语。

Result: 理论证明了魔法可以不依赖纠缠而以非局域形式存在；实现了可分态中的ENM编码与恢复；实验演示了激活密钥协议、魔法秘密共享及ENM相关协议在超导平台上的可行性。

Conclusion: 非稳定子性（魔法）可以通过经典关联被激活、局域化和秘密共享，而无需纠缠，这为分布式量子计算提供了新的资源控制范式。

Abstract: Nonstabilizerness, or magic, is an archetypal \emph{quantum} resource that is necessary for quantum computational advantage. Here we uncover a phenomenon seemingly at odds with the quantum nature of magic: entirely nonlocal magic (ENM)---magic present only in correlations and absent from each party's marginal---can live without entanglement. We systematically study this separation and show it is universal and operationally reversible: every magical state or channel can be encoded into and recovered from a separable ENM realization using only local stabilizer processing and classical communication. We leverage this mechanism to devise an activation key protocol in which a classical key controls access to non-Clifford operations. We further formulate magic secret sharing, in which computational power inaccessible to any party alone becomes accessible through cooperation. On a superconducting quantum processor, we experimentally demonstrate activation key and network computing primitives, together with separable ENM state preparation and extraction protocols. Together, our results establish that magic can be classically activated, localized, and secret-shared without entanglement, providing new resource-control primitives for distributed quantum computation.

</details>


### [156] [Position- and Momentum-Space Quantum Information Measures of the Double-Morse Oscillator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25953)
*Firoz Chogle,Ernesto Damiani,Berihu Teklu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究了双Morse势中粒子在位置和动量空间下的量子信息特性，分析了Shannon熵、Fisher信息等随势参数变化的行为，发现基态和激发态在单双势阱转变过程中表现出不同的结构复杂性和局域化特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究双Morse势模型在量子信息度量中的行为，理解势形变对量子态复杂性的影响。

Method: 利用模型的准精确可解性，获得前两个束缚态的解析波函数，计算位置和动量空间中的Shannon熵、Onicescu能量、Fisher信息、统计复杂度和Fisher-Shannon积。

Result: 位置空间分布在双势阱明显时更离域且结构更复杂，动量空间则呈互补趋势；随着势阱合并，基态Fisher-Shannon积趋近高斯参考值，而激发态仍保持较强的非高斯特征。

Conclusion: 双Morse势的几何转变显著影响量子态的信息特征，基态与激发态在趋向高斯性上表现出不同行为，揭示了势形变对量子复杂性的调控作用。

Abstract: We investigate the quantum-information properties of a particle confined by the double Morse potential in position and momentum spaces. The quasi-exact solvability of the model gives analytical expressions for the first two bound states, allowing the corresponding probability densities to be analyzed directly. Shannon entropy, Onicescu energy, Fisher information, statistical complexity, and Fisher-Shannon products are evaluated as functions of the parameter $A$, which controls the transition from a well-separated double well to a merged single-well profile. The position distribution is more delocalized and structurally complex when the double-well character is pronounced, whereas the momentum distribution exhibits the complementary trend. As the wells merge, the ground-state Fisher--Shannon product approaches its Gaussian reference value, whereas the excited state retains stronger non-Gaussian structure.

</details>


### [157] [Dissipatively Stabilized 0-n Fock Qubits for Noise-Biased Quantum Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25959)
*Su Direkci,Simon Lieu,Kyungjoo Noh,Connor T. Hann*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种“0-n”福克比特架构，通过在非线性多能级系统中编码信息来维持噪声偏置，实现了比特翻转错误的指数抑制，并展示了在实际实验参数下受控X门的比特翻转概率接近10^-8，同时在重复码的综合征提取中达到了兆量子级别的逻辑错误率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在使用非偏置控制比特进行门操作时噪声偏置被破坏的问题，以实现容错量子计算。

Method: 利用多模式损耗滤波器实现频率选择性耗散，通过耗散稳定化将信息编码在非线性多能级系统的基态和第n激发态中。

Result: 理论上证明了比特翻转错误随能级数量呈指数抑制；数值模拟显示，在使用n≥9的0-n比特作为辅助时，猫比特的受控X门的比特翻转概率可接近10^-8；在距离仅为d=9的情况下，重复码的综合征提取达到兆量子级别的逻辑错误率。

Conclusion: 0-n福克比特架构能够有效保持噪声偏置，显著降低比特翻转错误率，为实现高效容错量子计算提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Noise-biased qubits have bit-flip errors that are exponentially suppressed relative to phase-flip errors, and offer a promising route toward fault-tolerant quantum computing. However, this bias can be compromised during gate operations with non-biased control qubits. To address this limitation, we propose a "0-n" Fock qubit architecture that maintains the noise bias by encoding information in the ground state and n-th excited state of a nonlinear multi-level system, such as a transmon. This encoding is achieved via a dissipative stabilization that acts as decay and gain for lower and upper intermediate levels, respectively. We first analytically demonstrate that bit-flip errors are exponentially suppressed with the number of levels. Then, we present a practical implementation using a multi-mode lossy filter to achieve the frequency-selective dissipation. Finally, we numerically demonstrate that bit-flip probabilities approaching $10^{-8}$ are achievable for controlled-X gates on cat qubits using the 0-n qubit as an ancilla with $n \geq 9$ (i.e. ten or more levels), for realistic experimental parameters. Building on this, we simulate syndrome extraction in a repetition code, achieving logical error rates in the megaquop regime with only a distance of $d=9$.

</details>


### [158] [Superadditivity of classical communication over quantum channels via random and deterministic permutations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25961)
*Benjamin Lovitz,Peixue Wu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出用随机置换替代Haar随机酉矩阵来解释量子信道经典通信的超可加性现象，将连续问题转化为离散组合问题，并通过算法实现渐近去随机化，尽管实际构造仍具挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找Hastings证明中量子信道超可加性的结构化解释，避免依赖Haar随机酉矩阵的集中现象。

Method: 用随机置换矩阵替代Haar随机酉矩阵，利用Bordenave与Collins的定理和O'Donnell与Wu的算法进行渐近去随机化构造。

Result: 证明了随机置换具有所需极限行为，实现了多项式时间内的渐近确定性构造；数值估计显示存在超过五千七百万个置换在极大维度下展现非可加性。

Conclusion: Haar随机性可被随机置换取代，为超可加性提供新的组合视角并开启去随机化路径，但实用化构造仍待解决。

Abstract: Since Hastings' proof of superadditivity of classical communication over quantum channels, considerable effort has been devoted to finding a structural explanation of this phenomenon that was originally established by concentration of measure for Haar random unitaries.
  The main observation of this work is that Haar randomness can be replaced by random permutations without changing the limiting geometry responsible for nonadditivity. This replacement turns a continuous problem over unitary matrices into a discrete combinatorial problem over zero--one permutation matrices, and thereby opens a path toward derandomization.
  The theorem of Bordenave and Collins shows that random permutations have the required limiting behavior and the algorithm of O'Donnell and Wu then provides a deterministic asymptotic construction, running in polynomial time in the size when the channel parameters and accuracy are fixed. Thus the random construction can be derandomized in an asymptotic algorithmic sense, although finding a simple closed-form or practically computable counterexample remains open.
  Finally, a quantitative random permutation estimate by Chen, Garza-Vargas, Tropp and van Handel gives a fully numerical estimate: there exists a tuple of 57,836,025 permutations acting on a set of size \[ N \le 5.422\times 10^{116216}\] such that the associated finite dimensional channel exhibits nonadditivity. This enormous value remains an obstacle to a practical construction.

</details>


### [159] [From Round-Trip State Echo to Error Recovery: Snapshot-Resolved Quantum-Hardware Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26010)
*Isaac Barouch Essayag,Aryeh Lev Zabokritskiy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种编译显式的屏幕-压力分析方法，使用往返状态回显（RTSE）作为诊断工具，评估不同工作负载、编译方式和执行时间下的量子硬件性能。实验结果显示，即使整体RTSE估计值变化很小，通信重运行仍会改变路由级对比度；并且在不同执行快照下，恢复概率显著变化，表明硬件性能评估需结合具体工作负载和执行环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了更准确地评估量子硬件性能，考虑到端到端的评分可能不会在不同工作负载、编译方式或执行时间之间转移，需要一种新的诊断方法来捕捉这些差异。

Method: 提出并实施了编译显式的屏幕-压力分析框架，其中包含往返状态回显（RTSE）测试：准备四个四面体量子态之一，在路由根部通过交换将其移出再移回，然后在根部应用逆向准备操作并记录结果为零。利用执行快照（即带有能力文档的任务批次提交日期）进行性能评估。

Result: 在稀疏超导硬件上，字节相同的通信重运行改变了路由级别的对比度，尽管总体RTSE估计值仅相差0.00125。在一个前瞻性冻结的双窗口长度研究中，RTSE和远程逆向空操作的根边缘均从长度2下降到长度10。64个删除-恢复单元的平均选定输出返回概率在两个IQM执行快照间从0.738降至0.624。在宣传五条虚拟线全连接的离子阱服务中，两个窗口的恢复率分别为0.911和0.923，超过冻结的三分之二参考值，恢复减去伴随控制差值分别为0.446和0.443。

Conclusion: 实验结果支持基于工作负载、布局或虚拟线合约、编译、架构及执行快照索引的量子硬件性能评估方法，强调了考虑实际执行环境的重要性。

Abstract: End-to-end quantum-hardware scores need not transfer across workloads, compilations, or execution times. We specify a compilation-explicit screen-and-stress profile whose opening diagnostic is round-trip state echo (RTSE): prepare one of four tetrahedral qubit states at a route root, move it out and back by swaps, apply inverse preparation at the root, and record zero. An execution snapshot means a dated submitted task batch together with its captured capability document where available, not a certified calibration epoch. On sparse superconducting hardware, a byte-identical communication rerun changed route-level contrasts although the aggregate RTSE estimates differed by only 0.00125. In a separate prospectively frozen two-window length study, RTSE and the remote-inverse do-nothing predecessor's root marginal both fell from length 2 to length 10; the prespecified interaction did not support superior RTSE retention. The mean selected-output return probability across 64 deletion-recovery cells changed from 0.738 to 0.624 between IQM execution snapshots. On a trapped-ion service advertising all-to-all connectivity among five submitted virtual wires, recovery was 0.911 and 0.923 in two windows, exceeding the frozen two-thirds reference; recovery-minus-adjoint-control differences were 0.446 and 0.443. These are execution-workload diagnostics, not coding-gain, error-suppression, physical-loss, fault-tolerance, or architecture-ranking claims. The results support assessment indexed by workload, placement or virtual-wire contract, compilation, architecture, and execution snapshot.

</details>


### [160] [The Gain-Engineered Transmon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26018)
*Ian Yang,Francesco Adinolfi,Alessandro Bruno,Venus Hasanuzzaman Kamrul,Daniel Z. Haxell,Alexander Grimm*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出并实验实现了一种在标准transmon电路中通过添加单个微波信号来构建噪声偏置量子比特的方法，显著延长了弛豫时间，同时保持高保真度操作，为高效量子纠错提供了硬件简化的新路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了降低容错量子计算对物理系统数量的需求，需要构建具有噪声偏置的量子比特，但传统方法在物理层面复杂度较高，限制了可扩展性。因此，亟需一种简单且兼容现有架构的噪声偏置实现方式。

Method: 利用标准transmon-readout谐振器电路，仅增加一个微波驱动信号，将量子比特编码在|g⟩和|f⟩态之间，并通过设计频率选择性增益通道来抑制计算态之间的单光子损耗误差。

Result: 实验实现了比传统|g⟩-|e⟩编码高一个数量级的弛豫时间提升，回波相干时间仅下降两倍，且验证了该量子比特支持快速、高保真度的操作。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了一种结构简单、易于集成的噪声偏置量子比特方案，可在主流超导架构中直接应用，为实现硬件高效的量子错误检测与纠正提供了可行路径。

Abstract: The interaction between a qubit and its environment can be engineered such that one error channel dominates over all others, resulting in noise bias. This property enables error correction codes to focus on the dominant error type, thereby significantly reducing the number of physical systems required for fault-tolerant quantum computation. However, engineering noise bias typically introduces complexity at the physical system level, which decreases its usefulness by limiting scalability. Here, we introduce and experimentally realize a noise-biased qubit in a standard transmon-readout resonator circuit, one of the most common superconducting architectures, by only adding a single microwave tone. We encode the qubit in the transmon $|\mathrm{g}\rangle$- and $|\mathrm{f}\rangle$-states, and engineer a frequency-selective gain channel that counteracts single-photon loss errors between the computational states. We demonstrate an order-of-magnitude enhancement in relaxation time compared to the $|\mathrm{g}\rangle-|\mathrm{e}\rangle$ encoding, conceding only a factor-of-two decrease in the echo-coherence time. Furthermore, we show that this qubit is compatible with fast, high-fidelity operations. Our results open a path towards using this system as a simple building-block for hardware-efficient quantum error detection and correction schemes.

</details>


### [161] [Immittance formulas for exact blackbox quantization and divergence-free effective models in circuit QED](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26027)
*Philippe Gigon,Peter Rabl,Adrian Parra-Rodriguez*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于一阶电路量子化方法，提出了构建约瑟夫森结超导多能级系统与多种线性环境耦合的精确哈密顿量的简单公式，并推导了弱耦合下的无发散色散哈密顿量和主方程，避免了传统方法中的兰姆位移发散问题，拓展了黑箱量子化框架至多端口、耗散和非互易场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理超导多能级系统与复杂环境耦合时存在近似导致的发散问题，且难以适用于多端口、非互易或连续耗散环境，因此需要一种更严格、普适且可扩展的量子化框架。

Method: 利用阻抗或导纳矩阵直接表征线性环境，结合一阶电路量子化方法，构建精确的全量子哈密顿量；在弱耦合下进一步推导无发散的色散哈密顿量和适用于连续耗散环境的主方程。

Result: 得到了适用于电容、电感或直接耦合的精确哈密顿量形式，揭示了模式结构、频率重整化、环境诱导相互作用、衰减速率和定向交叉耦合均源于因果性的导纳响应，并成功应用于谐振器滤波器、有限带宽超材料、非互易波导量子电动力学系统和巨型原子等实例。

Conclusion: 该方法统一且严格地处理了多端口、多模、耗散及非互易环境下的量子电路建模，避免了传统近似中的发散问题，为大规模超导量子硬件的自动化电磁设计提供了可扩展的理论基础。

Abstract: Building on the first-order circuit quantization method [arXiv:2304.12252, arXiv:2401.09120], we provide simple formulas to construct exact Hamiltonians for Josephson-junction-based superconducting qudits capacitively, inductively, or galvanically coupled to passive linear environments. These environments may be multiport, multimode, discrete or continuous, reciprocal or nonreciprocal, and are characterized directly by their impedance or admittance matrices. In the weak-coupling regime, we further derive \emph{divergence-free} dispersive Hamiltonians for mode-resolved environments and transition-resolved weak-coupling master equations for dissipative continua. Mode structure, frequency renormalizations, environment-mediated interactions, decay rates, and directional cross couplings then follow from the same causal immittance response, while spurious Lamb-shift divergences arising from uncontrolled approximations in previous treatments are made explicit and avoided. We apply the theory to a set of illustrative circuits comprising a discrete resonator filter, finite-band metamaterial environments, nonreciprocal waveguide-QED systems, and superconducting giant atoms, for which analytical response matrices can be obtained, although the method is particularly well suited to numerical responses from electromagnetic solvers or experimental characterization. We thereby extend the black-box quantization framework to multiport, dissipative, and nonreciprocal settings, establishing a simple and scalable route toward optimized and automated electromagnetic design of large-scale superconducting quantum hardware.

</details>


### [162] [A heterogeneously integrated coupled-cavity frequency beam splitter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26028)
*Lucas M. Cohen,Manuel H. Muñoz-Arias,Mohan Sarovar,Nicholas A. Boynton,Shawn C. Arterburn,Thomas A. Friedmann,Nils T. Otterstrom,Paul S. Davids,Michael Gehl*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文展示了一种基于硅光平台的异质集成薄膜铌酸锂耦合腔调制器，可实现高效、可调的频率模式转换，适用于高维量子信息处理，并展示了材料和功能集成的灵活性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了实现可扩展的高维量子信息处理，需要高效的光学频率模式相干混合器件。传统的方案在集成度和可扩展性方面存在局限，因此需要一种兼容现有光子集成技术的新型调制器结构。

Method: 采用异质集成的薄膜铌酸锂-on-硅耦合腔调制器，利用单个微波驱动信号激发混合光学超模，实现频率模式间的可调双向转换，包括50/50分束和高效频率交换。同时，在同一平台上集成了钽酸锂薄膜以验证材料灵活性。

Result: 实现了10 GHz超模分裂下的50/50频率分束和超过20 dB泵浦抑制的近乎完全频率交换，并证明了该技术与硅光子平台的兼容性，支持光源、滤波器、调谐元件和单光子探测器的共集成。

Conclusion: 该工作为构建可扩展的集成频率编码量子光子电路提供了高效且材料灵活的技术路径，推动了高频维度量子信息处理的实用化进程。

Abstract: Frequency encoded photonic qubits promise a scalable path towards high-dimensional quantum information processing, but require efficient components for coherently mixing frequency modes. Coupled cavity modulators provide this functionality by using only a single driving microwave tone to couple hybridized optical supermodes. Here, we demonstrate a heterogeneously integrated thin-film lithium-niobate-on-silicon coupled-cavity modulator that realizes tunable bidirectional frequency mode transformations, including \(50/50\) beam splitting and near complete frequency swapping with \(>20~\mathrm{dB}\) pump extinction at a \(10~\mathrm{GHz}\) supermode splitting. Because the electro-optic film is bonded onto a foundry fabricated silicon photonics platform, the approach is compatible with co-integration of photon pair sources, spectral filters, active tuning elements, and single photon detectors. We also bond thin-film lithium tantalate onto the same coupled-cavity platform, demonstrating material flexibility for scalable integrated frequency bin quantum photonic circuits.

</details>


### [163] [Asymptotically optimal purification of noisy unitary channels in any dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26061)
*Ryotaro Niwa,Satoshi Yoshida,Mio Murao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了含噪声的幺正信道净化问题，提出了在低噪声条件下通过自适应序列策略实现最优保真度的方法，并分析了其查询复杂度优于传统方法。同时，还探讨了噪声幺正共轭问题，发现其最优保真度与净化问题在主导阶上一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决在存在去极化噪声的情况下如何高效恢复未知幺正信道的问题，并探索相关任务如幺正共轭的极限性能。

Method: 通过优化任意自适应序列策略，解析推导出在噪声强度和信道使用次数的主导阶上的最优保真度，并提出一种具体的SU(d)协变并行策略以达到该最优值。

Result: 得到了低噪声下实现目标不保真度ε的查询复杂度为Θ(d²p/ε)，优于结合最优态净化与信道存储检索的传统方法；同时发现噪声幺正共轭任务的最优保真度与净化任务在主导阶上相同。

Conclusion: 所提出的策略在噪声环境下能更高效地恢复未知幺正操作，且净化与共轭任务在低噪声极限下具有相似的性能界限。

Abstract: We consider the problem of noisy unitary purification. Given access to an unknown $d$-dimensional unitary channel followed by depolarizing noise of strength $p$, we aim to construct a superchannel that universally purifies the noisy unitary back to the original unknown unitary. We optimize over arbitrary adaptive sequential strategies and analytically derive the optimal fidelity to the leading order in the noise strength and number of channel uses, while also providing a concrete $\mathrm{SU}(d)$-covariant parallel strategy that attains the optimum. Our result implies the query complexity $Θ(d^2p/ε)$ for achieving leading-order infidelity $ε$ in the low-noise regime, which scales better than the naive approach combining optimal state purification and storage-and-retrieval of quantum channels. We also consider the dual problem of noisy unitary conjugation, where the goal is to obtain the best approximation of the complex conjugate of the original unknown unitary from access to noisy queries. We show that the optimal fidelity for this task coincides with that of noisy unitary purification to the leading-order in the low-noise and large-query limit.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [164] [A Control-Theoretic Approach for Resource-Aware Consensus in Multi-Agent AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25099)
*James Flagg,Esteban A. Hernandez-Vargas*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种将大语言模型多智能体系统中的共识动力学建模为离散时间切换系统的新方法，结合计算资源约束，建立了具有资源可行性的共识安全集，并给出了保证有限时间内收敛且不耗尽资源的证书区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型多智能体系统在解决复杂推理任务时缺乏对共识性能与计算资源之间关系的严格理论保证。

Method: 将信念动力学建模为具有不同共识-收缩率和token成本的通信拓扑下的离散时间切换系统，并引入剩余计算预算构建共识安全集，推导出共识时间和token消耗的显式边界。

Result: 得出了共识时间与资源消耗的显式界限，构造了确保有限时间收敛且不耗尽资源的共识-预算证书区域，并验证了自适应拓扑切换策略在收敛速度与通信成本之间的权衡优势。

Conclusion: 控制理论驱动的证书方法可用于实现AI系统中兼顾资源效率与共识性能的智能协调机制。

Abstract: Large language model multi-agent systems (LLM-MAS) rely on inter-agent communication to solve complex reasoning tasks, yet rigorous guarantees relating consensus performance to computational resources remain limited. Here, we present a novel way to characterize collective belief dynamics as a discrete-time switched system in which communication topologies have distinct consensus-contraction rates and token costs. By augmenting the belief dynamics with the remaining computational budget, we define a consensus safe set that jointly captures agreement and resource feasibility. We derive explicit bounds on consensus time and token expenditure and construct a consensus-budget certificate region guaranteeing finite-time convergence without resource exhaustion. We further establish conditions under which adaptive topology switching achieves a trade-off between convergence speed and communication cost relative to fixed-topology strategies. Numerical experiments and live LLM-MAS deployments show the predicted consensus-cost trade-offs, demonstrating how control-theoretic certificates can enable resource-aware coordination in AI systems.

</details>


### [165] [Barycentric Weak Inner-Product Gromov-Wasserstein](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25145)
*Youssef Mroueh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种弱Gromov-Wasserstein（weak GW）框架，用于在一对多场景中比较分布，通过保留条件均值来处理目标分布的几何结构细化问题，并在点云、图特征及单细胞多组学数据中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的Gromov-Wasserstein距离在一对多设置下过于敏感，无法有效捕捉源状态到多个目标结果的几何关系，因此需要一种能聚焦于条件均值的新方法。

Method: 引入弱GW框架，利用目标条件分布之间的关系进行比较；针对内积关系，使用条件均值m_π(x)，并通过正则化和对偶性转化为A-B min-max问题，设计迭代算法求解。

Result: 证明了wIGW²(μ,ν)等于在ν的均值保持扩展η下的IGW²(μ,η)的下确界，获得了最优耦合的存在性与收敛性保证，并在有限支持测度上实现了有效算法。

Conclusion: 所提出的弱内积GW方法能够有效处理一对多的分布匹配问题，在保持条件均值的前提下允许目标分布的几何细化，实验表明该方法在多种数据类型上具有应用潜力。

Abstract: Gromov-Wasserstein (GW) compares distributions through relations within each space. This pointwise comparison can be too sensitive in one-to-many settings, where several target outcomes refine one source state and their mean carries the geometry of interest. We introduce a weak GW framework that compares source relations with relations between the target conditional laws induced by a coupling. For inner-product relations, we retain the conditional means $m_π(x)=\mathbb{E}_π[Y\mid X=x]$. The resulting barycentric weak inner-product GW (wIGW) satisfies $\mathrm{wIGW}_{\mathrm{bar}}^2(μ,ν)=\inf_{η\preceq_{\mathrm{cx}}ν}\mathrm{IGW}^2(μ,η)$. Here $η\preceq_{\mathrm{cx}}ν$ means that $ν$ is a mean-preserving spread of $η$. Thus wIGW searches for an intermediate target geometry that can be refined into the prescribed target law without changing conditional means. Under finite second moments, minimizers exist and martingale gluing recovers an optimal coupling. With ridge regularization, moment duality gives an $A$-$B$ min-max problem whose inner step is weak optimal transport with a quadratic cost parameterized by $A$ and $B$; the outer problem optimizes these matrices. For finitely supported measures, we give an iterative algorithm. Under a quantitative ridge condition, the reduced problem is convex--concave, and the projected outer iteration satisfies an explicit contraction bound for inexact inner solves. Point cloud and graph feature refinement experiments illustrate how mean-preserving target refinements can have zero cost. A paired peripheral blood mononuclear cell (PBMC) multiome study evaluates atlas based cell type transfer through RNA/ATAC alignment in cell to cell and prototype to cell settings, with the prototype to cell setting representing the one-to-many case.

</details>


### [166] [Optimization of Closed-Loop Pumped-Storage Hydropower Siting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25175)
*Luiz Rodolpho Albuquerque,Rafael Kelman,Tarcisio Castro,Ana Petrungaro,Tiago Andrade*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为HERA-S的计算建模框架，用于加速闭环抽水蓄能电站（PSH）选址评估，结合空间筛选、大坝优化和成本估算，提升可再生能源整合效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为应对可再生能源并网带来的运行挑战，特别是弃电问题，亟需长时储能技术；传统水电受环境社会限制，闭环PSH成为可行替代方案，但缺乏高效的区域选址工具。

Method: 开发了HERA-S框架，自动化执行空间筛选、大坝优化、成本估算和社会环境影响评分，并采用整数规划模型优化上下水库间的土方平衡与距离。

Result: 在巴西里约热内卢的案例研究中，该框架显著提高了前期可行性规划的敏捷性，有效支持区域级PSH站点 prospecting。

Conclusion: HERA-S为闭环抽水蓄能项目的早期选址提供了标准化、高效的技术工具，有助于推动可持续长时储能发展和电网稳定性提升。

Abstract: Accelerating variable renewable energy integration introduces substantial operational challenges to modern power grids. Frequent curtailment of surplus renewable generation highlights the pressing need for long-duration energy storage capable of shifting generation to high-demand periods while providing essential ancillary services and grid inertia. Socio-environmental constraints increasingly limit conventional hydropower development; however, closed-loop pumped-storage hydropower (PSH) presents a viable alternative because its off-river reservoirs avoid natural watercourses. This paper introduces HERA-S, a computational modeling framework developed by PSR (supported by EDF, CTG, Brookfield, and Rio Light) to streamline and standardize regional PSH site prospecting. HERA-S automates spatial screening, dam optimization, cost estimation, and socio-environmental impact scoring. Applied to a case study in Rio de Janeiro, Brazil, the framework employs an integer programming model to optimize dam-fill and excavation mass balances against inter-reservoir distance, significantly improving pre-feasibility planning agility.

</details>


### [167] [Approximating Energy-Regulation Feasible Region of Virtual Power Plants: A Data-driven Inverse Optimization Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25248)
*Ruike Lyu,Hongye Guo,Qixin Chen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种数据驱动的方法来近似虚拟电厂（VPP）的电能-调节可行域，通过虚拟电池模型和逆向优化技术提高分布式能源资源灵活性的利用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可行域聚合方法依赖于解析方法，存在通用性和适应性问题，因此需要一种更灵活、适应性强的方法来准确聚合虚拟电厂内部分布式能源资源的可行域。

Method: 采用数据驱动方法，利用虚拟电池模型近似虚拟电厂的聚合可行域，并基于多场景运行数据通过逆向优化确定模型参数。

Result: 数值测试验证了所提方法在近似虚拟电厂能量-调节可行域方面的准确性。

Conclusion: 该方法能够有效提升虚拟电厂对分布式能源资源灵活性的聚合能力，有助于未来市场出清和调度中更好地利用分布式资源。

Abstract: System operators will probably allow virtual power plants (VPPs) to submit their feasible region (FR) for market clearing and dispatch. A VPP needs to determine its FR to submit as a whole based on the individual operation model of its internal distributed energy resources (DERs), which is an FR aggregation problem. Existing FR aggregation approaches rely on analytical methods, which have issues with generality and adaptability. In this paper, we propose a data-driven approach to approximate the energy-regulation FR of VPPs. It adopts the virtual battery model to approximate the aggregate FR of a VPP and determines the model parameters through inverse optimization based on generated multi-scenario operation data using the original operation model. Numerical tests verified the accuracy of the proposed method. We believe that our work helps to better leverage the flexibility of DERs.

</details>


### [168] [Optimal Control in Hilbert Complex Spaces with Finite Element Exterior Calculus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25266)
*Farid Bozorgnia,Michael Holst,Anshu Kumar,Vyacheslav Kungurtsev,Martin Licht,Gantumur Tsogtgerel*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在Hilbert复形上用于偏微分方程约束最优控制的框架，并利用有限元外微积分（FEEC）进行结构保持离散化，特别关注由于非平凡拓扑引起的全局物理模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非平凡拓扑会导致物理上有意义的全局模式，传统方法难以有效处理这些模式，因此需要一种能够保持拓扑和几何结构的控制框架。

Method: 通过将局部状态与表示全局环流或通量的调和分量分离，引入有限维拓扑执行器，并采用FEEC进行离散化，保留调和结构。

Result: 建立了问题的适定性、弱最优性条件、可达性结果及FEEC误差估计；数值实验验证了环流与腔体通量控制、秩依赖的可达性、网格无关优化以及拓扑诱导的奇异收敛行为。

Conclusion: 该框架有效结合了拓扑结构与最优控制，能够在静态与动态（如Maxwell控制）问题中保持物理守恒律与可达性，为复杂拓扑域上的控制提供了新方法。

Abstract: We develop a framework for PDE-constrained optimal control on Hilbert complexes and its structure-preserving discretization by finite element exterior calculus (FEEC), with emphasis on problems where nontrivial topology creates physically meaningful global modes. We separate the gauge-fixed local state from harmonic components representing global circulation or flux, and introduce finite-dimensional topological actuators whose reachable modes are determined jointly by domain topology and actuator rank. We establish well-posedness, weak optimality conditions, reachability results, and FEEC error estimates preserving the harmonic structure. Numerical experiments on contractible and multiply connected domains demonstrate circulation and cavity- flux control, rank-dependent reachability, mesh-independent optimization, and topology-induced singular convergence. We further extend the framework to time-dependent Maxwell control, where harmonic electric circulation and magnetic flux become dynamical states exhibiting explicit conservation and reachability properties.

</details>


### [169] [Schrödinger Bridges over Kinetic Swarming Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25281)
*Asmaa Eldesoukey,Md Zulfiqur Haider,Italo Napolitano,Yongxin Chen,Abhishek Halder*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了在有限时间范围内通过最小能量控制实现受随机扰动影响的惯性群体系统的集体引导问题，采用基于Schrödinger桥理论的方法，结合Cucker--Smale对齐或Morse吸引-排斥相互作用模型，提出了一种状态反馈控制策略，能够有效利用或抵消群体内部相互作用力以实现期望的分布转移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在生物启发的群体系统中，仅靠个体间的相互作用难以在规定时间内将群体引导至目标构型，因此需要外部控制手段实现快速、节能的集体行为调控，满足如靶向治疗、集体运输和应急疏散等实际应用需求。

Method: 基于Schrödinger桥理论，将无控的交互随机动力学视为先验模型，最优控制视为实现预定终端分布所需的最小能量修正漂移项；针对Cucker--Smale或Morse相互作用下的均场模型，推导出具有时间对称结构的非线性耦合最优性条件，并采用嵌套定点迭代法进行数值求解。

Result: 得到了一组非线性耦合的必要最优性系统，数值结果表明所设计的最优控制能够根据任务需求动态地利用或对抗群体内部的相互作用力，从而高效实现从初始到目标分布（全相空间或仅位置边缘分布）的有限时间转移。

Conclusion: Schrödinger桥框架为受相互作用影响的随机多智能体系统的有限时间、最小能量集体引导提供了有效的理论与计算工具，能够在存在噪声的情况下精确调控群体的整体分布演化。

Abstract: Paradigmatic interaction models explain how collective behaviors can emerge in complex systems from interactions among the constituent agents. In bio-inspired swarms, however, interactions alone may not suffice to bring the population to a desired aggregate configuration within a prescribed time horizon, as needed in applications ranging from targeted therapy to collective transport and emergency evacuation. In the present work, we consider finite-horizon minimum-energy collective steering for inertial swarms that are subject to stochastic disturbances. We focus on the mean-field representations of these multi-agent systems driven by Cucker--Smale alignment or Morse attraction--repulsion interactions. Our objective is to steer the swarm between prescribed endpoint distributions using a state-feedback control, where the endpoint specifications can be full phase-space distributions (positions and velocities) or position marginals alone. Our formalism is rooted in the theory of Schrödinger bridges, which has inspired contemporary developments spanning statistical inference, biological modeling, stochastic control, and generative learning. Within the bridges framework, the uncontrolled interacting stochastic dynamics are viewed as a prior model, and the optimal control as the minimum-energy corrective drift needed to realize the prescribed distributions. We derive nonlinear, coupled necessary optimality systems with a time-symmetric structure reminiscent of classical Schrödinger bridges, and propose nested fixed-point schemes to numerically solve them. Numerical examples show that the obtained optimal control (corrective drift) can dynamically exploit or counteract the interaction forces, depending on whether the latter are favorable or adversarial to the steering task.

</details>


### [170] [Accelerated Gradient Flow with Endogenous Gradient-Memory Anchor: Selection and Restoring Damping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25312)
*Chinedu Izuchukwu,Ernest Obini,Jen-Chih Yao,Shengda Zeng*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种带有内生梯度记忆锚点和非线性恢复-阻尼反馈的加速梯度流，仅使用目标函数的一阶信息实现加速收敛，并在适当假设下证明了解的存在唯一性、目标值的加速衰减（O(t^{-2})）以及轨迹强收敛到最小化解。当解集为仿射时，极限可显式表示为初始锚点在解集上的欧氏投影，特别适用于低秩最小二乘问题中选择最接近初始锚点的解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计一种不依赖Hessian信息但能实现加速收敛的优化动力学方法，同时具备良好的收敛性和解选择机制，尤其针对病态或非唯一解的问题（如低秩最小二乘）。

Method: 引入一个基于加权历史梯度动态演化的内生锚点，并构造具有坐标级位移调制的非线性恢复-阻尼反馈项，构建仅依赖一阶信息的加速梯度流；通过Lyapunov分析和对相变量的耗散估计研究其收敛性质。

Result: 建立了全局强解的存在唯一性；获得目标值加速衰减速率O(t^{-2})；证明原变量轨迹强收敛到最小化解集；当解集为仿射时，极限为初始锚点在解集上的投影；在特定条件下，相变量获得额外多项式衰减因子；数值实验验证了最小化解选择、加速衰减和恢复-阻尼机制。

Conclusion: 该方法通过内生锚点和非线性反馈实现了无需二阶信息的加速优化，兼具理论保证与实用价值，特别能够在非唯一解问题中显式选择特定解（如最近解），具有良好的应用潜力。

Abstract: We introduce an accelerated gradient flow with an endogenous gradient-memory anchor and a nonlinear restoring--damping feedback. The anchor evolves from a weighted history of the gradients, while the nonlinear feedback is modulated by the squared coordinatewise displacement from the anchor. The resulting dynamics uses only first-order information from the objective function and does not involve explicit Hessian information. Under suitable assumptions, we establish global existence and uniqueness of strong solutions. A Lyapunov analysis yields the accelerated objective-value estimate $F(x(t))-F^\star=\mathcal{O}(t^{-2})$, with the nonlinear restoring--damping term contributing additional dissipation. Under similar assumptions, the primal trajectory converges strongly to a minimizer. When, in addition, the solution set $S$ is affine, the limit is identified explicitly as the Euclidean projection $P_S(z_0)$ of the initial anchor $z_0$ onto $S$. In particular, for rank-deficient least-squares problems, the dynamics selects the least-squares solution closest to $z_0$. We also establish a finite weighted dissipation estimate for the phase variable. Moreover, in coordinates where the nonlinear feedback is active, if the trajectory remains separated from the anchor over an interval, then the corresponding homogeneous phase dynamics acquires an additional polynomial decay factor. Numerical experiments illustrate the minimizer-selection, accelerated objective-value decay, and restoring--damping mechanisms.

</details>


### [171] [A Multi-View Coupled Tensor Decomposition for Lightweight Online Adaptive Traffic Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25498)
*Quan Yu,Jie Ni,Yu-Hong Dai,Xiongjun Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于多视角耦合张量分解（MVCTD）的在线交通预测模型，通过建模共享空间结构和各视角时间动态，并引入组稀疏正则化抑制异常影响，实现对不完整多源交通数据的高效准确预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在感知不完善的条件下，如观测缺失和异常干扰，传统单视角交通预测方法难以保持准确性，因此需要一种能融合多视角信息并应对数据质量问题的在线预测模型。

Method: 提出MVCTD模型，采用耦合张量分解构建潜在预测空间，联合建模跨视角的共享空间结构与视角特定的时间动态；引入组稀疏正则化以捕捉由真实交通异常引起的关联异常响应；在流式部署中，仅对当前潜在张量进行迭代优化，其余变量通过基于历史汇总信息的闭式更新，提升计算效率。

Result: 在真实交通数据集上的实验表明，MVCTD在高比例数据缺失情况下仍能实现准确预测，且运行时间表现优异，显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: MVCTD模型能够有效应对多视角交通数据中的缺失与异常问题，具备高精度和低延迟特性，适用于实际场景中的在线交通预测。

Abstract: Accurate online traffic prediction is essential for intelligent transportation systems, where forecasting must be performed continuously under imperfect sensing conditions. Missing observations and anomalous disturbances make this task challenging, particularly when prediction relies on a single traffic view. This paper proposes a Multi-View Coupled Tensor Decomposition (MVCTD) model for online traffic prediction from imperfect multi-view observations, such as speed, flow, and occupancy. The proposed model uses coupled tensor decomposition to build a structured latent forecasting space, in which shared spatial structures across traffic views and view-specific temporal dynamics are jointly modeled. A group sparse regularization is further introduced to capture correlated abnormal responses induced by real traffic anomalies and thus reduce their influence on forecasts. For streaming deployment, MVCTD performs iterative refinement only on the current latent tensor, while the remaining model variables are updated by lightweight closed-form steps based on summarized historical information, thereby avoiding repeated optimization over the full historical sequence. Experiments on real-world traffic datasets demonstrate that MVCTD achieves accurate forecasts with favorable runtime under severe missingness, confirming its suitability for online traffic prediction.

</details>


### [172] [Inertial Primal-Dual Dynamics Methods Featuring Implicit Hessian-Driven Damping for Convex Optimization Problems in Continuous and Discrete Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25519)
*Xiangkai Sun,Zeying Gao,Liang He,Kok Lay Teo*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有隐式Hessian驱动阻尼的惯性 primal-dual 动力学，用于解决带线性等式约束的强凸优化问题，提出了实现指数收敛的连续系统及其离散算法，并通过李雅普诺夫方法证明了其收敛性，推广至非光滑情形并验证了数值效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为解决带线性等式约束的强凸优化问题，设计具有快速收敛性的连续动力系统及对应的离散算法。

Method: 提出带有隐式Hessian驱动阻尼的惯性primal-dual动力系统，通过隐式时间离散化得到加速算法，并采用Lyapunov方法分析收敛性。

Result: 建立了目标函数误差、可行性度量及轨迹和速度的快速收敛率，在适当参数下实现指数收敛；离散算法保持了连续系统的收敛速率，并推广到非光滑情形。

Conclusion: 所提动力系统及算法对强凸优化问题具有优异的收敛性能，理论与数值结果验证了其有效性。

Abstract: This paper investigates inertial primal-dual dynamics with implicit Hessian-driven damping for strongly convex optimization problems with linear equality constraints. We first establish fast convergence rates for the objective function value error, the feasibility measure, and both the trajectory and its corresponding velocity vector. By appropriately adjusting parameters, we show that the proposed system achieves exponential convergence rates. Through implicit time discretization of the dynamical system, we derive an inertial accelerated primal-dual algorithm for solving the strongly convex optimization problems. Using Lyapunov-based method, we show that the proposed algorithm achieves convergence rates consistent with those of its continuous-time counterpart. We also extend the obtained results to non-smooth convex optimization case. Furthermore, we conduct numerical experiments to illustrate theoretical results.

</details>


### [173] [A Decomposed Bilevel Search for Variable-Metric Proximal Gradient Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25557)
*Xinpeng Li,Ya-xiang Yuan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Decomposed Bilevel Search (DBS) 的新方法，用于加速复合凸优化中的变量度量近端方法，通过使用对角加秩一因子分解来有效求解高维、病态条件下的近端子问题，并在SLOPE和组Lasso回归任务中展现出优于传统FISTA的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于拟牛顿度量导致的缩放近端映射通常没有闭式解，限制了变量度量近端方法在复合凸优化中的应用效率，因此需要一种高效且可扩展的近端子问题求解方法。

Method: 提出Decomposed Bilevel Search (DBS)，利用满足弱割线条件的对角加秩一因子分解X=D+uv^⊤，将缩放近端步转化为二维单调残差系统；每一步残差计算仅需一次对角度量近端映射，通过双层优化策略高效求解。

Result: 在强凸条件下，外层方法在线性收敛；内层求解器以O((d+Tp)log²(1/ε))的复杂度达到精度ε；实验显示DBS在高维SLOPE问题（条件数高达4×10⁶）中仅需数百次普通近端计算即可求解，并在外层优化中显著减少梯度评估次数，在real-sim数据上表现出明显的时间优势；在组Lasso逻辑回归中也表现最优。

Conclusion: DBS为变量度量近端方法提供了一种高效、实用的实现方式，特别适用于高维、病态和结构化正则化问题，在理论收敛性和实际计算效率方面均表现出色。

Abstract: Variable-metric proximal methods accelerate composite convex optimization,
  but the scaled proximal map induced by a quasi-Newton metric rarely
  has a closed form. We develop \emph{Decomposed Bilevel Search} (DBS), based
  on a diagonal-plus-rank-one factor \(X=D+uv^\top\) whose diagonal scaling
  satisfies a weak secant equation. The induced inverse metric
  \(B^{-1}=XX^\top\) recovers zero-memory DFP/BFGS-type Broyden members, while
  the factor form reduces each scaled proximal step to a two-dimensional
  monotone residual system. Each residual evaluation requires one
  diagonal-metric proximal map, and a certified bilevel solve reaches target
  accuracy \(ε\) in
  \(\mathcal O((d+T_p)\log^2(1/ε))\) work, where \(T_p\) is the cost of
  that proximal map. Under strong convexity, the outer method converges
  linearly with exact and inexact inner solves. In the scalar
  specialization \(D=αI\), the oracle uses only ordinary proximal
  evaluations of the regularizer and no generalized Jacobian or active-set
  information.
  Experiments on ordered-weighted \(\ell_1\) (SLOPE/OWL) and group-lasso
  logistic regression evaluate both the inner oracle and the full outer
  method. The oracle solves
  high-dimensional SLOPE scaled proximal subproblems of
  condition number up to \(4\times10^6\) using a few hundred ordinary
  proximal evaluations. In SLOPE outer benchmarks, the
  warm-started oracle keeps the scaled-proximal overhead controlled and DBS
  reaches stringent targets with far fewer gradient evaluations than
  Lipschitz-normalized FISTA; on
  \texttt{real-sim} this becomes a clear target-time advantage. On
  group-lasso logistic regression, DBS is reliable on synthetic correlated
  instances and fastest on a real-data grouped-copy instance.

</details>


### [174] [Sequential Stability of the Value Function and the Solution Mapping in Berge's Maximum Theorem via Variational Convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25789)
*John Cotrina,Raúl Fierro,Rubén López*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了参数优化问题在目标函数和可行映射序列扰动下的稳定性，利用变分逼近方法分析值函数序列和解映射的收敛性，并推广了Berge最大值定理的相关结论，应用于广义纳什均衡与有限时域动态规划模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于优化数据中存在固有的不准确性，传统Berge最大值定理的应用受到限制，因此需要研究在目标函数和约束集同时受到扰动时优化问题的稳定性。

Method: 采用下半连续、上半连续、epi-收敛、hypo-收敛等函数收敛性概念，以及多重映射的下/上连续收敛和图像收敛来分析值函数和解映射的渐近行为，并通过变分逼近处理数据扰动。

Result: 建立了序列值函数和解映射的收敛性结果，揭示了不同类型收敛性之间的关系，给出了相关例子和反例，扩展了现有稳定性理论。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法推广并补充了文献中的稳定性结果，能够有效处理更广泛的扰动情形，并成功应用于广义纳什均衡问题和新的动态规划扰动模型。

Abstract: Berge's maximum theorem ensures the continuity of the value function and the upper semicontinuity of the solution mapping in parametric optimization problems. This theorem plays a central role in optimization theory, game theory, and dynamic programming. Motivated by the inherent inaccuracies in optimization data, this paper investigates the stability of such problems under sequential perturbations of both the objective function and the feasible mapping. The analysis focuses on the convergence of sequences of value functions and solution mappings via variational approximations of the data. To this end, we employ lower and upper continuous, epi- and hypo-convergence notions for functions, together with lower and upper continuous and graphical convergence notions for multifunctions. In addition, we study some relationships among these types of convergence and provide examples and counterexamples associated with the corresponding notions. Our results extend and complement existing stability results in the literature. We provide applications to generalized Nash equilibrium problems, where stability is obtained via a direct approach, as well as to finite-horizon dynamic programming models under novel perturbation assumptions.

</details>


### [175] [A unified continuous-discrete framework for Nesterov acceleration: transitions between convex and strongly convex regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26014)
*Xin He,Ya-Ping Fang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种统一的连续-离散框架，用于在凸和强凸情形之间系统过渡，兼顾早期收敛速度和渐近收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Nesterov加速方法在强凸性参数较小时，直接使用强凸系数可能导致初期收敛变慢，尽管其渐近速率较好。因此需要一种能同时保持初期快速收敛和良好渐近性能的方法。

Method: 通过构造包含阻尼和惯性参数的双态耦合连续时间动力学，并采用统一的Lyapunov分析框架，推导出兼具O(1/t²)和指数收敛性的动态系统，再将其离散化得到两类加速前向-后向算法。

Result: 所提框架实现了从凸到强凸的平滑过渡，保留了初期加速行为并达到强凸渐近速率；理论分析覆盖凸、强凸及中间情形，且数值实验验证了在小强凸参数下的有效性。

Conclusion: 该统一框架成功融合了经典凸与强凸加速机制，通过多种过渡函数族（如双曲、指数、代数等）实现了性能优势的结合，适用于更广泛的问题设定。

Abstract: Classical Nesterov acceleration employs different choices of damping and inertial parameters in the convex and strongly convex settings, both for continuous-time dynamics and for discrete algorithms. When the strong convexity parameter is small, directly using the strongly convex damping or inertial coefficient may lead to slower early-stage convergence than the corresponding convex choice, despite its favorable asymptotic exponential or linear rate. We develop a unified continuous-discrete framework that encompasses both classical regimes and provides systematic transitions between them. The resulting coefficient families retain the accelerated convex behavior at early stages while attaining the strongly convex asymptotic rate. The continuous-time dynamics arise from a two-state coupling and are analyzed within a unified Lyapunov framework that yields simultaneous $\mathcal{O}(1/t^2)$ and exponential convergence estimates, thereby recovering the classical convex and strongly convex rates. We further derive two classes of accelerated forward-backward algorithms by discretizing the proposed dynamics and establish convergence estimates covering the convex, strongly convex, and intermediate regimes. The framework recovers the classical Nesterov inertial coefficients and generates hyperbolic, exponential, algebraic, and polynomial transition families. Numerical experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed methods when the strong convexity parameter is small.

</details>


### [176] [The Value of Human Expertise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26051)
*Bradley Sturt*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种利用人类专业知识改进优化问题性能保证的方法，适用于参数未知但决策者对名义问题最优值有先验信念的场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在参数未知的优化问题中，决策者常拥有数据之外的领域知识或物理世界经验，认为名义问题的最优值不太可能很大，但现有方法未能有效利用此类信息。

Method: 构建一个评估策略的新框架，若策略最坏情况性能的计算可表示为凸规划，则通过分析一个max-min问题的极小极大间隙来量化人类专业知识的价值。

Result: 证明了人类专业知识的价值等于该max-min问题的极小极大间隙，并在商品组合优化和最短路径问题中验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提方法能有效融合人类先验信念，在满足条件时提供更紧的性能保证，从而提升不确定环境下决策的质量。

Abstract: We consider optimization applications with unknown parameters where the decision maker believes that the optimal value of the nominal problem-the optimization problem they would have solved if the true parameters were known-is unlikely to be large. This belief derives from information that humans have that is not captured in datasets, obtained from domain knowledge and interacting with the physical world. We propose an approach to evaluating policies that provides tighter performance guarantees if the decision maker's belief happens to be correct. Our main result shows that if computing a policy's worst-case performance is a convex program, then the value of human expertise-the maximum improvement in performance guarantees that can be obtained from the belief about the nominal problem-is equal to the minimax gap of a max-min problem. We illustrate our developments in assortment optimization and shortest path problems.

</details>
